then append to the end of the file:

```ts
describe('chunkText', () => {
  const body = (chunk: string): string => chunk.slice(chunk.indexOf('\n') + 1);

  it('returns one chunk with the TBX 1/1 header for short text', () => {
    expect(chunkText('{"v":1}')).toEqual(['TBX 1/1\n{"v":1}']);
  });

  it('returns one empty chunk for empty text', () => {
    expect(chunkText('')).toEqual(['TBX 1/1\n']);
  });

  it('splits by UTF-8 bytes without breaking code points', () => {
    const text = 'ab€😀c'.repeat(7); // 1+1+3+4+1 = 10 bytes per repeat
    const chunks = chunkText(text, 8);
    for (const [i, chunk] of chunks.entries()) {
      expect(chunk.startsWith(`TBX ${i + 1}/${chunks.length}\n`)).toBe(true);
      expect(new TextEncoder().encode(body(chunk)).length).toBeLessThanOrEqual(8);
      expect(body(chunk)).not.toMatch(/[\uD800-\uDBFF]$/); // no dangling high surrogate
    }
    expect(chunks.map(body).join('')).toBe(text);
  });

  it('fills every chunk but the last to within one code point of maxBytes', () => {
    const chunks = chunkText('ab€😀c'.repeat(7), 8);
    expect(chunks.length).toBeGreaterThan(1);
    for (const chunk of chunks.slice(0, -1)) {
      expect(new TextEncoder().encode(body(chunk)).length).toBeGreaterThan(8 - 4);
    }
  });

  it('rejects a maxBytes that cannot hold every code point', () => {
    expect(() => chunkText('x', 3)).toThrow(RangeError);
    expect(() => chunkText('x', 10.5)).toThrow(RangeError);
  });

  it('RF4: text larger than one QR → several chunks that each encode, decode and reassemble', () => {
    const record = { k: 'mediasession', detail: 'nexttrack — wheel tilt ✓', t: 12.3 };
    const text = JSON.stringify(Array.from({ length: 120 }, (_, i) => ({ ...record, i })));
    expect(new TextEncoder().encode(text).length).toBeGreaterThan(2953);
    expect(() => encodeQr(text)).toThrow(RangeError);

    const chunks = chunkText(text);
    expect(chunks.length).toBeGreaterThan(1);
    const decoded = chunks.map((chunk, i) => {
      const result = decode(encodeQr(chunk));
      expect(result.data).toBe(chunk);
      expect(result.data.startsWith(`TBX ${i + 1}/${chunks.length}\n`)).toBe(true);
      return result.data;
    });
    expect(decoded.map(body).join('')).toBe(text);
    expect(JSON.parse(decoded.map(body).join(''))).toHaveLength(120);
  });
});

describe('drawQr', () => {
  /** Fake canvas whose 2D context paints fillRect calls into an RGBA buffer. */
  function fakeCanvas() {
    let pixels = new Uint8ClampedArray(0);
    const canvas = { width: 0, height: 0 };
    const ctx = {
      fillStyle: '#000000',
      fillRect(x: number, y: number, w: number, h: number) {
        if (pixels.length !== canvas.width * canvas.height * 4) {
          pixels = new Uint8ClampedArray(canvas.width * canvas.height * 4);
        }
        const v = ctx.fillStyle === '#ffffff' ? 255 : 0;
        for (let yy = y; yy < y + h; yy++) {
          for (let xx = x; xx < x + w; xx++) {
            const p = (yy * canvas.width + xx) * 4;
            pixels[p] = v;
            pixels[p + 1] = v;
            pixels[p + 2] = v;
            pixels[p + 3] = 255;
          }
        }
      },
    };
    const el = Object.assign(canvas, { getContext: (id: string) => (id === '2d' ? ctx : null) });
    return { el: el as unknown as HTMLCanvasElement, canvas, pixels: () => pixels };
  }

  it('sizes the canvas with the default scale 6 and quiet zone 4', () => {
    const { el, canvas } = fakeCanvas();
    drawQr(el, encodeQr('hello'));
    expect(canvas.width).toBe((21 + 8) * 6);
    expect(canvas.height).toBe((21 + 8) * 6);
  });

  it('paints a symbol that jsQR decodes', () => {
    const { el, canvas, pixels } = fakeCanvas();
    drawQr(el, encodeQr('TBX 1/2\n{"v":1,"sha":"abc1234"}'), 4, 4);
    const result = jsQR(pixels(), canvas.width, canvas.height);
    expect(result?.data).toBe('TBX 1/2\n{"v":1,"sha":"abc1234"}');
  });

  it('throws when the 2D context is unavailable', () => {
    const el = { width: 0, height: 0, getContext: () => null } as unknown as HTMLCanvasElement;
    expect(() => drawQr(el, encodeQr('x'))).toThrow('2D canvas context unavailable');
  });
});
```

The fake canvas rasterises `fillRect` calls into an RGBA buffer, so the `drawQr` output itself is
decoded by jsQR — no DOM or canvas package needed.

- [ ] **Step 12: Append chunking and drawing to the end of `src/platform/qr.ts`**

```ts
function utf8Length(codePoint: number): number {
  if (codePoint < 0x80) return 1;
  if (codePoint < 0x800) return 2;
  if (codePoint < 0x10000) return 3; // includes lone surrogates, which encode as U+FFFD
  return 4;
}

/**
 * Splits `text` into chunks whose payload is at most `maxBytes` UTF-8 bytes, never inside a
 * code point, each prefixed "TBX i/n\n" (i is 1-based). Reassemble by dropping each chunk's
 * first line and concatenating in order. Empty text yields one empty chunk.
 */
export function chunkText(text: string, maxBytes = 1100): string[] {
  if (!Number.isInteger(maxBytes) || maxBytes < 4) {
    throw new RangeError(`maxBytes must be an integer ≥ 4, got ${maxBytes}`);
  }
  const parts: string[] = [];
  let current = '';
  let currentBytes = 0;
  for (const ch of text) {
    const n = utf8Length(ch.codePointAt(0) ?? 0);
    if (currentBytes + n > maxBytes) {
      parts.push(current);
      current = '';
      currentBytes = 0;
    }
    current += ch;
    currentBytes += n;
  }
  if (current !== '' || parts.length === 0) parts.push(current);
  return parts.map((part, i) => `TBX ${i + 1}/${parts.length}\n${part}`);
}

/** Draws the symbol black-on-white with a light quiet zone; sets the canvas backing size. */
export function drawQr(canvas: HTMLCanvasElement, m: QrMatrix, scale = 6, quiet = 4): void {
  const px = (m.size + quiet * 2) * scale;
  canvas.width = px;
  canvas.height = px;
  const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
  if (!ctx) throw new Error('2D canvas context unavailable');
  ctx.fillStyle = '#ffffff';
  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, px, px);
  ctx.fillStyle = '#000000';
  for (let y = 0; y < m.size; y++) {
    for (let x = 0; x < m.size; x++) {
      if (m.modules[y * m.size + x] === 1) {
        ctx.fillRect((x + quiet) * scale, (y + quiet) * scale, scale, scale);
      }
    }
  }
}
```

**Files:**

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes:
  - `src/contracts.ts`: `type OutputRoute = 'direct' | 'media-element' | 'direct-anchor'`.
  - T2 `src/platform/detect.ts`: `type Features`, `type Mcu`, `interface ViewportInfo`.
  - T5 `src/probe/gps-stats.ts`: `interface GpsModeSummary` (contract shape).
  - T7 `src/probe/audio.ts`: `interface AudioProbeState`, `interface BenchResult` (contract shape).
  - T8 `src/probe/inputs.ts`: `interface InputCounts`, `interface MotionReport` (contract shape).
  - T9 `src/probe/storage-probe.ts`: `interface StorageReport` (contract shape).

- Produces:

Also `export function errText(e: unknown): string` — `"<Name>: <message>"` for an `Error` whose name is not `'Error'`, just the message for a plain `Error`, `String(e)` otherwise; the single error formatter T5, T7, T8, T9 and T10 import. And `export function withTimeout<T>(p: Promise<T>, ms: number, label: string): Promise<T>` — rejects with `Error('<label> timed out after <ms> ms')` if `p` has not settled within `ms` (timer cleared once `p` settles), else settles like `p` with its rejection value unchanged; the single timeout helper T7 (including its 500 ms resume check) and T9 import.
  - `src/probe/summary.ts`: `export interface RouteObservation`, `export interface ProbeSummary` (exactly the contract shape, `v: 1`); `export const FORBIDDEN_KEYS: readonly string[]` = `['lat', 'lon', 'latitude', 'longitude', 'coords', 'position']` (frozen); `export function assertNoCoordinates(value: unknown): void` — throws `Error('Probe summary contains forbidden coordinate key at <path>')` for any own enumerable key matching `FORBIDDEN_KEYS` **case-insensitively** at any depth; path syntax `$.a.b[2].c`; cycles visited once; typed arrays skipped; `export function buildSummary(parts: Omit<ProbeSummary, 'v' | 'logTail'>, log: EventLog): ProbeSummary` — builds the object with keys in contract order (`v` first), copies only contract fields (extra properties on `parts` are dropped), `logTail` = last ≤ 40 entries formatted ``t=${(tMs / 1000).toFixed(1)}s ${kind} ${detail}``, then calls `assertNoCoordinates` before returning; `export function formatEntry(e: LogEntry): string` — that entry formatter (pure), which T10 reuses for its on-screen log so the screen and the QR `logTail` never drift apart.

```ts
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { errText, EventLog, withTimeout, type LogEntry } from '../../src/probe/log';

afterEach(() => {

});

describe('EventLog', () => {

    log.push('note', 'a', 10);
    log.push('key', 'b', 20);
    expect(log.entries()).toEqual([
      { tMs: 10, kind: 'note', detail: 'a' },
      { tMs: 20, kind: 'key', detail: 'b' },
    ]);
  });

  it('stamps entries with performance.now() by default', () => {
    vi.spyOn(performance, 'now').mockReturnValue(1234.5);
    const log = new EventLog();
    log.push('audio', 'running');
    expect(log.entries()[0]?.tMs).toBe(1234.5);
  });

  it('keeps only the newest `capacity` entries across many pushes, in order', () => {
    const log = new EventLog(3);
    for (let i = 1; i <= 7; i++) log.push('note', `n${i}`, i);
    expect(log.entries().map((e) => e.detail)).toEqual(['n5', 'n6', 'n7']);
    log.push('note', 'n8', 8);
    expect(log.entries().map((e) => e.detail)).toEqual(['n6', 'n7', 'n8']);
  });

  it('returns a partial buffer before it wraps', () => {
    const log = new EventLog(5);
    log.push('note', 'only', 1);
    expect(log.entries().map((e) => e.detail)).toEqual(['only']);
    expect(new EventLog(5).entries()).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('defaults to capacity 500', () => {
    const log = new EventLog();
    for (let i = 0; i < 501; i++) log.push('note', `n${i}`, i);
    const entries = log.entries();
    expect(entries).toHaveLength(500);
    expect(entries[0]?.detail).toBe('n1');
    expect(entries[499]?.detail).toBe('n500');
  });

  it('counts every push ever made, per kind and in total, including evicted ones', () => {
    const log = new EventLog(2);
    log.push('key', 'a', 1);
    log.push('key', 'b', 2);
    log.push('wheel', 'c', 3);
    log.push('key', 'd', 4);
    expect(log.count()).toBe(4);
    expect(log.count('key')).toBe(3);
    expect(log.count('wheel')).toBe(1);
    expect(log.count('gps')).toBe(0);
    expect(log.entries()).toHaveLength(2);
  });

  it('returns frozen entries that callers cannot corrupt', () => {
    const log = new EventLog();
    log.push('note', 'x', 1);
    const [entry] = log.entries();
    expect(Object.isFrozen(entry)).toBe(true);
    const copy = log.entries();
    copy.pop();
    expect(log.entries()).toHaveLength(1);
  });

  it('notifies subscribers on every push until they unsubscribe', () => {
    const log = new EventLog();
    const seen: LogEntry[] = [];
    const unsubscribe = log.subscribe((e) => seen.push(e));
    log.push('gamepad', 'connected', 1);
    log.push('visibility', 'hidden', 2);
    unsubscribe();
    log.push('visibility', 'visible', 3);
    expect(seen.map((e) => e.detail)).toEqual(['connected', 'hidden']);
    unsubscribe(); // idempotent
  });

  it('lets a subscriber unsubscribe itself during a notification', () => {
    const log = new EventLog();
    const calls: string[] = [];
    const off = log.subscribe(() => {
      calls.push('once');
      off();
    });
    log.subscribe((e) => calls.push(e.detail));
    log.push('note', 'a', 1);
    log.push('note', 'b', 2);
    expect(calls).toEqual(['once', 'a', 'b']);
  });

    const log = new EventLog();

    });
    log.subscribe((e) => seen.push(e.detail));
    expect(() => log.push('error', 'boom', 1)).not.toThrow();
    expect(seen).toEqual(['boom']);
    expect(log.count('error')).toBe(1);

    expect(errText(new DOMException('denied', 'SecurityError'))).toBe('SecurityError: denied');
    expect(errText('plain')).toBe('plain');

    const done = expect(p).rejects.toThrow('caches.open timed out after 500 ms');
    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(500);

});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `src/probe/log.ts`**

```ts
export type LogKind =
  | 'mediasession'
  | 'key'
  | 'wheel'
  | 'gamepad'
  | 'visibility'
  | 'audio'
  | 'gps'
  | 'motion'
  | 'storage'
  | 'note'
  | 'error';

export interface LogEntry {
  /** performance.now() at push, ms. */
  tMs: number;
  kind: LogKind;
  detail: string;
}

/**
 * Fixed-capacity ring buffer of probe events. Keeps the newest `capacity` entries, counts every
 * push ever made (per kind and in total), and notifies subscribers synchronously on each push.
 * Main-thread only; entries are frozen so subscribers and readers can share them safely.
 */
export class EventLog {
  private readonly capacity: number;
  private readonly buffer: (LogEntry | undefined)[];
  private head = 0; // index the next push writes to
  private size = 0;
  private total = 0;
  private readonly totals = new Map<LogKind, number>();
  private readonly subscribers = new Set<(e: LogEntry) => void>();

  constructor(capacity = 500) {
    if (!Number.isInteger(capacity) || capacity < 1) {
      throw new RangeError(`EventLog capacity must be a positive integer, got ${capacity}`);
    }
    this.capacity = capacity;
    this.buffer = new Array<LogEntry | undefined>(capacity).fill(undefined);
  }

  push(kind: LogKind, detail: string, tMs: number = performance.now()): void {
    const entry: LogEntry = Object.freeze({ tMs, kind, detail });
    this.buffer[this.head] = entry;
    this.head = (this.head + 1) % this.capacity;
    if (this.size < this.capacity) this.size++;
    this.total++;
    this.totals.set(kind, (this.totals.get(kind) ?? 0) + 1);
    for (const fn of Array.from(this.subscribers)) {
      try {
        fn(entry);
      } catch (err) {
        // A broken UI subscriber must not stop logging or starve the other subscribers.
        console.error('EventLog subscriber failed', err);
      }
    }
  }

  /** Retained entries, oldest → newest. */
  entries(): LogEntry[] {
    const out: LogEntry[] = [];
    const start = (this.head - this.size + this.capacity) % this.capacity;
    for (let i = 0; i < this.size; i++) {
      const entry = this.buffer[(start + i) % this.capacity];
      if (entry) out.push(entry);
    }
    return out;
  }

  /** Number of entries ever pushed (of `kind`, or of every kind), including evicted ones. */
  count(kind?: LogKind): number {
    return kind === undefined ? this.total : (this.totals.get(kind) ?? 0);
  }

  /** Calls `fn` for every later push; returns the unsubscribe function. */
  subscribe(fn: (e: LogEntry) => void): () => void {
    this.subscribers.add(fn);
    return () => {
      this.subscribers.delete(fn);
    };
  }
}

/** Uniform error text for log details: "<Name>: <message>", or just the message for a plain Error. */
export function errText(e: unknown): string {
  if (e instanceof Error) return e.name && e.name !== 'Error' ? `${e.name}: ${e.message}` : e.message;
  return String(e);
}

/**
 * Rejects with `Error('<label> timed out after <ms> ms')` if `p` has not settled within `ms`, so a
 * hung browser promise cannot stall the caller; otherwise settles exactly like `p` (a rejection
 * value is passed through unchanged — `errText` formats any value).
 */
export function withTimeout<T>(p: Promise<T>, ms: number, label: string): Promise<T> {
  return new Promise<T>((resolve, reject) => {
    const timer = setTimeout(() => reject(new Error(`${label} timed out after ${ms} ms`)), ms);
    p.then(
      (v) => {
        clearTimeout(timer);
        resolve(v);
      },
      (e: unknown) => {
        clearTimeout(timer);
        reject(e);
      },
    );
  });
}
```

`errText` is the one formatter every probe module (T7, T8, T9, T10) uses for thrown values, so the
same failure reads the same in every log line and in the QR log tail. `withTimeout` is the one
timeout wrapper T7 (`addModule`, `close`, the 500 ms resume check) and T9 (Cache Storage,
service-worker registration) import; the two `withTimeout` tests above pin its pass-through and
timeout behaviour and that its timer is cleared once `p` settles (removing either `clearTimeout`
fails the first one), and T7's and T9's fake-timer tests exercise it again in context.

`Array.from(this.subscribers)` snapshots the set so a subscriber may unsubscribe (or subscribe
another) during notification without affecting the current delivery.

```ts
  lastSeen: '2026-01-02T00:00:00.000Z',
  cacheStorage: true,
  cachePersisted: true,
  cacheAgeS: 86400,
  swSupported: true,
  swRegistered: true,
  swControlled: false,
};

const inputs: InputCounts = {
  mediaSession: { nexttrack: 2, previoustrack: 1 },
  keys: ['MediaTrackNext'],
  wheel: 0,
  gamepads: [],
  visibilityChanges: 2,
};

function parts(): Omit<ProbeSummary, 'v' | 'logTail'> {
  return {
    sha: 'abc1234',
    at: '2026-01-02T03:04:05.000Z',
    env: { ua: 'Mozilla/5.0 (X11; Linux x86_64)', viewport, webgl: 'x', mcu: 'intel', features },
    audio: { ...audioState, bench: [{ k: 1, ratio: 1, overloaded: false, busyFrac: 0.1 }], headroomK: 1 },
    routes: {
      'direct-anchor': {
        miniplayer: 'yes',
        survivesMinimise: '?',
        wheelTilt: 'no',
        music: 'paused',
        hiddenClockRatio: null,
      },
    },
    gps: [gpsSummary],
    motion,
    storage,
    inputs,
    notes: { model: 'Model 3 2021' },
  };
}

describe('FORBIDDEN_KEYS', () => {
  it('lists every coordinate-bearing key name', () => {
    expect([...FORBIDDEN_KEYS]).toEqual(['lat', 'lon', 'latitude', 'longitude', 'coords', 'position']);
  });
});

describe('assertNoCoordinates', () => {
  it('accepts primitives, null and clean nested data', () => {
    expect(() => assertNoCoordinates(null)).not.toThrow();
    expect(() => assertNoCoordinates(42)).not.toThrow();
    expect(() => assertNoCoordinates('lat')).not.toThrow(); // values are not keys
    expect(() => assertNoCoordinates({ a: [{ b: { latency: 1, plateau: 2 } }] })).not.toThrow();
  });

  it.each(FORBIDDEN_KEYS.map((k) => [k]))('rejects key "%s" and names its path', (key) => {
    const value = { gps: [{ fixes: 1 }, { nested: { [key]: 0 } }] };
    expect(() => assertNoCoordinates(value)).toThrow(`$.gps[1].nested.${key}`);
  });

  it('matches keys case-insensitively', () => {
    expect(() => assertNoCoordinates({ Latitude: 1 })).toThrow('$.Latitude');
    expect(() => assertNoCoordinates({ COORDS: {} })).toThrow('$.COORDS');
  });

  it('rejects a forbidden key at the root and inside arrays of arrays', () => {
    expect(() => assertNoCoordinates({ lon: 0 })).toThrow('$.lon');
    expect(() => assertNoCoordinates([[{ position: 1 }]])).toThrow('$[0][0].position');
  });

  it('terminates on cyclic structures', () => {
    const a: Record<string, unknown> = { name: 'a' };
    a.self = a;
    expect(() => assertNoCoordinates(a)).not.toThrow();
  });
});

describe('buildSummary', () => {
  it('adds v: 1 and the formatted log tail', () => {
    const log = new EventLog();
    log.push('audio', 'running', 12_345);
    log.push('mediasession', 'nexttrack', 20_000);
    const summary = buildSummary(parts(), log);
    expect(summary.v).toBe(1);
    expect(summary.sha).toBe('abc1234');
    expect(summary.logTail).toEqual(['t=12.3s audio running', 't=20.0s mediasession nexttrack']);
  });

  it('keeps only the last 40 log entries', () => {
    const log = new EventLog();
    for (let i = 0; i < 45; i++) log.push('note', `n${i}`, i * 100);
    const { logTail } = buildSummary(parts(), log);
    expect(logTail).toHaveLength(40);
    expect(logTail[0]).toBe('t=0.5s note n5');
    expect(logTail[39]).toBe('t=4.4s note n44');
  });

  it('survives a JSON round trip unchanged and starts with "v"', () => {
    const summary = buildSummary(parts(), new EventLog());
    const json = JSON.stringify(summary);
    expect(json.startsWith('{"v":1,')).toBe(true);
    expect(JSON.parse(json)).toEqual(summary);
  });

  it('drops properties that are not part of ProbeSummary', () => {
    const extra = { ...parts(), debugCoords: 'x' } as Omit<ProbeSummary, 'v' | 'logTail'>;
    expect(Object.keys(buildSummary(extra, new EventLog()))).toEqual([
      'v', 'sha', 'at', 'env', 'audio', 'routes', 'gps', 'motion', 'storage', 'inputs', 'notes', 'logTail',
    ]);
  });

  it('accepts null audio, motion and storage', () => {
    const summary = buildSummary({ ...parts(), audio: null, motion: null, storage: null }, new EventLog());
    expect(summary.audio).toBeNull();
  });

  it('throws when a note smuggles in a coordinate key', () => {
    const p = parts();
    p.notes = { lat: '0.000' };
    expect(() => buildSummary(p, new EventLog())).toThrow('$.notes.lat');
  });

  it('throws when nested data carries coordinates', () => {
    const gps = { ...gpsSummary, coords: { latitude: 0, longitude: 0 } } as GpsModeSummary;
    expect(() => buildSummary({ ...parts(), gps: [gps] }, new EventLog())).toThrow('$.gps[0].coords');
  });
});
```

Fixture values are illustrative statistics only — no positions anywhere.

- [ ] **Step 8: Implement `src/probe/summary.ts`**

```ts
import type { OutputRoute } from '../contracts';
import type { Features, Mcu, ViewportInfo } from '../platform/detect';
import type { AudioProbeState, BenchResult } from './audio';
import type { GpsModeSummary } from './gps-stats';
import type { InputCounts, MotionReport } from './inputs';
import type { EventLog, LogEntry } from './log';
import type { StorageReport } from './storage-probe';

export interface RouteObservation {
  miniplayer: 'yes' | 'no' | '?';
  survivesMinimise: 'yes' | 'no' | '?';
  wheelTilt: 'yes' | 'no' | '?';
  music: 'paused' | 'mixed' | '?';
  hiddenClockRatio: number | null;
}

/** Statistics-only probe export (spec §10.2 Diagnostics QR). Never contains coordinates. */
export interface ProbeSummary {
  v: 1;
  sha: string;
  /** ISO timestamp. */
  at: string;
  env: { ua: string; viewport: ViewportInfo; webgl: string | null; mcu: Mcu; features: Features };
  audio: (AudioProbeState & { bench: BenchResult[]; headroomK: number | null }) | null;
  routes: Partial<Record<OutputRoute, RouteObservation>>;
  gps: GpsModeSummary[];
  motion: MotionReport | null;
  storage: StorageReport | null;
  inputs: InputCounts;
  notes: Record<string, string>;
  /** Last ≤ 40 log entries formatted "t=12.3s kind detail". */
  logTail: string[];
}

export const FORBIDDEN_KEYS: readonly string[] = Object.freeze([
  'lat',
  'lon',
  'latitude',
  'longitude',
  'coords',
  'position',
]);

const FORBIDDEN = new Set(FORBIDDEN_KEYS);
const LOG_TAIL = 40;

/** One log entry as text: "t=12.3s kind detail". Shared by the QR log tail and the probe page's log view. */
export function formatEntry(e: LogEntry): string {
  return `t=${(e.tMs / 1000).toFixed(1)}s ${e.kind} ${e.detail}`;
}

/**
 * Throws if any own enumerable key at any depth matches FORBIDDEN_KEYS (case-insensitive).
 * The message names the JSON path, e.g. `$.gps[0].lat`. Cycles are visited once.
 */
export function assertNoCoordinates(value: unknown): void {
  const seen = new WeakSet<object>();
  const walk = (node: unknown, path: string): void => {
    if (typeof node !== 'object' || node === null || ArrayBuffer.isView(node)) return;
    if (seen.has(node)) return;
    seen.add(node);
    if (Array.isArray(node)) {
      node.forEach((item, i) => walk(item, `${path}[${i}]`));
      return;
    }
    for (const [key, child] of Object.entries(node)) {
      const childPath = `${path}.${key}`;
      if (FORBIDDEN.has(key.toLowerCase())) {
        throw new Error(`Probe summary contains forbidden coordinate key at ${childPath}`);
      }
      walk(child, childPath);
    }
  };
  walk(value, '$');
}

/** Assembles the export: adds `v: 1` and the formatted log tail, then enforces the privacy guard. */
export function buildSummary(parts: Omit<ProbeSummary, 'v' | 'logTail'>, log: EventLog): ProbeSummary {
  const summary: ProbeSummary = {
    v: 1,
    sha: parts.sha,
    at: parts.at,
    env: parts.env,
    audio: parts.audio,
    routes: parts.routes,
    gps: parts.gps,
    motion: parts.motion,
    storage: parts.storage,
    inputs: parts.inputs,
    notes: parts.notes,
    logTail: log.entries().slice(-LOG_TAIL).map(formatEntry),
  };
  assertNoCoordinates(summary);
  return summary;
}
```

The guard matches keys case-insensitively (stricter than an exact match, so `Latitude`/`COORDS`
are caught too) and walks only own enumerable string keys — the same keys `JSON.stringify`
serialises.

### Task 5: Geodesy, GPS statistics and the GPS mode runner

**Files:**
- Create: `src/platform/geo.ts`
- Create: `src/probe/gps-stats.ts`
- Create: `src/probe/gps.ts`
- Test: `tests/unit/geo.test.ts`
- Test: `tests/unit/gps-stats.test.ts`
- Test: `tests/unit/gps.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes:
  - `src/platform/storage.ts` (T2): `getJSON<T>(key: string, fallback: T): T`, `setJSON(key: string, value: unknown): boolean`, `remove(key: string): void`. storage.ts adds the `tesbox.v1.` prefix, so keys passed here are unprefixed.
  - `src/probe/log.ts` (T4): `class EventLog { constructor(capacity?: number); push(kind: LogKind, detail: string, tMs?: number): void; entries(): LogEntry[]; count(kind?: LogKind): number; subscribe(fn: (e: LogEntry) => void): () => void }`, with `LogKind` including `'gps'` and `'error'`; `errText(e: unknown): string` (value import; the single error formatter for log details).
  - DOM lib: `Geolocation`, `GeolocationPosition`, `GeolocationPositionError`, `PositionOptions`.
- Produces:
  - `src/platform/geo.ts`: `export function haversineM(lat1: number, lon1: number, lat2: number, lon2: number): number`. It uses R = 6371008.8 m, allocates nothing, and never returns NaN, even for antipodal points. Phase 1 SimCore imports it.
  - `src/probe/gps-stats.ts`: `export interface GpsFix`, `export interface GpsModeSummary` (the exact contract fields) and `export class GpsStats { constructor(mode: string); add(fix: GpsFix): void; addError(code: 1 | 2 | 3): void; summary(durationS: number, crashedPreviously: boolean): GpsModeSummary }`. Every `summary()` call returns a fresh object. Rounding keeps the QR payload compact (the contract leaves this open): percentages are 0–100 to 0.1; interval and age statistics are integer ms; `accuracyMedianM` is to 0.1 m; `maxSpeedMps` is to 0.01. A non-finite `speedMps` counts as null.
  - `src/probe/gps.ts`: `export interface GpsMode { method: 'poll' | 'watch'; highAccuracy: boolean }`, `export const GPS_MODES: GpsMode[]`, `export function modeName(m: GpsMode): string`, `export const CRASH_KEY = 'probe.gpsRun'` and `export class GpsRunner { constructor(geo: Geolocation, log: EventLog, now?: () => number); readonly running: boolean; run(mode: GpsMode, durationS: number, isHidden?: () => boolean): Promise<GpsModeSummary>; stop(): void; static previousCrash(): { mode: string; startedAt: string } | null }`.
  - Behaviour later tasks rely on:
    - `run()` returns a promise rejected with `Error('GPS run already in progress (<mode>)')` while another run is active.
    - The summary's `durationS` is the elapsed time in seconds, to 0.1 s and capped at the requested duration. `stop()` resolves the pending promise synchronously.
    - `crashedPreviously` is `true` on the first later run, in this page session, of a mode whose crash marker was found, either by `previousCrash()` or by `run()` itself.
    - Log lines are kind `'gps'`: `run <mode> start <d>s`, `fix age=<int>ms acc=<int>m speed=<null|x.xx>`, `error code=<1|2|3>` and `run <mode> end fixes=<n> errors=<perm>/<unavail>/<timeout>`. Coordinates are never logged.
    - The default `isHidden` is `document.visibilityState === 'hidden'`, which is false when there is no document.

**Cross-task premises:**
- T1: `vite.config.ts` runs Vitest with `include: ['tests/unit/**/*.test.ts']` and `environment: 'node'`. `tsconfig.json` has `strict`, `noUncheckedIndexedAccess` and `types: ["vite/client", "node"]`, and includes `src` and `tests`. The `lint`, `typecheck` and `test` scripts exist.
- T2: `src/platform/storage.ts` exports `getJSON`, `setJSON` and `remove` with the contract signatures and adds the prefix itself. `tests/unit/gps.test.ts` replaces the module with `vi.mock`, so only the export names and signatures matter here.
- T4: `src/probe/log.ts` exports `EventLog` with the contract API and `LogKind` including `'gps'` and `'error'`. `push()` defaults `tMs` to `performance.now()`.
- T4 `assertNoCoordinates`: `GpsModeSummary` has no `lat`, `lon`, `latitude`, `longitude`, `coords` or `position` key at any depth. This holds by construction, and the GpsStats test asserts it.
- T10: calls `GpsRunner.previousCrash()` once on page load to show the crash banner, passes `navigator.geolocation` to the constructor, lists `GPS_MODES` with `modeName()` labels, and disables the **Run** buttons while `running` is true.
- Phase 1 imports `haversineM` from `src/platform/geo.ts` with exactly this signature.

Test-design notes:
- Vitest 5 `vi.useFakeTimers()` fakes `performance` and `Date`.
- When fake timers run a callback, a `setTimeout(fn, 0)` scheduled inside it is clamped to 1 ms. So `gps.ts` starts the next poll directly when no wait is needed, rather than through a 0 ms timer.
- `previousCrash()` records crashed modes in module-level state (it is static). The RF2 test therefore consumes every marker it plants by running that mode, which keeps tests independent.

```ts
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { haversineM } from '../../src/platform/geo';

const R = 6371008.8;
const ONE_DEG_M = (R * Math.PI) / 180; // 111195.08 m

describe('haversineM', () => {
  it('is 0 for identical points', () => {
    expect(haversineM(0, 0, 0, 0)).toBe(0);
    expect(haversineM(0.25, -0.5, 0.25, -0.5)).toBe(0);
  });

  it('gives R·Δφ for a pure latitude step', () => {
    expect(haversineM(0, 0, 1, 0)).toBeCloseTo(ONE_DEG_M, 3);
  });

  it('gives R·Δλ for a longitude step on the equator', () => {
    expect(haversineM(0, 0, 0, 1)).toBeCloseTo(ONE_DEG_M, 3);
  });

  it('shrinks longitude steps by cos(latitude)', () => {
    // 1e-4° of longitude at 60° latitude: R·Δλ·cos 60° = 5.5598 m
    expect(haversineM(60, 0, 60, 0.0001)).toBeCloseTo((ONE_DEG_M / 10000) * 0.5, 4);
  });

  it('is accurate for sub-metre steps (no cancellation)', () => {
    // 1e-6° of latitude = 0.111195 m
    expect(haversineM(0, 0, 1e-6, 0)).toBeCloseTo(ONE_DEG_M * 1e-6, 6);
  });

  it('is symmetric', () => {
    expect(haversineM(0.1, 0.2, -0.3, 0.4)).toBeCloseTo(haversineM(-0.3, 0.4, 0.1, 0.2), 9);
  });

  it('returns π·R for antipodal points and never NaN', () => {
    const d = haversineM(0, 0, 0, 180);
    expect(Number.isNaN(d)).toBe(false);
    expect(d).toBeCloseTo(Math.PI * R, 0);
    expect(haversineM(90, 0, -90, 0)).toBeCloseTo(Math.PI * R, 0);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `haversineM`**

Create `src/platform/geo.ts`:

```ts
/** Mean Earth radius (IUGG R1), metres. */
const EARTH_RADIUS_M = 6371008.8;
const DEG_TO_RAD = Math.PI / 180;

/**
 * Great-circle distance in metres between two WGS84 points (haversine formula, spherical Earth).
 * Allocation-free: safe to call from SimCore's per-fix path (Phase 1).
 */
export function haversineM(lat1: number, lon1: number, lat2: number, lon2: number): number {
  const sinHalfDLat = Math.sin(((lat2 - lat1) * DEG_TO_RAD) / 2);
  const sinHalfDLon = Math.sin(((lon2 - lon1) * DEG_TO_RAD) / 2);
  const h =
    sinHalfDLat * sinHalfDLat +
    Math.cos(lat1 * DEG_TO_RAD) * Math.cos(lat2 * DEG_TO_RAD) * sinHalfDLon * sinHalfDLon;
  // Rounding can push h a hair above 1 for antipodal points; asin(>1) would be NaN.
  return 2 * EARTH_RADIUS_M * Math.asin(Math.sqrt(Math.min(1, h)));
}
```

```ts
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { GpsStats, type GpsFix } from '../../src/probe/gps-stats';

const M_PER_DEG = (6371008.8 * Math.PI) / 180;
/** Latitude (degrees) of a point `m` metres north of 0,0. Fake positions only. */
const north = (m: number): number => m / M_PER_DEG;

function fix(over: Partial<GpsFix> = {}): GpsFix {
  return {
    arrivalPerfMs: 0,
    arrivalEpochMs: 1_000_000,
    timestampMs: 999_000,
    lat: 0,
    lon: 0,
    accuracyM: 5,
    speedMps: 10,
    altitudeM: null,
    hidden: false,
    ...over,
  };
}

describe('GpsStats', () => {
  it('summarises an empty run with zeros and nulls', () => {
    const s = new GpsStats('poll-hiacc-off').summary(60, false);
    expect(s).toEqual({
      mode: 'poll-hiacc-off',
      durationS: 60,
      fixes: 0,
      errors: { permission: 0, unavailable: 0, timeout: 0 },
      intervalMedianMs: null,
      intervalP95Ms: null,
      ageMedianMs: null,
      ageP95Ms: null,
      staleOver3sPct: 0,
      duplicatePct: 0,
      speedNullPct: 0,
      speedZeroPct: 0,
      speedZeroWhileMovingPct: 0,
      accuracyMedianM: null,
      altitudePresentPct: 0,
      fixesWhileHidden: 0,
      maxSpeedMps: null,
      crashedPreviously: false,
    });
  });

  it('computes interval and age percentiles by nearest rank', () => {
    const st = new GpsStats('m');
    // arrivals 0, 1000, 2100, 3000 → intervals [1000, 1100, 900]
    // ages 1000, 2000, 3000, 4000 (arrivalEpoch − timestamp)
    const arrivals = [0, 1000, 2100, 3000];
    arrivals.forEach((a, i) =>
      st.add(fix({ arrivalPerfMs: a, arrivalEpochMs: 10_000 + a, timestampMs: 10_000 + a - (i + 1) * 1000, lat: north(i) })),
    );
    const s = st.summary(4, false);
    expect(s.fixes).toBe(4);
    expect(s.intervalMedianMs).toBe(1000); // sorted [900,1000,1100], rank ceil(1.5)=2
    expect(s.intervalP95Ms).toBe(1100); // rank ceil(2.85)=3
    expect(s.ageMedianMs).toBe(2000); // sorted [1000..4000], rank 2
    expect(s.ageP95Ms).toBe(4000); // rank ceil(3.8)=4
    expect(s.staleOver3sPct).toBe(25); // only 4000 > 3000 (3000 is not stale)
  });

  it('uses nearest-rank p95 = 19th of 20 values', () => {
    const st = new GpsStats('m');
    for (let i = 0; i < 20; i++) {
      st.add(fix({ arrivalPerfMs: i * 1000, accuracyM: i + 1, lat: north(i * 10), timestampMs: i }));
    }
    const s = st.summary(20, false);
    expect(s.accuracyMedianM).toBe(10); // rank 10 of 1..20
    expect(s.intervalMedianMs).toBe(1000);
    // ages = 1_000_000 − i → sorted 999_981 … 1_000_000: nearest-rank p95 is the 19th value, not the max
    expect(s.ageMedianMs).toBe(999_990);
    expect(s.ageP95Ms).toBe(999_999);
  });

  it('counts duplicates only against the immediately previous fix', () => {
    const st = new GpsStats('m');
    st.add(fix({ arrivalPerfMs: 0, timestampMs: 1, lat: north(0) }));
    st.add(fix({ arrivalPerfMs: 1000, timestampMs: 1, lat: north(0) })); // duplicate of previous
    st.add(fix({ arrivalPerfMs: 2000, timestampMs: 2, lat: north(5) }));
    st.add(fix({ arrivalPerfMs: 3000, timestampMs: 1, lat: north(0) })); // equals #1, not the previous
    st.add(fix({ arrivalPerfMs: 4000, timestampMs: 2, lat: north(6) })); // same timestamp, moved
    expect(st.summary(5, false).duplicatePct).toBe(20);
  });

  it('reports speed null/zero percentages, max speed and altitude presence', () => {
    const st = new GpsStats('m');
    st.add(fix({ arrivalPerfMs: 0, speedMps: null, altitudeM: 12 }));
    st.add(fix({ arrivalPerfMs: 1000, speedMps: 0, timestampMs: 1 }));
    st.add(fix({ arrivalPerfMs: 2000, speedMps: Number.NaN, timestampMs: 2 })); // non-finite → null
    st.add(fix({ arrivalPerfMs: 3000, speedMps: 13.456, altitudeM: 0, timestampMs: 3 }));
    const s = st.summary(4, false);
    expect(s.speedNullPct).toBe(50);
    expect(s.speedZeroPct).toBe(25);
    expect(s.maxSpeedMps).toBe(13.46);
    expect(s.altitudePresentPct).toBe(50); // 12 and 0 are both present
  });

  it('rounds percentages to one decimal', () => {
    const st = new GpsStats('m');
    st.add(fix({ arrivalPerfMs: 0, speedMps: null }));
    st.add(fix({ arrivalPerfMs: 1000, timestampMs: 1 }));
    st.add(fix({ arrivalPerfMs: 2000, timestampMs: 2 }));
    expect(st.summary(3, false).speedNullPct).toBe(33.3);
  });

  it('flags speed=0 while moving > 20 m relative to the fix ≥ 5 s earlier', () => {
    const st = new GpsStats('m');
    // Driving north at 10 m/s, one fix per second; speed reported 0 at t=3 s and t=6 s.
    for (let t = 0; t <= 7; t++) {
      st.add(
        fix({
          arrivalPerfMs: t * 1000,
          timestampMs: t * 1000,
          lat: north(10 * t),
          speedMps: t === 3 || t === 6 ? 0 : 10,
        }),
      );
    }
    const s = st.summary(8, false);
    // t=3: no fix arrived ≤ −2000 → not counted. t=6: fix at t=1 (arrival 1000 ≤ 1000) is 50 m back → counted.
    expect(s.speedZeroPct).toBe(25);
    expect(s.speedZeroWhileMovingPct).toBe(12.5);
  });

  it('measures from the NEAREST fix ≥ 5 s earlier, not an older one', () => {
    const st = new GpsStats('m');
    // 100 m north at t=0, then parked at 100 m from t=1 s; speed reads 0 at t=7 s.
    st.add(fix({ arrivalPerfMs: 0, timestampMs: 0, lat: north(0), speedMps: 10 }));
    for (let t = 1; t <= 7; t++) {
      st.add(fix({ arrivalPerfMs: t * 1000, timestampMs: t * 1000, lat: north(100), speedMps: t === 7 ? 0 : 0.5 }));
    }
    // Nearest fix with arrival ≤ 2000 ms is t=2 (0 m away): not moving. The t=0 fix (100 m) must not be used.
    expect(st.summary(8, false).speedZeroWhileMovingPct).toBe(0);
  });

  it('does not flag speed=0 while parked (GPS jitter < 20 m)', () => {
    const st = new GpsStats('m');
    for (let t = 0; t <= 7; t++) {
      st.add(fix({ arrivalPerfMs: t * 1000, timestampMs: t * 1000, lat: north(t % 2 === 0 ? 0 : 3), speedMps: 0 }));
    }
    const s = st.summary(8, false);
    expect(s.speedZeroPct).toBe(100);
    expect(s.speedZeroWhileMovingPct).toBe(0);
  });

  it('counts errors per code, hidden fixes and passes duration/crash flag through', () => {
    const st = new GpsStats('watch-hiacc-on');
    st.addError(1);
    st.addError(2);
    st.addError(3);
    st.addError(3);
    st.add(fix({ hidden: true }));
    st.add(fix({ arrivalPerfMs: 1000, timestampMs: 5, hidden: false }));
    const s = st.summary(12.5, true);
    expect(s.errors).toEqual({ permission: 1, unavailable: 1, timeout: 2 });
    expect(s.fixesWhileHidden).toBe(1);
    expect(s.durationS).toBe(12.5);
    expect(s.crashedPreviously).toBe(true);
    expect(s.mode).toBe('watch-hiacc-on');
  });

  it('is stateful across calls: summary() is repeatable and later adds update it', () => {
    const st = new GpsStats('m');
    st.add(fix({ arrivalPerfMs: 0 }));
    const a = st.summary(1, false);
    const b = st.summary(1, false);
    expect(b).toEqual(a);
    b.errors.permission = 99; // mutating a returned summary must not leak into the stats
    st.add(fix({ arrivalPerfMs: 1000, timestampMs: 7 }));
    const c = st.summary(2, false);
    expect(c.fixes).toBe(2);
    expect(c.intervalMedianMs).toBe(1000);
    expect(c.errors.permission).toBe(0);
  });

  it('never exposes coordinates in the summary', () => {
    const st = new GpsStats('m');
    st.add(fix({ lat: 0.0123456, lon: 0.0654321 }));
    const json = JSON.stringify(st.summary(1, false));
    expect(json).not.toContain('0.0123456');
    expect(json).not.toContain('0.0654321');
    expect(json).not.toMatch(/"lat"|"lon"/);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 7: Implement GpsStats**

Create `src/probe/gps-stats.ts`:

```ts
import { haversineM } from '../platform/geo';

export interface GpsFix {
  arrivalPerfMs: number;
  arrivalEpochMs: number;
  timestampMs: number;
  lat: number;
  lon: number;
  accuracyM: number;
  speedMps: number | null;
  altitudeM: number | null;
  hidden: boolean;
}

export interface GpsModeSummary {
  mode: string;
  durationS: number;
  fixes: number;
  errors: { permission: number; unavailable: number; timeout: number };
  intervalMedianMs: number | null;
  intervalP95Ms: number | null;
  ageMedianMs: number | null;
  ageP95Ms: number | null;
  staleOver3sPct: number;
  duplicatePct: number;
  speedNullPct: number;
  speedZeroPct: number;
  speedZeroWhileMovingPct: number;
  accuracyMedianM: number | null;
  altitudePresentPct: number;
  fixesWhileHidden: number;
  maxSpeedMps: number | null;
  crashedPreviously: boolean;
}

const STALE_AGE_MS = 3000;
const MOVING_LOOKBACK_MS = 5000;
const MOVING_DISTANCE_M = 20;

/** Nearest-rank percentile (p in 1..100) of an ascending array; null when empty. */
function nearestRank(sorted: readonly number[], p: number): number | null {
  const n = sorted.length;
  if (n === 0) return null;
  const rank = Math.min(n, Math.max(1, Math.ceil((p * n) / 100)));
  return sorted[rank - 1] ?? null;
}

function ascending(values: readonly number[]): number[] {
  return [...values].sort((a, b) => a - b);
}

/** Rounds to `digits` decimals; keeps the QR summary compact. */
function round(value: number | null, digits: number): number | null {
  if (value === null) return null;
  const f = 10 ** digits;
  return Math.round(value * f) / f;
}

/** Collects one GPS mode run. Coordinates stay inside this object and never appear in the summary. */
export class GpsStats {
  private readonly fixList: GpsFix[] = [];
  private readonly intervals: number[] = [];
  private readonly ages: number[] = [];
  private readonly accuracies: number[] = [];
  private readonly errors = { permission: 0, unavailable: 0, timeout: 0 };
  private stale = 0;
  private duplicates = 0;
  private speedNull = 0;
  private speedZero = 0;
  private speedZeroMoving = 0;
  private altitudePresent = 0;
  private hiddenFixes = 0;
  private maxSpeed: number | null = null;

  constructor(private readonly mode: string) {}

  add(fix: GpsFix): void {
    const prev = this.fixList[this.fixList.length - 1];
    if (prev !== undefined) {
      this.intervals.push(fix.arrivalPerfMs - prev.arrivalPerfMs);
      if (prev.timestampMs === fix.timestampMs && prev.lat === fix.lat && prev.lon === fix.lon) {
        this.duplicates++;
      }
    }
    const ageMs = fix.arrivalEpochMs - fix.timestampMs;
    this.ages.push(ageMs);
    if (ageMs > STALE_AGE_MS) this.stale++;

    const speed = fix.speedMps !== null && Number.isFinite(fix.speedMps) ? fix.speedMps : null;
    if (speed === null) {
      this.speedNull++;
    } else {
      if (speed === 0) {
        this.speedZero++;
        if (this.movedOverThreshold(fix)) this.speedZeroMoving++;
      }
      if (this.maxSpeed === null || speed > this.maxSpeed) this.maxSpeed = speed;
    }

    this.accuracies.push(fix.accuracyM);
    if (fix.altitudeM !== null) this.altitudePresent++;
    if (fix.hidden) this.hiddenFixes++;
    this.fixList.push(fix);
  }

  addError(code: 1 | 2 | 3): void {
    if (code === 1) this.errors.permission++;
    else if (code === 2) this.errors.unavailable++;
    else this.errors.timeout++;
  }

  summary(durationS: number, crashedPreviously: boolean): GpsModeSummary {
    const fixes = this.fixList.length;
    const pct = (n: number): number => (fixes === 0 ? 0 : Math.round((n / fixes) * 1000) / 10);
    const intervals = ascending(this.intervals);
    const ages = ascending(this.ages);
    const accuracies = ascending(this.accuracies);
    return {
      mode: this.mode,
      durationS,
      fixes,
      errors: { ...this.errors },
      intervalMedianMs: round(nearestRank(intervals, 50), 0),
      intervalP95Ms: round(nearestRank(intervals, 95), 0),
      ageMedianMs: round(nearestRank(ages, 50), 0),
      ageP95Ms: round(nearestRank(ages, 95), 0),
      staleOver3sPct: pct(this.stale),
      duplicatePct: pct(this.duplicates),
      speedNullPct: pct(this.speedNull),
      speedZeroPct: pct(this.speedZero),
      speedZeroWhileMovingPct: pct(this.speedZeroMoving),
      accuracyMedianM: round(nearestRank(accuracies, 50), 1),
      altitudePresentPct: pct(this.altitudePresent),
      fixesWhileHidden: this.hiddenFixes,
      maxSpeedMps: round(this.maxSpeed, 2),
      crashedPreviously,
    };
  }

  /** Displacement from the latest earlier fix that arrived ≥ 5 s before `fix` exceeds 20 m. */
  private movedOverThreshold(fix: GpsFix): boolean {
    const cutoff = fix.arrivalPerfMs - MOVING_LOOKBACK_MS;
    for (let i = this.fixList.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
      const earlier = this.fixList[i];
      if (earlier !== undefined && earlier.arrivalPerfMs <= cutoff) {
        return haversineM(earlier.lat, earlier.lon, fix.lat, fix.lon) > MOVING_DISTANCE_M;
      }
    }
    return false;
  }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 9: Write the failing GpsRunner test (includes RF1 and RF2)**

Create `tests/unit/gps.test.ts`. It uses a scriptable fake `Geolocation`, fake timers and an in-memory `vi.mock` of storage.ts.
- RF1 is the `GpsRunner errors (RF1)` block: permission denied on every request, timeouts, a request that never settles, `stop()` mid-run, and a late answer after `stop()`.
- RF2 is the `GpsRunner crash marker (RF2)` block.

```ts
import { describe, it, expect, vi, beforeEach, afterEach } from 'vitest';
import { EventLog } from '../../src/probe/log';

// In-memory stand-in for src/platform/storage.ts (contract API; keys arrive without the prefix).
const store = vi.hoisted(() => new Map<string, unknown>());
vi.mock('../../src/platform/storage', () => ({
  storageAvailable: (): boolean => true,
  getJSON: <T>(key: string, fallback: T): T => (store.has(key) ? (store.get(key) as T) : fallback),
  setJSON: (key: string, value: unknown): boolean => {
    store.set(key, JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(value)) as unknown);
    return true;
  },
  remove: (key: string): void => {
    store.delete(key);
  },
}));

import { CRASH_KEY, GPS_MODES, GpsRunner, modeName, type GpsMode } from '../../src/probe/gps';

const POLL: GpsMode = { method: 'poll', highAccuracy: false };
const WATCH_HI: GpsMode = { method: 'watch', highAccuracy: true };
const M_PER_DEG = (6371008.8 * Math.PI) / 180;
const FAKE_LON = 0.0654321; // fake position near 0,0 — must never appear in the log

function position(n: number, speed: number | null = 10): GeolocationPosition {
  const coords = {
    latitude: (10 * n) / M_PER_DEG,
    longitude: FAKE_LON,
    accuracy: 4.4,
    altitude: null,
    altitudeAccuracy: null,
    heading: null,
    speed,
    toJSON: () => ({}),
  };
  return { coords, timestamp: Date.now() - 800, toJSON: () => ({}) };
}

function positionError(code: 1 | 2 | 3): GeolocationPositionError {
  return { code, message: `code ${code}`, PERMISSION_DENIED: 1, POSITION_UNAVAILABLE: 2, TIMEOUT: 3 };
}

type Behaviour =
  | { kind: 'fix'; latencyMs: number }
  | { kind: 'error'; code: 1 | 2 | 3; latencyMs: number }
  | { kind: 'timeout' } // answers TIMEOUT after options.timeout, like a browser without a fix
  | { kind: 'hang' }; // never answers (e.g. permission prompt still open)

class FakeGeo implements Geolocation {
  behaviour: Behaviour = { kind: 'fix', latencyMs: 50 };
  requestOptions: PositionOptions[] = [];
  requestStarts: number[] = [];
  watchOptions: PositionOptions[] = [];
  clearedWatchIds: number[] = [];
  inFlight = 0;
  maxInFlight = 0;
  private n = 0;
  private nextWatchId = 41;
  private watches = new Map<number, ReturnType<typeof setInterval>>();

  getCurrentPosition(success: PositionCallback, error?: PositionErrorCallback | null, options?: PositionOptions): void {
    this.requestOptions.push(options ?? {});
    this.requestStarts.push(performance.now());
    this.inFlight++;
    this.maxInFlight = Math.max(this.maxInFlight, this.inFlight);
    const b = this.behaviour;
    if (b.kind === 'hang') return;
    const delay = b.kind === 'timeout' ? (options?.timeout ?? 0) : b.latencyMs;
    setTimeout(() => {
      this.inFlight--;
      if (b.kind === 'fix') success(position(this.n++));
      else error?.(positionError(b.kind === 'timeout' ? 3 : b.code));
    }, delay);
  }

  watchPosition(success: PositionCallback, error?: PositionErrorCallback | null, options?: PositionOptions): number {
    this.watchOptions.push(options ?? {});
    const id = this.nextWatchId++;
    const b = this.behaviour;
    this.watches.set(
      id,
      setInterval(() => {
        if (b.kind === 'fix') success(position(this.n++));
        else if (b.kind === 'error') error?.(positionError(b.code));
        else if (b.kind === 'timeout') error?.(positionError(3));
      }, 1000),
    );
    return id;
  }

  clearWatch(watchId: number): void {
    clearInterval(this.watches.get(watchId));
    this.watches.delete(watchId);
    this.clearedWatchIds.push(watchId);
  }
}

let geo: FakeGeo;
let log: EventLog;
let runner: GpsRunner;

beforeEach(() => {
  vi.useFakeTimers({ toFake: ['setTimeout', 'clearTimeout', 'setInterval', 'clearInterval', 'Date', 'performance'] });
  store.clear();
  geo = new FakeGeo();
  log = new EventLog();
  runner = new GpsRunner(geo, log);
});

afterEach(() => {
  runner.stop();
  vi.useRealTimers();
});

describe('GPS_MODES / modeName', () => {
  it('lists the 4 probe modes in contract order with stable names', () => {
    expect(GPS_MODES).toEqual([
      { method: 'poll', highAccuracy: false },
      { method: 'poll', highAccuracy: true },
      { method: 'watch', highAccuracy: false },
      { method: 'watch', highAccuracy: true },
    ]);
    expect(GPS_MODES.map(modeName)).toEqual(['poll-hiacc-off', 'poll-hiacc-on', 'watch-hiacc-off', 'watch-hiacc-on']);
  });
});

describe('GpsRunner poll mode', () => {
  it('keeps one request in flight with the contract options and ≥ 1000 ms spacing', async () => {
    const p = runner.run(POLL, 5);
    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(5000);
    const s = await p;
    expect(geo.requestOptions[0]).toEqual({ enableHighAccuracy: false, timeout: 2000, maximumAge: 0 });
    expect(geo.maxInFlight).toBe(1);
    expect(geo.requestStarts).toEqual([0, 1000, 2000, 3000, 4000]);
    expect(s.fixes).toBe(5);
    expect(s.durationS).toBe(5);
    expect(s.intervalMedianMs).toBe(1000);
    expect(s.ageMedianMs).toBe(800);
    expect(s.mode).toBe('poll-hiacc-off');
  });

  it('issues the next request as soon as a slow request settles', async () => {
    geo.behaviour = { kind: 'fix', latencyMs: 1500 };
    const p = runner.run(POLL, 5);
    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(5000);
    await p;
    expect(geo.requestStarts).toEqual([0, 1500, 3000, 4500]);
    expect(geo.maxInFlight).toBe(1);
  });

  it('records hidden fixes', async () => {
    let hidden = false;
    const p = runner.run(POLL, 4, () => hidden);
    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(2000);
    hidden = true;
    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(2000);
    const s = await p;
    expect(s.fixes).toBe(4);
    expect(s.fixesWhileHidden).toBe(2);
    expect(s.speedNullPct).toBe(0);
    expect(s.maxSpeedMps).toBe(10);
  });
});

describe('GpsRunner normalisation', () => {
  it('turns NaN speed and NaN altitude into null before the stats and the log', async () => {
    const nanGeo: Geolocation = {
      getCurrentPosition: (ok) => {
        const p = position(0, Number.NaN);
        setTimeout(() => ok({ ...p, coords: { ...p.coords, altitude: Number.NaN } }), 10);
      },
      watchPosition: () => 0,
      clearWatch: () => undefined,
    };
    const r = new GpsRunner(nanGeo, log);
    const pending = r.run(POLL, 1);
    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(1000);
    const s = await pending;
    expect(s.fixes).toBe(1);
    expect(s.speedNullPct).toBe(100);
    expect(s.altitudePresentPct).toBe(0);
    expect(log.entries().some((e) => e.detail.endsWith('speed=null'))).toBe(true);
  });
});

describe('GpsRunner errors (RF1)', () => {
  it('permission denied on every request: resolves at durationS with errors counted', async () => {
    geo.behaviour = { kind: 'error', code: 1, latencyMs: 10 };
    let done = false;
    const p = runner.run(POLL, 5).then((s) => {
      done = true;
      return s;
    });
    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(4999);
    expect(done).toBe(false);
    expect(runner.running).toBe(true);
    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(1);
    const s = await p;
    expect(done).toBe(true);
    expect(runner.running).toBe(false);
    expect(s.durationS).toBe(5);
    expect(s.fixes).toBe(0);
    expect(s.errors).toEqual({ permission: 5, unavailable: 0, timeout: 0 });
    expect(log.entries().filter((e) => e.detail === 'error code=1')).toHaveLength(5);
  });

  it('timeouts: one request in flight, each times out after 2 s, run still ends at durationS', async () => {
    geo.behaviour = { kind: 'timeout' };
    const p = runner.run(POLL, 5);
    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(5000);
    const s = await p;
    expect(geo.maxInFlight).toBe(1);
    expect(geo.requestStarts).toEqual([0, 2000, 4000]);
    expect(s.errors.timeout).toBe(2); // the request started at 4000 would time out at 6000, after the run
    expect(s.durationS).toBe(5);
  });

  it('a request that never settles does not block the end of the run', async () => {
    geo.behaviour = { kind: 'hang' };
    const p = runner.run(POLL, 3);
    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(3000);
    const s = await p;
    expect(geo.requestStarts).toEqual([0]);
    expect(s.fixes).toBe(0);
    expect(s.durationS).toBe(3);
  });

  it('stop() mid-run resolves promptly with the elapsed duration and leaves no timers', async () => {
    const p = runner.run(POLL, 60);
    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(2500);
    runner.stop();
    const s = await p; // no timer advance needed
    expect(runner.running).toBe(false);
    expect(s.durationS).toBe(2.5);
    expect(s.fixes).toBe(3);
    expect(vi.getTimerCount()).toBe(0);
    expect(store.has(CRASH_KEY)).toBe(false);
  });

  it('ignores an answer that arrives after stop()', async () => {
    geo.behaviour = { kind: 'fix', latencyMs: 400 };
    const p = runner.run(POLL, 60);
    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(1100); // request #2 started at 1000, answers at 1400
    runner.stop();
    const s = await p;
    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(5000);
    expect(s.fixes).toBe(1);
    expect(geo.requestStarts).toEqual([0, 1000]);
    expect(log.entries().filter((e) => e.detail.startsWith('fix '))).toHaveLength(1);
  });

  it('caps durationS at the requested duration when the end timer fires late', async () => {
    const late = new GpsRunner(geo, log, () => performance.now() * 1.2); // clock reads 6 s when the 5 s timer fires
    const p = late.run(POLL, 5);
    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(5000);
    expect((await p).durationS).toBe(5);
  });

  it('rejects a second run while one is active, then allows a new run', async () => {
    const p = runner.run(POLL, 2);
    await expect(runner.run(WATCH_HI, 2)).rejects.toThrow(/already in progress/);
    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(2000);
    await p;
    const p2 = runner.run(POLL, 1);
    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(1000);
    expect((await p2).fixes).toBe(1);
  });
});

describe('GpsRunner watch mode', () => {
  it('uses watchPosition with the contract options and clears the watch at the end', async () => {
    const p = runner.run(WATCH_HI, 5);
    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(5000);
    const s = await p;
    expect(geo.watchOptions).toEqual([{ enableHighAccuracy: true, timeout: 2000, maximumAge: 0 }]);
    expect(geo.requestStarts).toEqual([]);
    expect(s.fixes).toBe(4); // fake emits at 1..4 s; the 5 s emission is cancelled by the end of the run
    expect(s.mode).toBe('watch-hiacc-on');
    expect(geo.clearedWatchIds).toEqual([41]);
    expect(vi.getTimerCount()).toBe(0);
  });

  it('counts watch errors and clears the watch on stop()', async () => {
    geo.behaviour = { kind: 'error', code: 2, latencyMs: 0 };
    const p = runner.run(WATCH_HI, 30);
    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(3000);
    runner.stop();
    const s = await p;
    expect(s.errors).toEqual({ permission: 0, unavailable: 3, timeout: 0 });
    expect(geo.clearedWatchIds).toEqual([41]);
  });
});

describe('GpsRunner crash marker (RF2)', () => {
  it('writes {mode, startedAt} while running and clears it when the run ends', async () => {
    const p = runner.run(WATCH_HI, 2);
    const marker = store.get(CRASH_KEY) as { mode: string; startedAt: string };
    expect(marker.mode).toBe('watch-hiacc-on');
    expect(new Date(marker.startedAt).toISOString()).toBe(marker.startedAt);
    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(2000);
    await p;
    expect(store.has(CRASH_KEY)).toBe(false);
  });

  it('previousCrash() returns a leftover marker once, clears it, and flags the next run of that mode once', async () => {
    store.set(CRASH_KEY, { mode: 'poll-hiacc-off', startedAt: '2026-01-01T00:00:00.000Z' });
    expect(GpsRunner.previousCrash()).toEqual({ mode: 'poll-hiacc-off', startedAt: '2026-01-01T00:00:00.000Z' });
    expect(store.has(CRASH_KEY)).toBe(false);
    expect(GpsRunner.previousCrash()).toBeNull();

    const other = runner.run(WATCH_HI, 1);
    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(1000);
    expect((await other).crashedPreviously).toBe(false);

    const first = runner.run(POLL, 1);
    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(1000);
    expect((await first).crashedPreviously).toBe(true);

    const second = runner.run(POLL, 1);
    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(1000);
    expect((await second).crashedPreviously).toBe(false);
  });

  it('run() consumes a marker the page never read', async () => {
    store.set(CRASH_KEY, { mode: 'watch-hiacc-on', startedAt: '2026-01-01T00:00:00.000Z' });
    const p = runner.run(WATCH_HI, 1);
    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(1000);
    expect((await p).crashedPreviously).toBe(true);
    expect(GpsRunner.previousCrash()).toBeNull();
  });

  it('discards a corrupt marker', () => {
    store.set(CRASH_KEY, { mode: 7 });
    expect(GpsRunner.previousCrash()).toBeNull();
    expect(store.has(CRASH_KEY)).toBe(false);
  });
});

describe('GpsRunner privacy', () => {
  it('logs fixes as "fix age=…ms acc=…m speed=…" without coordinates', async () => {
    const p = runner.run(POLL, 3);
    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(3000);
    await p;
    const fixes = log.entries().filter((e) => e.kind === 'gps' && e.detail.startsWith('fix '));
    expect(fixes).toHaveLength(3);
    for (const e of fixes) expect(e.detail).toMatch(/^fix age=-?\d+ms acc=\d+m speed=(null|-?\d+\.\d{2})$/);
    const all = log.entries().map((e) => e.detail).join('\n');
    expect(all).not.toContain('0.0654321');
    expect(all).not.toContain(String(FAKE_LON));
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 10: Run it to see it fail**

Run: `npx vitest run tests/unit/gps.test.ts`
Expected: FAIL with `Error: Cannot find module '…src/probe/gps' imported from …`. Vitest prints the path either as `'../../src/probe/gps'` or as `'/src/probe/gps'`.

- [ ] **Step 11: Implement GpsRunner**

Create `src/probe/gps.ts`:
- Poll mode keeps exactly one `getCurrentPosition` in flight. The next request starts when the previous one settles, and never sooner than 1000 ms after the previous start.
- Watch mode uses `watchPosition`, and `clearWatch` runs when the run ends.
- An identity check against the active run drops every callback that arrives after the run ends.

```ts
import { getJSON, remove, setJSON } from '../platform/storage';
import { GpsStats, type GpsFix, type GpsModeSummary } from './gps-stats';
import { errText, type EventLog } from './log';

export interface GpsMode {
  method: 'poll' | 'watch';
  highAccuracy: boolean;
}

export const GPS_MODES: GpsMode[] = [
  { method: 'poll', highAccuracy: false },
  { method: 'poll', highAccuracy: true },
  { method: 'watch', highAccuracy: false },
  { method: 'watch', highAccuracy: true },
];

export function modeName(m: GpsMode): string {
  return `${m.method}-hiacc-${m.highAccuracy ? 'on' : 'off'}`;
}

/** Storage key of the crash marker (storage.ts adds the "tesbox.v1." prefix). */
export const CRASH_KEY = 'probe.gpsRun';

const REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS = 2000;
const POLL_MIN_SPACING_MS = 1000;

interface CrashMarker {
  mode: string;
  startedAt: string;
}

function isCrashMarker(v: unknown): v is CrashMarker {
  if (typeof v !== 'object' || v === null) return false;
  const r = v as Record<string, unknown>;
  return typeof r.mode === 'string' && typeof r.startedAt === 'string';
}

/**
 * Modes whose crash marker was found (by previousCrash() or at run start) and not yet reported.
 * Module-level because previousCrash() is static and usually runs before any GpsRunner exists.
 */
const crashedModes = new Set<string>();

function finiteOrNull(v: number | null | undefined): number | null {
  return typeof v === 'number' && Number.isFinite(v) ? v : null;
}

function documentHidden(): boolean {
  return typeof document !== 'undefined' && document.visibilityState === 'hidden';
}

type Timer = ReturnType<typeof setTimeout>;

interface ActiveRun {
  readonly name: string;
  readonly stats: GpsStats;
  readonly options: PositionOptions;
  readonly t0: number;
  readonly durationS: number;
  readonly crashedPreviously: boolean;
  readonly isHidden: () => boolean;
  readonly resolve: (summary: GpsModeSummary) => void;
  endTimer: Timer | null;
  pollTimer: Timer | null;
  watchId: number | null;
}

/** Runs one GPS mode of the probe matrix. Coordinates go only into GpsStats, never into the log. */
export class GpsRunner {
  private active: ActiveRun | null = null;

  constructor(
    private readonly geo: Geolocation,
    private readonly log: EventLog,
    private readonly now: () => number = () => performance.now(),
  ) {}

  get running(): boolean {
    return this.active !== null;
  }

  /** Reads and clears the crash marker left by a run that never finished (page crash/reload). */
  static previousCrash(): { mode: string; startedAt: string } | null {
    const raw = getJSON<unknown>(CRASH_KEY, null);
    if (raw === null) return null;
    remove(CRASH_KEY);
    if (!isCrashMarker(raw)) return null;
    crashedModes.add(raw.mode);
    return { mode: raw.mode, startedAt: raw.startedAt };
  }

  /** Writes crash marker {mode, startedAt}, runs for durationS, clears marker, resolves summary. */
  run(mode: GpsMode, durationS: number, isHidden: () => boolean = documentHidden): Promise<GpsModeSummary> {
    if (this.active !== null) {
      return Promise.reject(new Error(`GPS run already in progress (${this.active.name})`));
    }
    const name = modeName(mode);
    GpsRunner.previousCrash(); // consume a marker the page has not read yet
    const crashedPreviously = crashedModes.delete(name);
    const marker: CrashMarker = { mode: name, startedAt: new Date().toISOString() };
    setJSON(CRASH_KEY, marker);
    const safeDurationS = Number.isFinite(durationS) && durationS > 0 ? durationS : 0;

    return new Promise<GpsModeSummary>((resolve) => {
      const run: ActiveRun = {
        name,
        stats: new GpsStats(name),
        options: { enableHighAccuracy: mode.highAccuracy, timeout: REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS, maximumAge: 0 },
        t0: this.now(),
        durationS: safeDurationS,
        crashedPreviously,
        isHidden,
        resolve,
        endTimer: null,
        pollTimer: null,
        watchId: null,
      };
      this.active = run;
      this.log.push('gps', `run ${name} start ${safeDurationS}s`);
      run.endTimer = setTimeout(() => this.finish(run), safeDurationS * 1000);
      try {
        if (mode.method === 'poll') {
          this.poll(run);
        } else {
          run.watchId = this.geo.watchPosition(
            (pos) => this.onPosition(run, pos),
            (err) => this.onError(run, err),
            run.options,
          );
        }
      } catch (e) {
        // A throwing Geolocation implementation must not leave the runner stuck; the run still ends at durationS.
        this.log.push('error', `gps ${name} start failed: ${errText(e)}`);
      }
    });
  }

  stop(): void {
    if (this.active !== null) this.finish(this.active);
  }

  /** One getCurrentPosition in flight; the next starts when it settles, ≥ 1000 ms after this one started. */
  private poll(run: ActiveRun): void {
    if (this.active !== run) return;
    run.pollTimer = null;
    const startedAt = this.now();
    let settled = false;
    const settle = (): void => {
      if (this.active !== run) return;
      const wait = POLL_MIN_SPACING_MS - (this.now() - startedAt);
      // wait ≤ 0 is only possible after ≥ 1 s, so calling poll() directly cannot recurse without bound.
      if (wait <= 0) this.poll(run);
      else run.pollTimer = setTimeout(() => this.poll(run), wait);
    };
    this.geo.getCurrentPosition(
      (pos) => {
        if (settled) return;
        settled = true;
        this.onPosition(run, pos);
        settle();
      },
      (err) => {
        if (settled) return;
        settled = true;
        this.onError(run, err);
        settle();
      },
      run.options,
    );
  }

  private onPosition(run: ActiveRun, pos: GeolocationPosition): void {
    if (this.active !== run) return;
    const c = pos.coords;
    const fix: GpsFix = {
      arrivalPerfMs: this.now(),
      arrivalEpochMs: Date.now(),
      timestampMs: pos.timestamp,
      lat: c.latitude,
      lon: c.longitude,
      accuracyM: c.accuracy,
      speedMps: finiteOrNull(c.speed),
      altitudeM: finiteOrNull(c.altitude),
      hidden: run.isHidden(),
    };
    run.stats.add(fix);
    const speed = fix.speedMps === null ? 'null' : fix.speedMps.toFixed(2);
    this.log.push(
      'gps',
      `fix age=${Math.round(fix.arrivalEpochMs - fix.timestampMs)}ms acc=${Math.round(fix.accuracyM)}m speed=${speed}`,
    );
  }

  private onError(run: ActiveRun, err: GeolocationPositionError): void {
    if (this.active !== run) return;
    const code: 1 | 2 | 3 = err.code === 1 || err.code === 3 ? err.code : 2;
    run.stats.addError(code);
    this.log.push('gps', `error code=${code}`);
  }

  private finish(run: ActiveRun): void {
    if (this.active !== run) return;
    this.active = null;
    if (run.endTimer !== null) clearTimeout(run.endTimer);
    if (run.pollTimer !== null) clearTimeout(run.pollTimer);
    if (run.watchId !== null) this.geo.clearWatch(run.watchId);
    remove(CRASH_KEY);
    const elapsedS = Math.min(run.durationS, Math.max(0, (this.now() - run.t0) / 1000));
    const summary = run.stats.summary(Math.round(elapsedS * 10) / 10, run.crashedPreviously);
    const e = summary.errors;
    this.log.push(
      'gps',
      `run ${run.name} end fixes=${summary.fixes} errors=${e.permission}/${e.unavailable}/${e.timeout}`,
    );
    run.resolve(summary);
  }
}
```

### Task 6: Work kernel, probe↔worklet protocol, probe worklet and drift monitor

**Files:**
- Create: `src/probe/work.ts`
- Create: `src/probe/audio-protocol.ts`
- Create: `src/probe/drift.ts`
- Create: `src/probe/audio-worklet.ts`
- Test: `tests/unit/work.test.ts`
- Test: `tests/unit/audio-protocol.test.ts`
- Test: `tests/unit/drift.test.ts`
- Test: `tests/unit/audio-worklet.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes:
  - `src/contracts.ts`: `QUANTUM` (= 128).
  - `src/types/audioworklet.d.ts` (T1): the globals `AudioWorkletProcessor` (a class with `readonly port: MessagePort`), `registerProcessor(name, ctor)`, `currentTime: number` and `sampleRate: number`.
- Produces:
  - `src/probe/work.ts`:
    - `export const WORK_OPS_PER_SAMPLE = 400`.
    - `export class WorkKernel { constructor(); run(frames: number, k: number): number }`. `run()` performs k × 400 serially dependent multiply-adds per frame and returns a checksum in (0, 2). It allocates nothing after construction.
    - When `frames` is a multiple of 64, `run(frames, 2)` is identical to two `run(frames, 1)` calls.
    - `k = 0` or `frames = 0` does no work.
  - `src/probe/audio-protocol.ts`:
    - `export type ProbeToWorklet` and `export type WorkletToProbe`, exactly the contract unions.
    - Three additions owned by this task and consumed by T7: `export const PROBE_PROCESSOR = 'tesbox-probe'`, `export const PROBE_TONE_HZ = 55` and `export const PROBE_GAIN_DB = -18`.
  - `src/probe/drift.ts`:
    - `export interface DriftSample { contextTimeS: number; perfMs: number }`.
    - `export interface DriftWindow { ratio: number; overloaded: boolean }`, where `overloaded` means this window's ratio is below the threshold.
    - `export class DriftMonitor { constructor(windowMs = 1000, threshold = 0.98); push(s: DriftSample): DriftWindow | null; readonly overloaded: boolean; reset(): void }`.
    - `overloaded` becomes true after 2 or more consecutive slow windows and stays true until `reset()`.
    - Samples with `contextTimeS === 0` or non-finite values are ignored. A backwards jump of either clock, as with a new AudioContext, restarts the window.
  - `src/probe/audio-worklet.ts`: calls `registerProcessor('tesbox-probe', …)`.
    - Output: a stereo 2 ms raised-cosine pulse per period at `toneHz`, through a one-pole low-pass at 2 kHz, scaled by `gainDb`. The low-pass state is flushed below 1e-20.
    - Work: `WorkKernel.run(frames, k)` runs every quantum.
    - Reports: one `report` once at least `round(sampleRate × 0.25)` frames have accumulated, that is every 94 quanta at 48 kHz and 87 at 44.1 kHz. `quanta` is cumulative.
    - `ping` → `pong` (same `id`, the worklet's `currentTime`). T7 sends one `ping` per node it builds and logs the round trip.
    - `config` clamps `k` to an integer in [0, 64], `toneHz` to [1, 1000] and `gainDb` to [−120, 0]. NaN or non-finite fields are ignored, except that `gainDb` −∞ clamps to −120.
    - `process()` allocates nothing: the report object is reused and `postMessage` clones it.
  - Loading the worklet (information for T10): `import workletUrl from './audio-worklet.ts?worker&url'` in `src/probe/main.ts`. This was verified during planning: Vite 8.3.3 emits a self-contained IIFE asset, `assets/audio-worklet-<hash>.js`. `tsc` accepts the import through the `vite/client` types, with or without `.ts`. In headless Chromium, `audioWorklet.addModule(workletUrl)` loads it, and reports flow at the expected rate.

**Cross-task premises:**
- T1: `src/types/audioworklet.d.ts` declares `AudioWorkletProcessor` as a class whose constructor can be called with no arguments and that has `readonly port: MessagePort`.
  - `registerProcessor` accepts a class whose `process` has the standard `(inputs, outputs, parameters) => boolean` shape. A 2-parameter `process` is assignable to it.
  - `currentTime` and `sampleRate` are global numbers.
  - Two declaration shapes were checked during planning, and this worklet compiles against both: a plain `declare class` plus an `AudioWorkletProcessorImpl` constructor type, and a `declare abstract class` with `abstract process(…)`.
  - No `/// <reference>` is needed, because tsconfig `include: ["src", …]` picks up the .d.ts, and `@typescript-eslint/triple-slash-reference` would reject it.
- T1: the ESLint config is `@eslint/js` recommended plus `typescript-eslint` recommended, with no type-aware rules. `no-non-null-assertion` is not in that set, so the `!` on the masked typed-array read in `work.ts` passes lint. typescript-eslint's recommended set disables `no-undef` for .ts files, so the AudioWorkletGlobalScope globals pass lint.
- T7 (same author) imports `PROBE_PROCESSOR`, `PROBE_TONE_HZ`, `PROBE_GAIN_DB`, `ProbeToWorklet`, `WorkletToProbe`, `DriftMonitor` and `DriftSample` exactly as named above.
- T10 obtains the worklet URL via `?worker&url` (see Produces) and passes it to `new AudioProbe(log, workletUrl, anchorUrl)`. The worklet imports only environment-free modules (`./work`, `./audio-protocol`, `../contracts`), so Vite bundles it as one self-contained asset.
- T11's CSP `script-src 'self'` covers `audioWorklet.addModule()` of the same-origin asset.

```ts
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { WORK_OPS_PER_SAMPLE, WorkKernel } from '../../src/probe/work';

describe('WorkKernel', () => {
  it('costs 400 multiply-adds per frame per k', () => {
    expect(WORK_OPS_PER_SAMPLE).toBe(400);
  });

  it('performs 400 serial multiply-adds per frame (checksum equals a reference loop)', () => {
    let x = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < 400; i++) x = x * 0.5 + ((i & 63) + 1) / 64; // initial state s[i] = (i + 1) / 64
    expect(new WorkKernel().run(1, 1)).toBe(x);
  });

  it('returns a finite checksum in (0, 2) and is deterministic', () => {
    const a = new WorkKernel().run(128, 4);
    const b = new WorkKernel().run(128, 4);
    expect(Number.isFinite(a)).toBe(true);
    expect(a).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    expect(a).toBeLessThan(2);
    expect(b).toBe(a);
  });

  it('k multiplies the work: run(128, 2) ≡ two run(128, 1) calls (state carries across calls)', () => {
    const once = new WorkKernel().run(128, 2);
    const twice = new WorkKernel();
    twice.run(128, 1);
    expect(twice.run(128, 1)).toBe(once);
    expect(new WorkKernel().run(128, 1)).not.toBe(once);
  });

  it('does nothing for k = 0 or 0 frames', () => {
    const w = new WorkKernel();
    const first = w.run(128, 1);
    expect(w.run(128, 0)).toBe(first);
    expect(w.run(0, 16)).toBe(first);
    const fresh = new WorkKernel();
    fresh.run(128, 1);
    expect(w.run(128, 1)).toBe(fresh.run(128, 1)); // k = 0 calls left the state untouched
  });

  it('stays bounded and finite over a long run at k = 16 (no drift to NaN, ∞ or subnormals)', () => {
    const w = new WorkKernel();
    let c = 0;
    for (let q = 0; q < 50; q++) c = w.run(128, 16);
    expect(Number.isFinite(c)).toBe(true);
    expect(c).toBeGreaterThan(1e-3);
    expect(c).toBeLessThan(2);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement the work kernel**

Create `src/probe/work.ts`:

```ts
/** ≈ estimated Full-tier DSP cost per sample (spec §5.7): multiply-adds the benchmark burns per frame at k = 1. */
export const WORK_OPS_PER_SAMPLE = 400;

/** Power of two so `index & STATE_MASK` keeps every access in bounds. */
const STATE_LEN = 64;
const STATE_MASK = STATE_LEN - 1;

/**
 * Synthetic DSP load for the headroom benchmark. Each op is one multiply-add on a serial
 * dependency chain (like a cascade of IIR filters), so the JIT can neither vectorise nor drop it.
 * Numerically closed: with every state value in (0, 1], x = 0.5·x + s stays in (0, 2) and the
 * state written back (0.5·x) stays in (0, 1) — no overflow, NaN or subnormals, ever.
 * Allocation-free after construction (safe inside AudioWorkletProcessor.process).
 */
export class WorkKernel {
  private readonly state: Float64Array;
  private carry = 0;

  constructor() {
    this.state = new Float64Array(STATE_LEN);
    for (let i = 0; i < STATE_LEN; i++) this.state[i] = (i + 1) / STATE_LEN;
  }

  /** Performs k × WORK_OPS_PER_SAMPLE multiply-adds per frame; returns a checksum (finite, in (0, 2)). */
  run(frames: number, k: number): number {
    const s = this.state;
    const n = frames * k;
    let x = this.carry;
    for (let f = 0; f < n; f++) {
      for (let i = 0; i < WORK_OPS_PER_SAMPLE; i++) {
        x = x * 0.5 + s[i & STATE_MASK]!; // masked index is always in bounds
      }
      s[f & STATE_MASK] = x * 0.5;
    }
    this.carry = x;
    return x;
  }
}
```

```ts
/** Name passed to registerProcessor() in the worklet and to new AudioWorkletNode() on the main thread. */
export const PROBE_PROCESSOR = 'tesbox-probe';
/** Default probe tone (contract: 55 Hz pulse train at −18 dB). */
export const PROBE_TONE_HZ = 55;
export const PROBE_GAIN_DB = -18;

export type ProbeToWorklet =
  | { type: 'config'; k: number; toneHz: number; gainDb: number }
  | { type: 'ping'; id: number };

export type WorkletToProbe =
  | {
      type: 'report';
      quanta: number;
      perfInWorklet: boolean;
      busyMs: number;
      windowMs: number;
      sampleRate: number;
      currentTime: number;
    } // every ~250 ms of audio time
  | { type: 'pong'; id: number; currentTime: number };
```
