- [ ] **Step 9: Write the failing DriftMonitor test**

Create `tests/unit/drift.test.ts`. The boundary test uses binary-exact values (0.875) so that floating-point rounding cannot decide it.

```ts
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { DriftMonitor, type DriftWindow } from '../../src/probe/drift';

/** Feeds samples every 100 ms for `seconds`, the audio clock running at `rate` × wall time. */
function feed(m: DriftMonitor, from: { ctx: number; perf: number }, seconds: number, rate: number): DriftWindow[] {
  const out: DriftWindow[] = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < seconds * 10; i++) {
    from.perf += 100;
    from.ctx += (0.1 * rate);
    const w = m.push({ contextTimeS: from.ctx, perfMs: from.perf });
    if (w) out.push(w);
  }
  return out;
}

describe('DriftMonitor', () => {
  it('returns null until a full window has elapsed, then ratio ≈ 1 for a healthy clock', () => {
    const m = new DriftMonitor();
    expect(m.push({ contextTimeS: 1, perfMs: 5000 })).toBeNull(); // window start
    expect(m.push({ contextTimeS: 1.5, perfMs: 5500 })).toBeNull();
    const w = m.push({ contextTimeS: 2, perfMs: 6000 });
    expect(w?.ratio).toBeCloseTo(1, 12);
    expect(w?.overloaded).toBe(false);
    expect(m.overloaded).toBe(false);
  });

  it('ignores samples with contextTimeS === 0 or non-finite values', () => {
    const m = new DriftMonitor();
    expect(m.push({ contextTimeS: 0, perfMs: 0 })).toBeNull();
    expect(m.push({ contextTimeS: 0, perfMs: 2000 })).toBeNull();
    expect(m.push({ contextTimeS: Number.NaN, perfMs: 2500 })).toBeNull();
    expect(m.push({ contextTimeS: 0.5, perfMs: 3000 })).toBeNull(); // first valid sample starts the window
    expect(m.push({ contextTimeS: 1.5, perfMs: 4000 })?.ratio).toBeCloseTo(1, 12);
  });

  it('computes ratio = Δctx·1000/Δperf and flags a single slow window', () => {
    const m = new DriftMonitor();
    m.push({ contextTimeS: 10, perfMs: 0 });
    const w = m.push({ contextTimeS: 10.95, perfMs: 1000 });
    expect(w?.ratio).toBeCloseTo(0.95, 12);
    expect(w?.overloaded).toBe(true);
    expect(m.overloaded).toBe(false); // one window is not sustained overload
  });

  it('becomes overloaded after 2 consecutive slow windows and latches until reset()', () => {
    const m = new DriftMonitor();
    const clock = { ctx: 1, perf: 0 };
    m.push({ contextTimeS: clock.ctx, perfMs: clock.perf });
    const slow = feed(m, clock, 2, 0.9);
    expect(slow.map((w) => w.overloaded)).toEqual([true, true]);
    expect(m.overloaded).toBe(true);
    const healthy = feed(m, clock, 2, 1);
    expect(healthy.map((w) => w.overloaded)).toEqual([false, false]);
    expect(m.overloaded).toBe(true); // latched
    m.reset();
    expect(m.overloaded).toBe(false);
  });

  it('a healthy window between slow ones resets the consecutive count', () => {
    const m = new DriftMonitor();
    const clock = { ctx: 1, perf: 0 };
    m.push({ contextTimeS: clock.ctx, perfMs: clock.perf });
    feed(m, clock, 1, 0.9);
    feed(m, clock, 1, 1);
    feed(m, clock, 1, 0.9);
    expect(m.overloaded).toBe(false);
    feed(m, clock, 1, 0.9);
    expect(m.overloaded).toBe(true);
  });

  it('treats exactly the threshold as healthy and honours custom window/threshold', () => {
    // Binary-exact values so the boundary is tested without rounding noise.
    const m = new DriftMonitor(500, 0.875);
    m.push({ contextTimeS: 2, perfMs: 0 });
    expect(m.push({ contextTimeS: 2.4375, perfMs: 500 })?.overloaded).toBe(false); // ratio 0.875 is not < 0.875
    expect(m.push({ contextTimeS: 2.8125, perfMs: 1000 })?.overloaded).toBe(true); // ratio 0.75
  });

  it('restarts the window when the context clock goes backwards (new AudioContext)', () => {
    const m = new DriftMonitor();
    m.push({ contextTimeS: 50, perfMs: 0 });
    expect(m.push({ contextTimeS: 0.2, perfMs: 600 })).toBeNull(); // new context: fresh start
    const w = m.push({ contextTimeS: 1.2, perfMs: 1600 });
    expect(w?.ratio).toBeCloseTo(1, 12);
  });

  it('a context-clock jump (new AudioContext) clears the consecutive slow-window count', () => {
    const m = new DriftMonitor();
    const clock = { ctx: 1, perf: 0 };
    m.push({ contextTimeS: clock.ctx, perfMs: clock.perf });
    feed(m, clock, 1, 0.9); // one slow window on the old context
    clock.ctx = 0.1; // new context: its clock restarts near 0
    m.push({ contextTimeS: clock.ctx, perfMs: clock.perf });
    feed(m, clock, 1, 0.9); // one slow window on the new context
    expect(m.overloaded).toBe(false);
  });

  it('after reset() the next sample starts a new window', () => {
    const m = new DriftMonitor();
    m.push({ contextTimeS: 1, perfMs: 0 });
    m.reset();
    expect(m.push({ contextTimeS: 5, perfMs: 2000 })).toBeNull();
    expect(m.push({ contextTimeS: 6, perfMs: 3000 })?.ratio).toBeCloseTo(1, 12);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 11: Implement DriftMonitor**

Create `src/probe/drift.ts`:

```ts
export interface DriftSample {
  contextTimeS: number;
  perfMs: number;
}

export interface DriftWindow {
  /** Δ(contextTimeS)·1000 / Δ(perfMs) over the window; ≈ 1 when the audio clock keeps up. */
  ratio: number;
  /** This window alone: ratio < threshold. */
  overloaded: boolean;
}

/**
 * Detects audio-thread overload from getOutputTimestamp() pairs: an overloaded context renders
 * fewer frames than wall time requires, so its clock advances slower than performance.now().
 */
export class DriftMonitor {
  private hasStart = false;
  private startCtxS = 0;
  private startPerfMs = 0;
  private consecutive = 0;
  private latched = false;

  constructor(
    private readonly windowMs = 1000,
    private readonly threshold = 0.98,
  ) {}

  /** True after ≥ 2 consecutive windows with ratio < threshold; stays true until reset(). */
  get overloaded(): boolean {
    return this.latched;
  }

  /** Feed getOutputTimestamp() pairs (~every 100 ms). Returns a window result each time ≥ windowMs elapsed, else null. */
  push(s: DriftSample): DriftWindow | null {
    if (s.contextTimeS === 0 || !Number.isFinite(s.contextTimeS) || !Number.isFinite(s.perfMs)) return null;
    if (!this.hasStart || s.contextTimeS < this.startCtxS || s.perfMs < this.startPerfMs) {
      // First sample, or a clock went backwards (new AudioContext): start a fresh window.
      if (this.hasStart) this.consecutive = 0;
      this.hasStart = true;
      this.startCtxS = s.contextTimeS;
      this.startPerfMs = s.perfMs;
      return null;
    }
    const elapsedMs = s.perfMs - this.startPerfMs;
    if (elapsedMs < this.windowMs) return null;
    const ratio = ((s.contextTimeS - this.startCtxS) * 1000) / elapsedMs;
    const overloaded = ratio < this.threshold;
    this.consecutive = overloaded ? this.consecutive + 1 : 0;
    if (this.consecutive >= 2) this.latched = true;
    this.startCtxS = s.contextTimeS;
    this.startPerfMs = s.perfMs;
    return { ratio, overloaded };
  }

  reset(): void {
    this.hasStart = false;
    this.consecutive = 0;
    this.latched = false;
  }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 14: Write the failing worklet test**

Create `tests/unit/audio-worklet.test.ts`. It stubs the AudioWorkletGlobalScope globals, then imports the module, which captures the processor class through the stubbed `registerProcessor`, and drives `process()` directly.

```ts
import { describe, it, expect, vi, beforeAll, afterAll } from 'vitest';
import type { ProbeToWorklet, WorkletToProbe } from '../../src/probe/audio-protocol';

const SR = 48000;

class FakePort {
  onmessage: ((e: { data: unknown }) => void) | null = null;
  readonly sent: WorkletToProbe[] = [];
  postMessage(m: unknown): void {
    this.sent.push(structuredClone(m) as WorkletToProbe); // real ports clone too
  }
  send(msg: ProbeToWorklet): void {
    this.onmessage?.({ data: msg });
  }
  reports(): Extract<WorkletToProbe, { type: 'report' }>[] {
    return this.sent.filter((m): m is Extract<WorkletToProbe, { type: 'report' }> => m.type === 'report');
  }
}

class FakeAudioWorkletProcessor {
  readonly port = new FakePort();
}

interface Proc {
  readonly port: FakePort;
  process(inputs: Float32Array[][], outputs: Float32Array[][]): boolean;
}

const registered: { name: string; ctor: (new () => Proc) | null } = { name: '', ctor: null };
let audioTime = 0;

beforeAll(async () => {
  // AudioWorkletGlobalScope stand-ins must exist before the module evaluates.
  vi.stubGlobal('AudioWorkletProcessor', FakeAudioWorkletProcessor);
  vi.stubGlobal('registerProcessor', (name: string, ctor: new () => Proc) => {
    registered.name = name;
    registered.ctor = ctor;
  });
  vi.stubGlobal('sampleRate', SR);
  Object.defineProperty(globalThis, 'currentTime', { get: () => audioTime, configurable: true });
  await import('../../src/probe/audio-worklet');
});

afterAll(() => {
  vi.unstubAllGlobals();
  Reflect.deleteProperty(globalThis, 'currentTime');
});

function make(): Proc {
  if (registered.ctor === null) throw new Error('processor was not registered');
  return new registered.ctor();
}

/** Runs `quanta` process() calls of `frames` each; returns the concatenated channels. */
function render(p: Proc, quanta: number, frames = 128): { left: Float32Array; right: Float32Array } {
  const left = new Float32Array(quanta * frames);
  const right = new Float32Array(quanta * frames);
  for (let q = 0; q < quanta; q++) {
    const l = new Float32Array(frames);
    const r = new Float32Array(frames);
    expect(p.process([], [[l, r]])).toBe(true);
    left.set(l, q * frames);
    right.set(r, q * frames);
    audioTime += frames / SR;
  }
  return { left, right };
}

function peak(x: Float32Array): number {
  let m = 0;
  for (const v of x) m = Math.max(m, Math.abs(v));
  return m;
}

/** Counts upward crossings of half the peak (one per pulse). */
function onsets(x: Float32Array): number {
  const thr = peak(x) / 2;
  let n = 0;
  for (let i = 1; i < x.length; i++) if ((x[i - 1] ?? 0) < thr && (x[i] ?? 0) >= thr) n++;
  return n;
}

const ONE_SECOND = SR / 128; // 375 quanta

describe('tesbox-probe worklet', () => {
  it("registers as 'tesbox-probe'", () => {
    expect(registered.name).toBe('tesbox-probe');
  });

  it('renders an identical stereo 55 Hz pulse train at −18 dB by default', () => {
    const { left, right } = render(make(), ONE_SECOND);
    expect(right).toEqual(left);
    expect(onsets(left)).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(54);
    expect(onsets(left)).toBeLessThanOrEqual(56);
    const p = peak(left);
    expect(p).toBeGreaterThan(0.1);
    expect(p).toBeLessThanOrEqual(10 ** (-18 / 20) + 1e-6);
    expect(left.every(Number.isFinite)).toBe(true);
  });

  it('each pulse is ~2 ms long: the signal has decayed to silence well before the next period', () => {
    const { left } = render(make(), ONE_SECOND);
    const period = SR / 55;
    for (let i = 0; i < left.length; i++) {
      const tInPeriodMs = ((i % period) / SR) * 1000;
      if (tInPeriodMs > 4 && tInPeriodMs < 17) expect(Math.abs(left[i] ?? 1)).toBeLessThan(1e-6);
    }
    // Lower bound too: a raised cosine is above half its peak for half its length (2 ms → 1 ms = 48 samples).
    const half = peak(left) / 2;
    const aboveHalf = left.filter((v) => v >= half).length / onsets(left);
    expect(aboveHalf).toBeGreaterThan(40); // measured 48.5 at 2 ms; 24.8 at 1 ms, 72.4 at 3 ms
    expect(aboveHalf).toBeLessThan(56);
  });

  it('flushes the low-pass state to exactly 0 between widely spaced pulses (no subnormals)', () => {
    const p = make();
    p.port.send({ type: 'config', k: 1, toneHz: 1, gainDb: -18 });
    const { left } = render(p, ONE_SECOND);
    expect(left[SR / 2]).toBe(0);
    // Without the flush the decaying tail stays non-zero (float32 subnormals) until sample ~470.
    expect(left.subarray(300, SR / 2).every((v) => v === 0)).toBe(true);
  });

  it('applies config: toneHz and gainDb', () => {
    const p = make();
    p.port.send({ type: 'config', k: 1, toneHz: 110, gainDb: -6 });
    const { left } = render(p, ONE_SECOND);
    expect(onsets(left)).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(109);
    expect(onsets(left)).toBeLessThanOrEqual(111);
    const ref = render(make(), ONE_SECOND).left;
    expect(peak(left) / peak(ref)).toBeCloseTo(10 ** (12 / 20), 1);
  });

  it('clamps config: k to at most 64 and gainDb to at most 0 dB', () => {
    // Bounded on purpose: an unclamped k = 1e9 would hang the worker in one synchronous loop
    // (Vitest's timeout cannot fire), whereas an unclamped k = 256 costs ~4× and still finishes.
    const control = make();
    control.port.send({ type: 'config', k: 64, toneHz: 55, gainDb: -18 });
    render(control, 20); // JIT warm-up
    const t0 = performance.now();
    render(control, 20);
    const t64 = performance.now() - t0;
    const p = make();
    p.port.send({ type: 'config', k: 256, toneHz: 55, gainDb: 60 });
    const t1 = performance.now();
    const { left } = render(p, 20);
    const t256 = performance.now() - t1;
    expect(t256 / t64).toBeLessThan(2); // clamped: ≈ 1 (measured 0.98–1.00); unclamped: ≈ 3.7
    expect(peak(left)).toBeGreaterThan(0.5);
    expect(peak(left)).toBeLessThanOrEqual(1 + 1e-6);
  });

  it('clamps toneHz to [1, 1000] Hz', () => {
    const hi = make();
    hi.port.send({ type: 'config', k: 1, toneHz: 1e6, gainDb: -18 });
    const n = onsets(render(hi, ONE_SECOND).left);
    expect(n).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(990);
    expect(n).toBeLessThanOrEqual(1001);
  });

  it('ignores non-finite config values', () => {
    const p = make();
    p.port.send({ type: 'config', k: Number.NaN, toneHz: Number.NaN, gainDb: Number.NaN });
    expect(render(p, 50).left).toEqual(render(make(), 50).left);
  });

  it('ignores +∞ gainDb and clamps −∞ gainDb to −120 dB', () => {
    const p = make();
    p.port.send({ type: 'config', k: 1, toneHz: 55, gainDb: Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY });
    expect(render(p, 50).left).toEqual(render(make(), 50).left);
    const q = make();
    q.port.send({ type: 'config', k: 1, toneHz: 55, gainDb: Number.NEGATIVE_INFINITY });
    expect(peak(render(q, 50).left)).toBeLessThan(1e-6);
  });

  it('keeps phase and filter state across calls (one 256-frame call ≡ two 128-frame calls)', () => {
    const one = render(make(), 3, 256).left;
    const two = render(make(), 6, 128).left;
    expect(two).toEqual(one);
  });

  it('posts a report every ~250 ms of audio time with cumulative quanta', () => {
    const p = make();
    render(p, 376);
    const reports = p.port.reports();
    expect(reports.map((r) => r.quanta)).toEqual([94, 188, 282, 376]);
    for (const r of reports) {
      expect(r.windowMs).toBeCloseTo((94 * 128 * 1000) / SR, 9);
      expect(r.sampleRate).toBe(SR);
      expect(r.perfInWorklet).toBe(true); // Node exposes performance.now
      expect(r.busyMs).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(0);
    }
    expect(reports[1]!.currentTime).toBeGreaterThan(reports[0]!.currentTime);
  });

  it('busy time grows with k (the work kernel runs k × per quantum)', () => {
    const idle = make();
    idle.port.send({ type: 'config', k: 0, toneHz: 55, gainDb: -18 });
    render(idle, 94);
    const heavy = make();
    heavy.port.send({ type: 'config', k: 16, toneHz: 55, gainDb: -18 });
    render(heavy, 94);
    const idleBusy = idle.port.reports()[0]!.busyMs;
    const heavyBusy = heavy.port.reports()[0]!.busyMs;
    expect(heavyBusy).toBeGreaterThan(idleBusy * 5);
  });

  it('answers ping with pong carrying the id and currentTime', () => {
    const p = make();
    audioTime = 12.5;
    p.port.send({ type: 'ping', id: 7 });
    expect(p.port.sent).toContainEqual({ type: 'pong', id: 7, currentTime: 12.5 });
  });

  it('survives a missing output bus and always returns true', () => {
    const p = make();
    expect(p.process([], [])).toBe(true);
    expect(p.process([], [[]])).toBe(true);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 16: Implement the probe worklet**

Create `src/probe/audio-worklet.ts`. `process()` must obey the hot-path rules: no `new`, no object or array literals, closures, template literals or `try`.

```ts
import { QUANTUM } from '../contracts';
import { PROBE_GAIN_DB, PROBE_PROCESSOR, PROBE_TONE_HZ, type ProbeToWorklet, type WorkletToProbe } from './audio-protocol';
import { WorkKernel } from './work';

const PULSE_S = 0.002; // raised-cosine pulse length
const LOWPASS_HZ = 2000; // one-pole corner
const REPORT_INTERVAL_S = 0.25; // audio time between reports
const MAX_K = 64;
const MIN_TONE_HZ = 1;
const MAX_TONE_HZ = 1000;
const MIN_GAIN_DB = -120;
const DENORMAL_FLUSH = 1e-20;

const PERF_IN_WORKLET = typeof performance !== 'undefined' && typeof performance.now === 'function';
const clockMs: () => number = PERF_IN_WORKLET ? () => performance.now() : () => Date.now();

type ReportMsg = Extract<WorkletToProbe, { type: 'report' }>;

function clamp(v: number, lo: number, hi: number): number {
  return v < lo ? lo : v > hi ? hi : v;
}

class TesboxProbeProcessor extends AudioWorkletProcessor {
  private readonly kernel = new WorkKernel();
  private readonly lpCoeff = 1 - Math.exp((-2 * Math.PI * LOWPASS_HZ) / sampleRate);
  private readonly reportFrames = Math.max(1, Math.round(sampleRate * REPORT_INTERVAL_S));
  /** Reused for every report: process() must not allocate. postMessage clones it. */
  private readonly report: ReportMsg = {
    type: 'report',
    quanta: 0,
    perfInWorklet: PERF_IN_WORKLET,
    busyMs: 0,
    windowMs: 0,
    sampleRate,
    currentTime: 0,
  };
  private k = 1;
  private toneHz = PROBE_TONE_HZ;
  private gain = 10 ** (PROBE_GAIN_DB / 20);
  private phase = 0; // position in the tone period, [0, 1)
  private lp = 0; // one-pole state
  private quanta = 0;
  private framesInWindow = 0;
  private busyMs = 0;
  private checksum = 0; // keeps the kernel result observable

  constructor() {
    super();
    this.port.onmessage = (e: MessageEvent<ProbeToWorklet>) => this.onMessage(e.data);
  }

  private onMessage(msg: ProbeToWorklet): void {
    if (typeof msg !== 'object' || msg === null) return;
    if (msg.type === 'config') {
      if (Number.isFinite(msg.k)) this.k = clamp(Math.round(msg.k), 0, MAX_K);
      if (Number.isFinite(msg.toneHz)) this.toneHz = clamp(msg.toneHz, MIN_TONE_HZ, MAX_TONE_HZ);
      // +∞ is ignored like NaN; −∞ means "silent" and clamps to −120 dB.
      if (Number.isFinite(msg.gainDb) || msg.gainDb === -Infinity) this.gain = 10 ** (clamp(msg.gainDb, MIN_GAIN_DB, 0) / 20);
    } else if (msg.type === 'ping') {
      const pong: WorkletToProbe = { type: 'pong', id: msg.id, currentTime };
      this.port.postMessage(pong);
    }
  }

  process(_inputs: Float32Array[][], outputs: Float32Array[][]): boolean {
    const t0 = clockMs();
    const out = outputs[0];
    const left = out !== undefined ? out[0] : undefined;
    const right = out !== undefined ? out[1] : undefined;
    const frames = left !== undefined ? left.length : QUANTUM;

    if (left !== undefined) {
      const inc = this.toneHz / sampleRate;
      const duty = Math.min(1, PULSE_S * this.toneHz); // pulse length as a fraction of the period
      const w = (2 * Math.PI) / duty;
      const a = this.lpCoeff;
      const g = this.gain;
      let phase = this.phase;
      let y = this.lp;
      for (let i = 0; i < frames; i++) {
        const x = phase < duty ? 0.5 - 0.5 * Math.cos(phase * w) : 0;
        y += a * (x - y);
        if (y < DENORMAL_FLUSH && y > -DENORMAL_FLUSH) y = 0;
        const v = y * g;
        left[i] = v;
        if (right !== undefined) right[i] = v;
        phase += inc;
        if (phase >= 1) phase -= 1;
      }
      this.phase = phase;
      this.lp = y;
    }

    this.checksum = this.kernel.run(frames, this.k);
    this.busyMs += clockMs() - t0;
    this.quanta++;
    this.framesInWindow += frames;
    if (this.framesInWindow >= this.reportFrames) {
      const r = this.report;
      r.quanta = this.quanta;
      r.busyMs = this.busyMs;
      r.windowMs = (this.framesInWindow / sampleRate) * 1000;
      r.currentTime = currentTime;
      this.port.postMessage(r);
      this.busyMs = 0;
      this.framesInWindow = 0;
    }
    return true;
  }
}

registerProcessor(PROBE_PROCESSOR, TesboxProbeProcessor);
```
