---

### Task 7: AudioProbe — context lifecycle, three output routes, Media Session, benchmark, recovery, hidden clock

**Files:**
- Create: `src/probe/audio.ts`
- Test: `tests/unit/audio.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes:
  - `src/contracts.ts`: `type OutputRoute = 'direct' | 'media-element' | 'direct-anchor'`.
  - From T6, `src/probe/audio-protocol.ts`: `PROBE_PROCESSOR`, `PROBE_TONE_HZ`, `PROBE_GAIN_DB`, `type ProbeToWorklet` and `type WorkletToProbe`.
  - From T6, `src/probe/drift.ts`: `new DriftMonitor(windowMs: number, threshold: number)` with `push(s: DriftSample): DriftWindow | null`, `readonly overloaded` and `type DriftSample`.
  - From T4: `EventLog.push(kind, detail)` with kinds `'audio'`, `'mediasession'`, `'note'` and `'error'`; `errText(e: unknown): string` and `withTimeout<T>(p: Promise<T>, ms: number, label: string): Promise<T>` (value imports from `./log`; `withTimeout` rejects with `<label> timed out after <ms> ms`).
  - DOM: `AudioContext`, `ctx.audioWorklet.addModule`, `AudioWorkletNode`, `ctx.createMediaStreamDestination()`, `ctx.getOutputTimestamp()`, `document.createElement('audio')`, `navigator.mediaSession`, `MediaMetadata` and `document.visibilityState`.
- Produces: in `src/probe/audio.ts`, `export interface BenchResult`, `export interface AudioProbeState` and `export class AudioProbe`, exactly as the contract specifies. The behaviours below are the ones other tasks rely on.
  - **Serialisation:** `start`, `setRoute`, `resume`, `recreate` and `stop` run one at a time in call order, and a failed call does not block later ones.
    - When no operation is pending, the call starts **synchronously**, so `new AudioContext` and `ctx.resume()` run inside the caller's click handler (spec §6.3); route `<audio>.play()` runs after `addModule` (or after the 500 ms resume check) resolves and relies on the document's sticky user activation. A call made while another is pending is queued and runs after it, in a later microtask; Chromium's sticky user activation still covers context creation and `play()` there.
    - As a result, there is never more than one live AudioContext, one connected route or one playing route `<audio>` element.
    - A route switch pauses the old element, clears its `src` and `srcObject`, and calls `load()` on it.
  - **`start(route, hint)`:**
    - On the first call, it builds the context, module, node, route and Media Session.
    - Called again with the same hint, it only switches the route (a no-op if the route is unchanged).
    - Called with a different hint, it closes the old context and builds a new one with that hint. This is the latencyHint A/B, and it is not counted as a recovery.
    - It rejects and leaves `snapshot.state === 'none'` if `addModule` or `new AudioWorkletNode` throws, logging `error` `worklet setup failed: …`. An `addModule` that has not settled after 10 s counts as a failure (`addModule timed out after 10000 ms`), and every awaited `ctx.close()` (here and in `stop()`) is abandoned after 2 s, so a hung browser promise cannot stall the operation queue.
    - The `media-element` route rejects with `media-element route unsupported` when `createMediaStreamDestination` is missing. When that happens while building, the new context is closed as well, so `snapshot.state === 'none'` and no Media Session is registered.
    - After building a node it sends one `ping` to the worklet and logs the `pong` round trip.
  - **`setRoute`:** before `start`, it logs a `note` and resolves; the route then comes from `start`'s own parameter.
  - **`resume()`:**
    - Resolves `true` only if `ctx.resume()` fulfils within 500 ms (checked with T4's `withTimeout`, like the initial resume in `build()`) and the state is then `'running'`. It counts `resumeOk` or `resumeFail`.
    - On success it restarts a paused route element.
    - It never calls `suspend()`.
  - **`recreate()`:**
    - Without a context (before any `start`, after a failed `start`, or after `stop()`) it does nothing and logs `recreate: no context (not started or stopped)`, even if `setRoute()` recorded a route.
    - Otherwise it increments `recreate` and calls `close()` on the old context without awaiting it, so that (with no other operation pending) the new context is created synchronously inside the tap. It then rebuilds with the same hint and the most recently requested route.
  - **`benchmark(ks = [1,2,4,8,16], secondsEach = 3)`:**
    - It rejects with `audio is not running`, with `benchmark already running`, or with `audio context changed during benchmark` (after a recreate or stop mid-run).
    - For each k it posts config k, waits 250 ms, then samples `getOutputTimestamp()` `{contextTime, performanceTime}` pairs every 100 ms for `secondsEach` (both values describe the same render callback, so there is no callback-period jitter; `performance.now()` stands in only when `performanceTime` is missing or 0, and `currentTime` + `performance.now()` when `getOutputTimestamp` is missing).
    - `ratio` is the whole-span ratio, to 3 decimals.
    - `overloaded` is `DriftMonitor.overloaded` when 2 or more 1 s windows completed; otherwise it is `ratio < 0.98`.
    - `busyFrac` is Σ`busyMs` / Σ`windowMs` over the reports received during the span (3 decimals), or null if there were none.
    - `headroomK` is the largest k such that it and every smaller benchmarked k are not overloaded, or 0 if the smallest is overloaded.
    - It restores the previous k afterwards, also on a node rebuilt mid-run (a rebuild posts the current, i.e. benchmark, k to the new node).
    - Duration is about `ks.length × (secondsEach + 0.25)` s; the quick bench `[1,2] × 1` takes about 2.5 s.
  - **`snapshot`:** a fresh object on every read. `quanta` and `perfInWorklet` come from the latest worklet report of the current node: 0 and null before the first report, and reset when the context is rebuilt.
  - **`lastWorkletReport`** (addition to the contract API, read by T10's live readout): `get lastWorkletReport(): Readonly<Extract<WorkletToProbe, { type: 'report' }>> | null` — a copy of the latest report of the current node (`quanta`, `perfInWorklet`, `busyMs`, `windowMs`, `sampleRate`, `currentTime`), null before the first one and after a rebuild or `stop()`.
  - **`setTone(toneHz: number): void`** (addition to the contract API, called by T10's **Pitch step** button): stores the tone and posts `{ type: 'config', k, toneHz, gainDb: PROBE_GAIN_DB }` to the current node (no-op without one). Every config message carries the stored tone (default `PROBE_TONE_HZ` = 55), so `setK`, the benchmark and a node rebuilt by `recreate()`/a hint switch keep it; the worklet clamps it to [1, 1000] Hz. It gives the tester an audible tap stimulus for judging each route's tap → sound delay (spec §6.4), which the context-level `outputLatency` cannot show.
  - **`hiddenClockRatio`:** (Δ`ctx.currentTime` × 1000) / Δ`performance.now()` across the last hidden→visible period of the same context, to 3 decimals; null if no such period has happened.
  - **Log lines:**
    - Kind `'audio'`: the exact `ctx.state` string on every `statechange` of the current context, plus `context <state> sr=<n> hint=<hint>`, `route <route>`, `<route> element playing`, `<route> element paused` (external pauses only), `resume ok|failed state=<s>`, `recreate`, `recreate: no context (not started or stopped)`, `pong rtt=<ms>ms ctxTime=<s.sss>`, `bench k=<k> ratio=<r> overloaded=<b> busy=<f|n/a>`, `bench headroomK=<k>`, `hidden <s>s clock ratio=<r> state=<s>` (pushed right after `hiddenClockRatio` is updated, and only then; T10 attributes the ratio on this line) and `stopped`.
    - Kind `'mediasession'`: the detail is exactly the action name.

**Cross-task premises:**
- T4: `EventLog` follows the contract and `push()` invokes subscribers synchronously; `log.ts` also exports `errText` and `withTimeout(p, ms, label)` (rejects with `<label> timed out after <ms> ms`), which this task imports instead of defining its own timeout race (`settlesWithin`, the 500 ms resume check, is a thin `withTimeout(…).then(() => true, () => false)` wrapper). `LogKind` includes `'audio'`, `'mediasession'`, `'note'` and `'error'`. `count(kind)` counts every push of that kind ever made, not just the retained ones.
- T6 (same author): the exports listed under Consumes exist with exactly those names. `DriftMonitor.overloaded` latches until `reset()`.
- T8 (same author) counts Media Session actions by subscribing to the log (kind `'mediasession'`, detail = action name). AudioProbe is the only code that calls `navigator.mediaSession.setActionHandler`, because each action has exactly one handler slot.
- T9 ships `public/probe/anchor.wav`, served at `/probe/anchor.wav`.
- T10:
  - Constructs `new AudioProbe(log, workletUrl, '/probe/anchor.wav')` once per page (the first **Start audio** tap) and reuses it across Stop/Start, so recoveries survive; `workletUrl` is taken from `import workletUrl from './audio-worklet.ts?worker&url'`.
  - Calls `start()` synchronously from the **Start audio** click listener, and `stop()` when a `start()` rejects.
  - Reads `snapshot`, `hiddenClockRatio` and `lastWorkletReport` every 250 ms, and subscribes to the log for the `hidden …` line.
  - Calls `benchmark([1, 2], 1)` when `?quickbench=1` is set.
  - Calls `resume()` and `recreate()` from button clicks, and `setTone(55 | 110)` from the **Pitch step** button (enabled only while audio runs).
- T4 `summary.ts` imports the types `AudioProbeState` and `BenchResult` from this file. Neither contains a forbidden coordinate key.
- T12 e2e expects `snapshot.quanta > 0` within 3 s of the Start click (the contract's window; it is not measured from `running`), working route switches, and `benchmark([1,2],1)` resolving with 2 results. This was verified during planning in headless Chromium (Playwright 1.63, Vite 8.3.3 build of the worklet):
  - 348 quanta after 1.2 s at 44.1 kHz.
  - All three routes played.
  - Ratios were 0.995–1.0 for k ≤ 16 and 0.339 at k = 64, which was flagged as overloaded.
  - `perfInWorklet` was `false`, so Chromium's AudioWorkletGlobalScope has no `performance` and busy time is measured with `Date.now()`.
  - recreate, the hint switch, resume and stop all worked.
- The TypeScript 6.0 DOM lib's `AudioContextState` already includes `'interrupted'` (checked), so no cast is needed.

Notes:
- Most fakes are plain classes installed with `vi.stubGlobal`; `vi.unstubAllGlobals()` restores Node's own `navigator` afterwards.
- The two "pending start" tests gate `addModule` behind a promise. During planning, replacing `serial()`'s body with `return op();` made exactly those two tests fail, so they are what guards the serialisation required by RF3. They still guard the queued path of the idle-synchronous `serial()` below. The test "creates the context synchronously when no operation is pending" guards the idle path: replacing `idle ? op() : this.queue.then(op)` with `this.queue.then(op)` makes exactly that test fail (checked during planning). The double-start and rapid-`setRoute` tests check the RF3 outcome directly.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the test harness and the `start` tests**

Create `tests/unit/audio.test.ts` with this content (fakes, harness and the first `describe`):

```ts
import { describe, it, expect, vi, beforeEach, afterEach } from 'vitest';
import { AudioProbe } from '../../src/probe/audio';
import { EventLog } from '../../src/probe/log';
import type { ProbeToWorklet, WorkletToProbe } from '../../src/probe/audio-protocol';

// ───────────── fakes (minimal Web Audio / DOM surface AudioProbe touches) ─────────────

class FakeNode {
  connections: unknown[] = [];
  connect(dest: unknown): unknown {
    this.connections.push(dest);
    return dest;
  }
  disconnect(): void {
    this.connections = [];
  }
}

class FakeStreamDestination extends FakeNode {
  readonly stream = { id: 'probe-stream' };
}

type ResumeMode = 'ok' | 'hang' | 'reject';

class FakeAudioContext {
  static instances: FakeAudioContext[] = [];
  /** When set, addModule() waits for it (simulates a slow module fetch). */
  static moduleGate: Promise<void> | null = null;
  state: AudioContextState = 'running';
  readonly sampleRate = 48000;
  readonly baseLatency = 0.01;
  readonly outputLatency = 0.04;
  currentTime = 0;
  readonly destination = new FakeNode();
  onstatechange: (() => void) | null = null;
  readonly modules: string[] = [];
  readonly streamDestinations: FakeStreamDestination[] = [];
  resumeMode: ResumeMode = 'ok';
  addModuleFails = false;
  closeCalls = 0;
  readonly audioWorklet = {
    addModule: (url: string): Promise<void> => {
      this.modules.push(url);
      if (this.addModuleFails) return Promise.reject(new Error('404'));
      return FakeAudioContext.moduleGate ?? Promise.resolve();
    },
  };
  constructor(readonly options: AudioContextOptions) {
    FakeAudioContext.instances.push(this);
  }
  createMediaStreamDestination(): FakeStreamDestination {
    const d = new FakeStreamDestination();
    this.streamDestinations.push(d);
    return d;
  }
  getOutputTimestamp(): AudioTimestamp {
    return { contextTime: this.currentTime, performanceTime: performance.now() };
  }
  resume(): Promise<void> {
    if (this.resumeMode === 'hang') return new Promise(() => undefined);
    if (this.resumeMode === 'reject') return Promise.reject(new Error('InvalidStateError'));
    this.setState('running');
    return Promise.resolve();
  }
  close(): Promise<void> {
    this.closeCalls++;
    this.setState('closed');
    return Promise.resolve();
  }
  setState(s: AudioContextState): void {
    this.state = s;
    this.onstatechange?.();
  }
}

class FakePort {
  onmessage: ((e: { data: unknown }) => void) | null = null;
  readonly sent: ProbeToWorklet[] = [];
  postMessage(m: ProbeToWorklet): void {
    this.sent.push(m);
  }
  emit(m: WorkletToProbe): void {
    this.onmessage?.({ data: m });
  }
}

class FakeWorkletNode extends FakeNode {
  static instances: FakeWorkletNode[] = [];
  readonly port = new FakePort();
  onprocessorerror: (() => void) | null = null;
  constructor(
    readonly ctx: FakeAudioContext,
    readonly name: string,
    readonly options: AudioWorkletNodeOptions,
  ) {
    super();
    FakeWorkletNode.instances.push(this);
  }
  lastK(): number | undefined {
    return this.port.sent.filter((m) => m.type === 'config').at(-1)?.k;
  }
}

class FakeAudioElement {
  static instances: FakeAudioElement[] = [];
  src = '';
  srcObject: unknown = null;
  loop = false;
  preload = '';
  paused = true;
  onpause: (() => void) | null = null;
  playCalls = 0;
  loadCalls = 0;
  constructor() {
    FakeAudioElement.instances.push(this);
  }
  play(): Promise<void> {
    this.playCalls++;
    this.paused = false;
    return Promise.resolve();
  }
  pause(): void {
    this.paused = true;
    this.onpause?.();
  }
  removeAttribute(name: string): void {
    if (name === 'src') this.src = '';
  }
  load(): void {
    this.loadCalls++;
  }
}

class FakeMediaSession {
  metadata: unknown = null;
  playbackState: MediaSessionPlaybackState = 'none';
  readonly handlers = new Map<string, (() => void) | null>();
  setActionHandler(action: string, handler: (() => void) | null): void {
    this.handlers.set(action, handler);
  }
}

class FakeMediaMetadata {
  constructor(readonly init: MediaMetadataInit) {}
}

class FakeDocument {
  visibilityState: DocumentVisibilityState = 'visible';
  readonly listeners = new Map<string, Set<() => void>>();
  addEventListener(type: string, fn: () => void): void {
    if (!this.listeners.has(type)) this.listeners.set(type, new Set());
    this.listeners.get(type)?.add(fn);
  }
  removeEventListener(type: string, fn: () => void): void {
    this.listeners.get(type)?.delete(fn);
  }
  createElement(tag: string): FakeAudioElement {
    if (tag !== 'audio') throw new Error(`unexpected element ${tag}`);
    return new FakeAudioElement();
  }
  setVisibility(v: DocumentVisibilityState): void {
    this.visibilityState = v;
    for (const fn of this.listeners.get('visibilitychange') ?? []) fn();
  }
}

// ───────────── harness ─────────────

const WORKLET_URL = '/assets/audio-worklet.js';
const ANCHOR_URL = '/probe/anchor.wav';
let log: EventLog;
let probe: AudioProbe;
let session: FakeMediaSession;
let doc: FakeDocument;

const ctxs = (): FakeAudioContext[] => FakeAudioContext.instances;
const lastCtx = (): FakeAudioContext => {
  const c = ctxs().at(-1);
  if (c === undefined) throw new Error('no AudioContext');
  return c;
};
const lastNode = (): FakeWorkletNode => {
  const n = FakeWorkletNode.instances.at(-1);
  if (n === undefined) throw new Error('no AudioWorkletNode');
  return n;
};
const playing = (): FakeAudioElement[] => FakeAudioElement.instances.filter((e) => !e.paused);
const openCtxs = (): FakeAudioContext[] => ctxs().filter((c) => c.state !== 'closed');
const details = (kind: string): string[] => log.entries().filter((e) => e.kind === kind).map((e) => e.detail);

beforeEach(() => {
  vi.useFakeTimers();
  FakeAudioContext.instances = [];
  FakeAudioContext.moduleGate = null;
  FakeWorkletNode.instances = [];
  FakeAudioElement.instances = [];
  session = new FakeMediaSession();
  doc = new FakeDocument();
  vi.stubGlobal('AudioContext', FakeAudioContext);
  vi.stubGlobal('AudioWorkletNode', FakeWorkletNode);
  vi.stubGlobal('MediaMetadata', FakeMediaMetadata);
  vi.stubGlobal('document', doc);
  vi.stubGlobal('navigator', { mediaSession: session });
  log = new EventLog();
  probe = new AudioProbe(log, WORKLET_URL, ANCHOR_URL);
});

afterEach(() => {
  vi.useRealTimers();
  vi.unstubAllGlobals();
});

// ───────────── tests ─────────────

describe('AudioProbe.start', () => {
  it('builds one context, adds the module, creates the node with the contract options and sends config', async () => {
    await probe.start('direct', 'balanced');
    expect(ctxs()).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(lastCtx().options).toEqual({ latencyHint: 'balanced' });
    expect(lastCtx().modules).toEqual([WORKLET_URL]);
    const node = lastNode();
    expect(node.name).toBe('tesbox-probe');
    expect(node.options).toEqual({ numberOfInputs: 0, numberOfOutputs: 1, outputChannelCount: [2] });
    expect(node.port.sent[0]).toEqual({ type: 'config', k: 1, toneHz: 55, gainDb: -18 });
    expect(node.connections).toEqual([lastCtx().destination]);
    expect(FakeAudioElement.instances).toHaveLength(0);
    expect(probe.snapshot).toMatchObject({
      state: 'running',
      sampleRate: 48000,
      baseLatency: 0.01,
      outputLatency: 0.04,
      latencyHint: 'balanced',
      route: 'direct',
      perfInWorklet: null,
      quanta: 0,
      recoveries: { resumeOk: 0, resumeFail: 0, recreate: 0 },
    });
  });

  it('registers the Media Session with metadata, playing state and all 7 handlers', async () => {
    await probe.start('direct', 'balanced');
    expect((session.metadata as FakeMediaMetadata).init).toEqual({ title: 'Tesbox probe', artist: 'Tesbox' });
    expect(session.playbackState).toBe('playing');
    expect([...session.handlers.keys()].sort()).toEqual(
      ['nexttrack', 'pause', 'play', 'previoustrack', 'seekbackward', 'seekforward', 'stop'],
    );
    session.handlers.get('nexttrack')?.();
    session.handlers.get('pause')?.();
    expect(details('mediasession')).toEqual(['nexttrack', 'pause']);
  });

  it('media-element route: node → MediaStreamDestination → <audio>.srcObject, playing', async () => {
    await probe.start('media-element', 'playback');
    const ctx = lastCtx();
    expect(ctx.options).toEqual({ latencyHint: 'playback' });
    const msd = ctx.streamDestinations[0];
    expect(lastNode().connections).toEqual([msd]);
    const [el] = FakeAudioElement.instances;
    expect(el?.srcObject).toBe(msd?.stream);
    expect(el?.paused).toBe(false);
    expect(session.playbackState).toBe('playing');
  });

  it('direct-anchor route: node → destination plus a looping anchor <audio>', async () => {
    await probe.start('direct-anchor', 'balanced');
    expect(lastNode().connections).toEqual([lastCtx().destination]);
    const [el] = FakeAudioElement.instances;
    expect(el?.src).toBe(ANCHOR_URL);
    expect(el?.loop).toBe(true);
    expect(el?.paused).toBe(false);
  });

  it('resumes a context that starts suspended', async () => {
    const Orig = FakeAudioContext;
    vi.stubGlobal(
      'AudioContext',
      class extends Orig {
        constructor(o: AudioContextOptions) {
          super(o);
          this.state = 'suspended';
        }
      },
    );
    await probe.start('direct', 'balanced');
    expect(probe.snapshot.state).toBe('running');
  });

  it('rejects and leaves no context when addModule fails', async () => {
    const Orig = FakeAudioContext;
    vi.stubGlobal(
      'AudioContext',
      class extends Orig {
        constructor(o: AudioContextOptions) {
          super(o);
          this.addModuleFails = true;
        }
      },
    );
    await expect(probe.start('direct', 'balanced')).rejects.toThrow('404');
    expect(probe.snapshot.state).toBe('none');
    expect(lastCtx().state).toBe('closed');
    expect(details('error').some((d) => d.startsWith('worklet setup failed'))).toBe(true);
  });

  it('rejects and leaves no context when the processor cannot be constructed', async () => {
    vi.stubGlobal(
      'AudioWorkletNode',
      class {
        constructor() {
          throw new DOMException("processor 'tesbox-probe' is not registered", 'InvalidStateError');
        }
      },
    );
    await expect(probe.start('direct', 'balanced')).rejects.toThrow('not registered');
    expect(probe.snapshot.state).toBe('none');
    expect(lastCtx().state).toBe('closed');
    await probe.start('direct', 'balanced').catch(() => undefined); // a retry builds a fresh context
    expect(ctxs()).toHaveLength(2);
  });

  it('media-element without MediaStreamAudioDestinationNode rejects and leaves no context', async () => {
    const Orig = FakeAudioContext;
    vi.stubGlobal(
      'AudioContext',
      class extends Orig {
        constructor(o: AudioContextOptions) {
          super(o);
          (this as unknown as { createMediaStreamDestination: unknown }).createMediaStreamDestination = undefined;
        }
      },
    );
    await expect(probe.start('media-element', 'balanced')).rejects.toThrow('media-element route unsupported');
    expect(probe.snapshot.state).toBe('none');
    expect(lastCtx().state).toBe('closed');
    expect(openCtxs()).toHaveLength(0);
    expect(session.handlers.size).toBe(0); // the Media Session was never registered
  });

  it('a start whose addModule never settles rejects after 10 s and does not block later ops', async () => {
    FakeAudioContext.moduleGate = new Promise<void>(() => undefined);
    const started = probe.start('direct', 'balanced');
    const rejected = expect(started).rejects.toThrow('addModule timed out');
    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(10_000);
    await rejected;
    expect(probe.snapshot.state).toBe('none');
    expect(openCtxs()).toHaveLength(0);
    await probe.stop();
  });

  it('creates the context synchronously when no operation is pending (still inside the click)', async () => {
    const started = probe.start('direct', 'balanced');
    expect(ctxs()).toHaveLength(1); // no microtask has run yet
    await started;
    const recreated = probe.recreate();
    expect(ctxs()).toHaveLength(2);
    await recreated;
  });

  it('pings the new node once and logs the pong round trip', async () => {
    await probe.start('direct', 'balanced');
    expect(lastNode().port.sent).toContainEqual({ type: 'ping', id: 1 });
    lastNode().port.emit({ type: 'pong', id: 1, currentTime: 0.5 });
    expect(details('audio').some((d) => d.startsWith('pong rtt=') && d.endsWith('ctxTime=0.500'))).toBe(true);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Append the RF3 single-instance tests**

Append to the end of `tests/unit/audio.test.ts`:

```ts
describe('AudioProbe single-instance guarantees (RF3)', () => {
  it('start() twice concurrently creates exactly one AudioContext and one node', async () => {
    await Promise.all([probe.start('direct-anchor', 'balanced'), probe.start('direct-anchor', 'balanced')]);
    expect(ctxs()).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(FakeWorkletNode.instances).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(playing()).toHaveLength(1);
  });

  it('start() again with the same hint reuses the context (only the route may change)', async () => {
    await probe.start('direct', 'balanced');
    await probe.start('media-element', 'balanced');
    expect(ctxs()).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(probe.snapshot.route).toBe('media-element');
    expect(playing()).toHaveLength(1);
  });

  it('start() with a different latencyHint replaces the context: one open context at a time', async () => {
    await probe.start('direct', 'balanced');
    await probe.start('direct', 'playback');
    expect(ctxs()).toHaveLength(2);
    expect(ctxs()[0]?.state).toBe('closed');
    expect(openCtxs()).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(lastCtx().options).toEqual({ latencyHint: 'playback' });
    expect(probe.snapshot.latencyHint).toBe('playback');
    expect(probe.snapshot.recoveries.recreate).toBe(0);
  });

  it('setRoute() called rapidly 3× ends with exactly one connected route and one playing element', async () => {
    await probe.start('direct', 'balanced');
    await Promise.all([
      probe.setRoute('media-element'),
      probe.setRoute('direct-anchor'),
      probe.setRoute('media-element'),
    ]);
    expect(ctxs()).toHaveLength(1);
    const node = lastNode();
    expect(node.connections).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(node.connections[0]).toBe(lastCtx().streamDestinations.at(-1));
    expect(playing()).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(playing()[0]?.srcObject).toBe(lastCtx().streamDestinations.at(-1)?.stream);
    for (const el of FakeAudioElement.instances.slice(0, -1)) {
      expect(el.paused).toBe(true);
      expect(el.loadCalls).toBe(1); // load() releases the old element's media resource
      expect(el.srcObject).toBeNull();
      expect(el.src).toBe('');
    }
    expect(probe.snapshot.route).toBe('media-element');
  });

  it('setRoute() during a pending start() is applied after start completes', async () => {
    let release = (): void => undefined;
    FakeAudioContext.moduleGate = new Promise<void>((r) => (release = r));
    const started = probe.start('direct', 'balanced');
    const routed = probe.setRoute('media-element');
    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(0);
    release();
    await Promise.all([started, routed]);
    expect(probe.snapshot.route).toBe('media-element');
    expect(lastNode().connections).toEqual([lastCtx().streamDestinations[0]]);
    expect(playing()).toHaveLength(1);
  });

  it('recreate() during a pending start() never leaves a second graph connected', async () => {
    let release = (): void => undefined;
    FakeAudioContext.moduleGate = new Promise<void>((r) => (release = r));
    const started = probe.start('direct-anchor', 'balanced');
    const recreated = probe.recreate();
    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(0);
    release();
    await Promise.all([started, recreated]);
    expect(openCtxs()).toHaveLength(1);
    const current = lastNode();
    expect(current.ctx).toBe(openCtxs()[0]);
    for (const n of FakeWorkletNode.instances) if (n !== current) expect(n.connections).toEqual([]);
    expect(playing()).toHaveLength(1);
  });

  it('switching to direct stops every media element', async () => {
    await probe.start('direct-anchor', 'balanced');
    await probe.setRoute('direct');
    expect(playing()).toHaveLength(0);
    expect(lastNode().connections).toEqual([lastCtx().destination]);
  });

  it('our own route switch does not log an element pause; an external pause does', async () => {
    await probe.start('direct-anchor', 'balanced');
    await probe.setRoute('media-element');
    expect(details('audio').some((d) => d.endsWith('element paused'))).toBe(false);
    FakeAudioElement.instances.at(-1)?.pause();
    expect(details('audio')).toContain('media-element element paused');
  });

  it('setRoute() before start only records the route', async () => {
    await probe.setRoute('media-element');
    expect(ctxs()).toHaveLength(0);
    expect(probe.snapshot.route).toBeNull();
  });

  it('start() after stop() rebuilds context, route, Media Session and the visibility listener', async () => {
    await probe.start('direct-anchor', 'balanced');
    await probe.stop();
    await probe.start('media-element', 'playback');
    expect(ctxs()).toHaveLength(2);
    expect(openCtxs()).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(lastCtx().options).toEqual({ latencyHint: 'playback' });
    expect(lastNode().connections).toEqual([lastCtx().streamDestinations[0]]);
    expect(playing()).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(session.playbackState).toBe('playing');
    expect([...session.handlers.values()].every((h) => typeof h === 'function')).toBe(true);
    expect(doc.listeners.get('visibilitychange')?.size).toBe(1);
    expect(probe.snapshot).toMatchObject({ state: 'running', route: 'media-element', latencyHint: 'playback' });
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Append the report, recovery, hidden-clock and benchmark tests**

Append to the end of `tests/unit/audio.test.ts`:

```ts
describe('AudioProbe worklet reports and state changes', () => {
  it('reflects the latest worklet report in the snapshot and in lastWorkletReport', async () => {
    await probe.start('direct', 'balanced');
    expect(probe.lastWorkletReport).toBeNull();
    lastNode().port.emit({
      type: 'report', quanta: 94, perfInWorklet: false, busyMs: 20, windowMs: 250, sampleRate: 48000, currentTime: 0.25,
    });
    expect(probe.snapshot.quanta).toBe(94);
    expect(probe.snapshot.perfInWorklet).toBe(false);
    expect(probe.lastWorkletReport).toMatchObject({ quanta: 94, busyMs: 20, windowMs: 250, currentTime: 0.25 });
    await probe.stop();
    expect(probe.lastWorkletReport).toBeNull();
  });

  it("logs statechange as log('audio', state)", async () => {
    await probe.start('direct', 'balanced');
    lastCtx().setState('interrupted');
    lastCtx().setState('suspended');
    expect(details('audio')).toEqual(expect.arrayContaining(['interrupted', 'suspended']));
  });

  it('setK posts a config message', async () => {
    await probe.start('direct', 'balanced');
    probe.setK(8);
    expect(lastNode().port.sent.at(-1)).toEqual({ type: 'config', k: 8, toneHz: 55, gainDb: -18 });
  });

  it('setTone posts a config message, and a rebuilt node gets the same tone', async () => {
    await probe.start('direct', 'balanced');
    probe.setTone(110);
    expect(lastNode().port.sent.at(-1)).toEqual({ type: 'config', k: 1, toneHz: 110, gainDb: -18 });
    await probe.recreate();
    expect(lastNode().port.sent[0]).toEqual({ type: 'config', k: 1, toneHz: 110, gainDb: -18 });
  });
});

describe('AudioProbe recovery', () => {
  it('resume() succeeds when the context reaches running', async () => {
    await probe.start('direct-anchor', 'balanced');
    lastCtx().setState('suspended');
    FakeAudioElement.instances[0]?.pause();
    expect(await probe.resume()).toBe(true);
    expect(probe.snapshot.recoveries).toEqual({ resumeOk: 1, resumeFail: 0, recreate: 0 });
    expect(FakeAudioElement.instances[0]?.paused).toBe(false); // route element restarted after the resume check
    expect(details('audio')).toContain('resume ok state=running');
  });

  it('resume() reports failure after 500 ms when the context is interrupted (promise never settles)', async () => {
    await probe.start('direct', 'balanced');
    lastCtx().setState('interrupted');
    lastCtx().resumeMode = 'hang';
    const p = probe.resume();
    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(499);
    let settled = false;
    void p.then(() => (settled = true));
    await Promise.resolve();
    expect(settled).toBe(false);
    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(1);
    expect(await p).toBe(false);
    expect(probe.snapshot.recoveries.resumeFail).toBe(1);
  });

  it('resume() reports failure when resume() fulfils but the context stays interrupted', async () => {
    await probe.start('direct', 'balanced');
    const ctx = lastCtx();
    ctx.setState('interrupted');
    ctx.resume = (): Promise<void> => Promise.resolve(); // fulfils without leaving 'interrupted'
    expect(await probe.resume()).toBe(false);
    expect(probe.snapshot.recoveries).toEqual({ resumeOk: 0, resumeFail: 1, recreate: 0 });
    expect(details('audio')).toContain('resume failed state=interrupted');
  });

  it('stop() abandons a close() that never settles after 2 s, and later operations still run', async () => {
    await probe.start('direct', 'balanced');
    lastCtx().close = (): Promise<void> => new Promise<void>(() => undefined);
    const stopped = probe.stop();
    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(2000);
    await stopped;
    expect(details('error')).toContain('close failed: close timed out after 2000 ms');
    await probe.start('direct', 'balanced');
    expect(ctxs()).toHaveLength(2);
    expect(probe.snapshot.state).toBe('running');
  });

  it('resume() reports failure when resume() rejects', async () => {
    await probe.start('direct', 'balanced');
    lastCtx().setState('suspended');
    lastCtx().resumeMode = 'reject';
    expect(await probe.resume()).toBe(false);
    expect(probe.snapshot.recoveries.resumeFail).toBe(1);
  });

  it('recreate() closes the old context and rebuilds module, node, route and session', async () => {
    await probe.start('media-element', 'playback');
    const old = lastCtx();
    const oldEl = FakeAudioElement.instances[0];
    session.handlers.clear();
    await probe.recreate();
    expect(ctxs()).toHaveLength(2);
    expect(old.state).toBe('closed');
    expect(openCtxs()).toHaveLength(1);
    const ctx = lastCtx();
    expect(ctx.options).toEqual({ latencyHint: 'playback' });
    expect(ctx.modules).toEqual([WORKLET_URL]);
    expect(FakeWorkletNode.instances).toHaveLength(2);
    expect(lastNode().connections).toEqual([ctx.streamDestinations[0]]);
    expect(oldEl?.paused).toBe(true);
    expect(playing()).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(session.handlers.size).toBe(7);
    expect(probe.snapshot.recoveries.recreate).toBe(1);
    expect(probe.snapshot.route).toBe('media-element');
    const before = log.count('audio');
    old.setState('suspended'); // stale context: ignored
    expect(log.count('audio')).toBe(before);
  });

  it('recreate() before any start does nothing', async () => {
    await probe.recreate();
    expect(ctxs()).toHaveLength(0);
    expect(probe.snapshot.recoveries.recreate).toBe(0);
  });

  it('recreate() after setRoute-before-start or after stop() does nothing', async () => {
    await probe.setRoute('media-element');
    await probe.recreate();
    expect(ctxs()).toHaveLength(0);
    await probe.start('direct', 'balanced');
    await probe.stop();
    await probe.recreate();
    expect(ctxs()).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(openCtxs()).toHaveLength(0);
    expect(probe.snapshot.recoveries.recreate).toBe(0);
  });

  it('stop() closes the context, stops elements and clears the Media Session', async () => {
    await probe.start('direct-anchor', 'balanced');
    await probe.stop();
    expect(lastCtx().state).toBe('closed');
    expect(playing()).toHaveLength(0);
    expect(session.playbackState).toBe('none');
    expect([...session.handlers.values()].every((h) => h === null)).toBe(true);
    expect(probe.snapshot).toMatchObject({ state: 'none', route: null });
    expect(doc.listeners.get('visibilitychange')?.size ?? 0).toBe(0);
  });
});

describe('AudioProbe hidden clock', () => {
  it('is null until a hidden period ends, then audio-clock / wall-clock over that period', async () => {
    await probe.start('direct', 'balanced');
    expect(probe.hiddenClockRatio).toBeNull();
    const ctx = lastCtx();
    ctx.currentTime = 10;
    doc.setVisibility('hidden');
    vi.advanceTimersByTime(10_000);
    ctx.currentTime = 15; // audio advanced only 5 s in 10 s of wall time
    doc.setVisibility('visible');
    expect(probe.hiddenClockRatio).toBe(0.5);
    expect(details('audio').some((d) => d.startsWith('hidden 10.0s clock ratio=0.5'))).toBe(true);
  });

  it('ignores a hidden period that spans a context recreation', async () => {
    await probe.start('direct', 'balanced');
    doc.setVisibility('hidden');
    await probe.recreate();
    vi.advanceTimersByTime(1000);
    doc.setVisibility('visible');
    expect(probe.hiddenClockRatio).toBeNull();
  });
});

describe('AudioProbe.benchmark', () => {
  /**
   * Simulates the audio thread: every 10 ms of fake wall time the context clock advances by
   * 10 ms × rate(k), and every 250 ms the node posts a busy report. k comes from the last config.
   */
  function simulateAudio(rate: (k: number) => number, busyFrac: (k: number) => number): void {
    let quanta = 0;
    let sinceReport = 0;
    setInterval(() => {
      const ctx = lastCtx();
      const node = lastNode();
      const k = node.lastK() ?? 1;
      ctx.currentTime += 0.01 * rate(k);
      sinceReport += 10;
      quanta += 4;
      if (sinceReport >= 250) {
        sinceReport = 0;
        node.port.emit({
          type: 'report', quanta, perfInWorklet: false, busyMs: 250 * busyFrac(k), windowMs: 250,
          sampleRate: 48000, currentTime: ctx.currentTime,
        });
      }
    }, 10);
  }

  it('finds headroomK = largest k below the first overloaded one and restores k', async () => {
    await probe.start('direct', 'balanced');
    lastCtx().currentTime = 1;
    simulateAudio((k) => (k >= 8 ? 0.9 : 1), (k) => Math.min(1, 0.1 * k));
    const p = probe.benchmark();
    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(5 * 3250 + 100);
    const { results, headroomK } = await p;
    expect(results.map((r) => r.k)).toEqual([1, 2, 4, 8, 16]);
    expect(results.map((r) => r.overloaded)).toEqual([false, false, false, true, true]);
    expect(results[0]?.ratio).toBeCloseTo(1, 2);
    expect(results[3]?.ratio).toBeCloseTo(0.9, 2);
    expect(results[1]?.busyFrac).toBeCloseTo(0.2, 2);
    expect(headroomK).toBe(4);
    expect(lastNode().lastK()).toBe(1);
    const ks = lastNode().port.sent.filter((m) => m.type === 'config').map((m) => m.k);
    expect(ks).toEqual([1, 1, 2, 4, 8, 16, 1]);
  });

  it('headroomK is 0 when k = 1 is already overloaded', async () => {
    await probe.start('direct', 'balanced');
    lastCtx().currentTime = 1;
    simulateAudio(() => 0.9, () => 1);
    const p = probe.benchmark([1, 2], 3);
    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(2 * 3250 + 100);
    const { results, headroomK } = await p;
    expect(results.every((r) => r.overloaded)).toBe(true);
    expect(headroomK).toBe(0);
  });

  it('quick bench ([1,2] × 1 s) completes with 2 results using the whole-span ratio', async () => {
    await probe.start('direct', 'balanced');
    lastCtx().currentTime = 1;
    simulateAudio(() => 1, () => 0.05);
    const p = probe.benchmark([1, 2], 1);
    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(2 * 1250 + 100);
    const { results, headroomK } = await p;
    expect(results).toHaveLength(2);
    expect(results.every((r) => !r.overloaded)).toBe(true);
    expect(headroomK).toBe(2);
  });

  it('a step too short for 2 drift windows is judged by the whole-span ratio', async () => {
    await probe.start('direct', 'balanced');
    lastCtx().currentTime = 1;
    simulateAudio((k) => (k >= 2 ? 0.9 : 1), () => 0.1);
    const p = probe.benchmark([1, 2], 1);
    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(2 * 1250 + 100);
    const { results, headroomK } = await p;
    expect(results.map((r) => r.overloaded)).toEqual([false, true]);
    expect(headroomK).toBe(1);
  });

  it('headroomK stops at the first overloaded k even if a larger k passes', async () => {
    await probe.start('direct', 'balanced');
    lastCtx().currentTime = 1;
    simulateAudio((k) => (k === 2 ? 0.9 : 1), () => 0.1);
    const p = probe.benchmark([1, 2, 4], 3);
    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(3 * 3250 + 100);
    const { results, headroomK } = await p;
    expect(results.map((r) => r.overloaded)).toEqual([false, true, false]);
    expect(headroomK).toBe(1);
  });

  it('rejects when audio is not running, or when a benchmark is already running', async () => {
    await expect(probe.benchmark()).rejects.toThrow('audio is not running');
    await probe.start('direct', 'balanced');
    lastCtx().currentTime = 1;
    simulateAudio(() => 1, () => 0.1);
    const p = probe.benchmark([1], 1);
    await expect(probe.benchmark([1], 1)).rejects.toThrow('already running');
    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(1350);
    await expect(p).resolves.toMatchObject({ headroomK: 1 });
  });

  it('restores the pre-benchmark k on a node rebuilt mid-benchmark', async () => {
    await probe.start('direct', 'balanced');
    lastCtx().currentTime = 1;
    simulateAudio(() => 1, () => 0.1);
    const p = probe.benchmark([1, 16], 1);
    const rejected = expect(p).rejects.toThrow('context changed');
    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(1600); // inside the k = 16 step
    expect(lastNode().lastK()).toBe(16);
    await probe.recreate();
    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(500);
    await rejected;
    expect(lastNode().lastK()).toBe(1);
  });

  it('rejects if the context is recreated mid-benchmark', async () => {
    await probe.start('direct', 'balanced');
    lastCtx().currentTime = 1;
    simulateAudio(() => 1, () => 0.1);
    const p = probe.benchmark([1, 2], 3);
    const rejected = expect(p).rejects.toThrow('context changed');
    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(500);
    await probe.recreate();
    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(500);
    await rejected;
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Run it to see it fail**

Run: `npx vitest run tests/unit/audio.test.ts`
Expected: FAIL with `Error: Cannot find module '…src/probe/audio' imported from …`. Vitest prints the path either as `'../../src/probe/audio'` or as `'/src/probe/audio'`.

- [ ] **Step 5: Implement AudioProbe**

Create `src/probe/audio.ts`:

```ts
import type { OutputRoute } from '../contracts';
import { PROBE_GAIN_DB, PROBE_PROCESSOR, PROBE_TONE_HZ, type ProbeToWorklet, type WorkletToProbe } from './audio-protocol';
import { DriftMonitor, type DriftSample } from './drift';
import type { EventLog } from './log';
import { errText, withTimeout } from './log';

export interface BenchResult {
  k: number;
  ratio: number;
  overloaded: boolean;
  busyFrac: number | null;
}

export interface AudioProbeState {
  state: AudioContextState | 'none';
  sampleRate: number | null;
  baseLatency: number | null;
  outputLatency: number | null;
  latencyHint: 'balanced' | 'playback';
  route: OutputRoute | null;
  perfInWorklet: boolean | null;
  quanta: number;
  recoveries: { resumeOk: number; resumeFail: number; recreate: number };
}

type Report = Extract<WorkletToProbe, { type: 'report' }>;
type LatencyHint = 'balanced' | 'playback';

const MEDIA_ACTIONS: readonly MediaSessionAction[] = [
  'play',
  'pause',
  'stop',
  'nexttrack',
  'previoustrack',
  'seekbackward',
  'seekforward',
];
const RESUME_TIMEOUT_MS = 500;
const ADD_MODULE_TIMEOUT_MS = 10_000; // a hung module fetch (car network stall) must not block the queue
const CLOSE_TIMEOUT_MS = 2000;
const BENCH_KS: readonly number[] = [1, 2, 4, 8, 16];
const BENCH_SECONDS = 3;
const BENCH_SETTLE_MS = 250; // let the new k reach the worklet and one report window flush
const BENCH_SAMPLE_MS = 100;
const DRIFT_WINDOW_MS = 1000;
const DRIFT_THRESHOLD = 0.98;

function delay(ms: number): Promise<void> {
  return new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, ms));
}

/** true if `p` fulfils within `ms`; false if it rejects or is still pending (e.g. an interrupted context). */
function settlesWithin(p: Promise<unknown>, ms: number): Promise<boolean> {
  return withTimeout(p, ms, 'resume').then(() => true, () => false);
}

function round3(v: number): number {
  return Math.round(v * 1000) / 1000;
}

/** Largest k such that it and every smaller benchmarked k ran without overload (0 if the smallest overloaded). */
function headroomOf(results: readonly BenchResult[]): number {
  let best = 0;
  for (const r of [...results].sort((a, b) => a.k - b.k)) {
    if (r.overloaded) break;
    best = r.k;
  }
  return best;
}

/**
 * Phase 0 audio probe: AudioContext lifecycle, the three output routes (spec §6.4), Media Session,
 * k× headroom benchmark, resume/recreate recovery and the hidden-page clock check.
 * Every state-changing call is serialised so overlapping taps can never build two contexts,
 * two connected routes or two playing media elements.
 */
export class AudioProbe {
  private ctx: AudioContext | null = null;
  private node: AudioWorkletNode | null = null;
  private routeEl: HTMLAudioElement | null = null;
  private route: OutputRoute | null = null;
  /** Route most recently asked for (start/setRoute); recreate() rebuilds it. */
  private wantedRoute: OutputRoute | null = null;
  private latencyHint: LatencyHint = 'balanced';
  private k = 1;
  private toneHz = PROBE_TONE_HZ;
  private lastReport: Report | null = null;
  private readonly recoveries = { resumeOk: 0, resumeFail: 0, recreate: 0 };
  private hiddenAt: { ctx: AudioContext; ctxTimeS: number; perfMs: number } | null = null;
  private hiddenRatio: number | null = null;
  private visibilityAttached = false;
  private benchRunning = false;
  private reportListener: ((r: Report) => void) | null = null;
  private pingId = 0;
  private pingSentAt = 0;
  private pendingOps = 0;
  private queue: Promise<void> = Promise.resolve();

  constructor(
    private readonly log: EventLog,
    private readonly workletUrl: string,
    private readonly anchorUrl: string,
  ) {}

  /** Ratio of audio-clock advance to wall time across the last hidden period, null if never hidden. */
  get hiddenClockRatio(): number | null {
    return this.hiddenRatio;
  }

  /** Latest worklet report of the current node (a copy), null before the first one. */
  get lastWorkletReport(): Readonly<Report> | null {
    return this.lastReport === null ? null : { ...this.lastReport };
  }

  get snapshot(): AudioProbeState {
    const ctx = this.ctx;
    return {
      state: ctx !== null ? ctx.state : 'none',
      sampleRate: ctx !== null ? ctx.sampleRate : null,
      baseLatency: ctx !== null && typeof ctx.baseLatency === 'number' ? ctx.baseLatency : null,
      outputLatency: ctx !== null && typeof ctx.outputLatency === 'number' ? ctx.outputLatency : null,
      latencyHint: this.latencyHint,
      route: this.route,
      perfInWorklet: this.lastReport !== null ? this.lastReport.perfInWorklet : null,
      quanta: this.lastReport !== null ? this.lastReport.quanta : 0,
      recoveries: { ...this.recoveries },
    };
  }

  /** MUST be called from a click handler. Creates the context, adds the module, connects the route, registers Media Session. */
  start(route: OutputRoute, latencyHint: LatencyHint): Promise<void> {
    return this.serial(async () => {
      this.wantedRoute = route;
      if (this.ctx !== null) {
        if (latencyHint !== this.latencyHint) {
          // The latencyHint A/B needs a fresh context: there is only ever one.
          this.latencyHint = latencyHint;
          await this.rebuild(route);
        } else if (route !== this.route) {
          this.connectRoute(route);
        }
        return;
      }
      this.latencyHint = latencyHint;
      this.attachVisibility();
      await this.build(route);
    });
  }

  /** Reconnects without recreating the context. */
  setRoute(route: OutputRoute): Promise<void> {
    return this.serial(async () => {
      this.wantedRoute = route;
      if (this.ctx === null) {
        this.log.push('note', `route ${route} applies on start`);
        return;
      }
      if (route !== this.route) this.connectRoute(route);
    });
  }

  setK(k: number): void {
    this.k = k;
    this.postConfig();
  }

  /**
   * Probe tone in Hz for the current node and every node built later (the worklet clamps it to
   * [1, 1000]). T10's Pitch step toggles 55 ↔ 110 Hz so the tester can hear each route's
   * tap → pitch-change delay (spec §6.4), which outputLatency alone does not show.
   */
  setTone(toneHz: number): void {
    this.toneHz = toneHz;
    this.postConfig();
  }

  /** Runs each k for secondsEach using DriftMonitor + worklet busy reports; headroomK = largest k not overloaded (0 if k=1 overloaded). */
  async benchmark(
    ks: number[] = [...BENCH_KS],
    secondsEach = BENCH_SECONDS,
  ): Promise<{ results: BenchResult[]; headroomK: number }> {
    if (this.benchRunning) throw new Error('benchmark already running');
    const ctx = this.ctx;
    if (ctx === null || this.node === null || ctx.state !== 'running') throw new Error('audio is not running');
    this.benchRunning = true;
    const prevK = this.k;
    const results: BenchResult[] = [];
    try {
      for (const k of ks) results.push(await this.benchOne(ctx, k, secondsEach));
    } finally {
      this.reportListener = null;
      this.benchRunning = false;
      this.setK(prevK); // also re-configures a node rebuilt mid-run (build() posted the bench k)
    }
    const headroomK = headroomOf(results);
    this.log.push('audio', `bench headroomK=${headroomK}`);
    return { results, headroomK };
  }

  /** From a tap. true when the context is running within 500 ms; logs the result. */
  resume(): Promise<boolean> {
    return this.serial(async () => {
      const ctx = this.ctx;
      if (ctx === null) {
        this.log.push('audio', 'resume: no context');
        return false;
      }
      const settled = await settlesWithin(ctx.resume(), RESUME_TIMEOUT_MS);
      const ok = settled && ctx.state === 'running';
      if (ok) this.recoveries.resumeOk++;
      else this.recoveries.resumeFail++;
      this.log.push('audio', `resume ${ok ? 'ok' : 'failed'} state=${ctx.state}`);
      const el = this.routeEl;
      if (ok && el !== null && el.paused && this.route !== null) this.playElement(el, this.route);
      return ok;
    });
  }

  /** Close + new context inside the tap; re-adds module, route and Media Session. No-op without a context. */
  recreate(): Promise<void> {
    return this.serial(async () => {
      const route = this.wantedRoute;
      if (this.ctx === null || route === null) {
        this.log.push('audio', 'recreate: no context (not started or stopped)');
        return;
      }
      this.recoveries.recreate++;
      this.log.push('audio', 'recreate');
      this.attachVisibility();
      await this.rebuild(route);
    });
  }

  stop(): Promise<void> {
    return this.serial(async () => {
      const ctx = this.teardown();
      this.clearMediaSession();
      this.detachVisibility();
      if (ctx !== null) {
        await withTimeout(ctx.close(), CLOSE_TIMEOUT_MS, 'close').catch((e: unknown) =>
          this.log.push('error', `close failed: ${errText(e)}`),
        );
        this.log.push('audio', 'stopped');
      }
    });
  }

  // ───────────────────────────── internals ─────────────────────────────

  /**
   * Runs ops one at a time, in call order; a failed op does not block later ones. When nothing is
   * queued the op starts synchronously, so start()/recreate() create the AudioContext and resume()
   * calls ctx.resume() inside the caller's click handler. The route element's play() runs after
   * addModule (or the resume check) resolves and relies on the document's sticky user activation.
   */
  private serial<T>(op: () => Promise<T>): Promise<T> {
    const idle = this.pendingOps === 0;
    this.pendingOps++;
    const result = idle ? op() : this.queue.then(op);
    this.queue = result.then(
      () => {
        this.pendingOps--;
      },
      () => {
        this.pendingOps--;
      },
    );
    return result;
  }

  private async build(route: OutputRoute): Promise<void> {
    const ctx = new AudioContext({ latencyHint: this.latencyHint });
    this.ctx = ctx;
    this.lastReport = null;
    ctx.onstatechange = () => {
      if (this.ctx === ctx) this.log.push('audio', ctx.state);
    };
    this.log.push('audio', `context ${ctx.state} sr=${ctx.sampleRate} hint=${this.latencyHint}`);
    let node: AudioWorkletNode;
    try {
      if (!ctx.audioWorklet) throw new Error('AudioWorklet unavailable');
      await withTimeout(ctx.audioWorklet.addModule(this.workletUrl), ADD_MODULE_TIMEOUT_MS, 'addModule');
      node = new AudioWorkletNode(ctx, PROBE_PROCESSOR, {
        numberOfInputs: 0,
        numberOfOutputs: 1,
        outputChannelCount: [2],
      });
    } catch (e) {
      this.log.push('error', `worklet setup failed: ${errText(e)}`);
      this.ctx = null;
      await withTimeout(ctx.close(), CLOSE_TIMEOUT_MS, 'close').catch(() => undefined);
      throw e;
    }
    node.port.onmessage = (e: MessageEvent<WorkletToProbe>) => this.onWorkletMessage(node, e.data);
    node.onprocessorerror = () => this.log.push('error', 'worklet processorerror');
    this.node = node;
    this.postConfig();
    try {
      this.connectRoute(route);
    } catch (e) {
      // e.g. media-element without MediaStreamAudioDestinationNode: leave no live context behind.
      this.teardown();
      await withTimeout(ctx.close(), CLOSE_TIMEOUT_MS, 'close').catch(() => undefined);
      throw e;
    }
    this.registerMediaSession();
    this.pingId++;
    this.pingSentAt = performance.now();
    const ping: ProbeToWorklet = { type: 'ping', id: this.pingId };
    node.port.postMessage(ping);
    if (ctx.state !== 'running') {
      const ok = await settlesWithin(ctx.resume(), RESUME_TIMEOUT_MS);
      this.log.push('audio', `initial resume ${ok ? 'ok' : 'pending'} state=${ctx.state}`);
    }
  }

  /** Close the old context (not awaited, so when serial() ran this op synchronously the new one is created inside the tap) and build anew. */
  private async rebuild(route: OutputRoute): Promise<void> {
    const old = this.teardown();
    if (old !== null) old.close().catch((e: unknown) => this.log.push('error', `close failed: ${errText(e)}`));
    await this.build(route);
  }

  /** Disconnects everything and forgets the context; returns it so the caller decides how to close it. */
  private teardown(): AudioContext | null {
    this.disconnectRoute();
    if (this.node !== null) {
      this.node.port.onmessage = null;
      this.node.onprocessorerror = null;
      this.node = null;
    }
    const ctx = this.ctx;
    this.ctx = null;
    this.hiddenAt = null;
    this.lastReport = null;
    return ctx;
  }

  private connectRoute(route: OutputRoute): void {
    const ctx = this.ctx;
    const node = this.node;
    if (ctx === null || node === null) return;
    if (route === 'media-element' && typeof ctx.createMediaStreamDestination !== 'function') {
      this.log.push('error', 'media-element route unsupported (no MediaStreamAudioDestinationNode)');
      throw new Error('media-element route unsupported');
    }
    this.disconnectRoute();
    if (route === 'media-element') {
      const msd = ctx.createMediaStreamDestination();
      node.connect(msd);
      const el = this.makeElement(route);
      el.srcObject = msd.stream;
      this.playElement(el, route);
    } else {
      node.connect(ctx.destination);
      if (route === 'direct-anchor') {
        const el = this.makeElement(route);
        el.src = this.anchorUrl;
        el.loop = true;
        this.playElement(el, route);
      }
    }
    this.route = route;
    this.log.push('audio', `route ${route}`);
  }

  private disconnectRoute(): void {
    this.node?.disconnect();
    const el = this.routeEl;
    if (el !== null) {
      this.routeEl = null;
      el.onpause = null; // our own pause is not an interruption
      el.pause();
      el.srcObject = null;
      el.removeAttribute('src');
      el.load(); // releases the media resource
    }
    this.route = null;
  }

  private makeElement(route: OutputRoute): HTMLAudioElement {
    const el = document.createElement('audio');
    el.preload = 'auto';
    el.onpause = () => {
      if (this.routeEl === el) this.log.push('audio', `${route} element paused`);
    };
    this.routeEl = el;
    return el;
  }

  private playElement(el: HTMLAudioElement, route: OutputRoute): void {
    // Not awaited: a play() that never settles must not block the operation queue.
    el.play().then(
      () => {
        if (this.routeEl === el) this.log.push('audio', `${route} element playing`);
      },
      (e: unknown) => {
        if (this.routeEl === el) this.log.push('error', `${route} element play failed: ${errText(e)}`);
      },
    );
  }

  private registerMediaSession(): void {
    const ms = typeof navigator !== 'undefined' ? navigator.mediaSession : undefined;
    if (ms === undefined) {
      this.log.push('note', 'Media Session unavailable');
      return;
    }
    try {
      if (typeof MediaMetadata === 'function') {
        ms.metadata = new MediaMetadata({ title: 'Tesbox probe', artist: 'Tesbox' });
      }
      ms.playbackState = 'playing';
    } catch (e) {
      this.log.push('error', `media session metadata: ${errText(e)}`);
    }
    for (const action of MEDIA_ACTIONS) {
      try {
        ms.setActionHandler(action, () => this.log.push('mediasession', action));
      } catch {
        this.log.push('note', `media session action unsupported: ${action}`);
      }
    }
  }

  private clearMediaSession(): void {
    const ms = typeof navigator !== 'undefined' ? navigator.mediaSession : undefined;
    if (ms === undefined) return;
    for (const action of MEDIA_ACTIONS) {
      try {
        ms.setActionHandler(action, null);
      } catch {
        // unsupported action: nothing was registered
      }
    }
    ms.playbackState = 'none';
    ms.metadata = null;
  }

  /** Current k and tone to the current node; build() calls it too, so a rebuilt node keeps both. */
  private postConfig(): void {
    const msg: ProbeToWorklet = { type: 'config', k: this.k, toneHz: this.toneHz, gainDb: PROBE_GAIN_DB };
    this.node?.port.postMessage(msg);
  }

  private onWorkletMessage(node: AudioWorkletNode, msg: WorkletToProbe): void {
    if (node !== this.node || typeof msg !== 'object' || msg === null) return;
    if (msg.type === 'report') {
      this.lastReport = msg;
      this.reportListener?.(msg);
    } else if (msg.type === 'pong' && msg.id === this.pingId) {
      const rttMs = Math.round(performance.now() - this.pingSentAt);
      this.log.push('audio', `pong rtt=${rttMs}ms ctxTime=${msg.currentTime.toFixed(3)}`);
    }
  }

  private async benchOne(ctx: AudioContext, k: number, secondsEach: number): Promise<BenchResult> {
    this.setK(k);
    await delay(BENCH_SETTLE_MS);
    this.assertCurrent(ctx);
    const monitor = new DriftMonitor(DRIFT_WINDOW_MS, DRIFT_THRESHOLD);
    let windows = 0;
    let busyMs = 0;
    let windowMs = 0;
    this.reportListener = (r) => {
      busyMs += r.busyMs;
      windowMs += r.windowMs;
    };
    let first: DriftSample | null = null;
    let last: DriftSample | null = null;
    const endAt = performance.now() + Math.max(0, secondsEach) * 1000;
    for (;;) {
      const s = this.sampleClock(ctx);
      if (s.contextTimeS !== 0) {
        first ??= s;
        last = s;
        if (monitor.push(s) !== null) windows++;
      }
      if (performance.now() >= endAt) break;
      await delay(BENCH_SAMPLE_MS);
      this.assertCurrent(ctx);
    }
    this.reportListener = null;
    const ratio =
      first !== null && last !== null && last.perfMs > first.perfMs
        ? ((last.contextTimeS - first.contextTimeS) * 1000) / (last.perfMs - first.perfMs)
        : 0;
    // ≥ 2 windows: sustained-overload rule (robust to clock quantisation); shorter runs: whole-span ratio.
    const overloaded = windows >= 2 ? monitor.overloaded : ratio < DRIFT_THRESHOLD;
    const result: BenchResult = {
      k,
      ratio: round3(ratio),
      overloaded,
      busyFrac: windowMs > 0 ? round3(busyMs / windowMs) : null,
    };
    this.log.push(
      'audio',
      `bench k=${k} ratio=${result.ratio} overloaded=${overloaded} busy=${result.busyFrac ?? 'n/a'}`,
    );
    return result;
  }

  /** Audio clock vs wall clock (spec §5.7): getOutputTimestamp() {contextTime, performanceTime} pairs. */
  private sampleClock(ctx: AudioContext): DriftSample {
    if (typeof ctx.getOutputTimestamp === 'function') {
      const ts = ctx.getOutputTimestamp();
      const perfMs = ts.performanceTime ?? 0;
      // contextTime and performanceTime describe the same render callback: no callback-period jitter.
      return { contextTimeS: ts.contextTime ?? 0, perfMs: perfMs > 0 ? perfMs : performance.now() };
    }
    return { contextTimeS: ctx.currentTime, perfMs: performance.now() };
  }

  private assertCurrent(ctx: AudioContext): void {
    if (this.ctx !== ctx) throw new Error('audio context changed during benchmark');
  }

  private attachVisibility(): void {
    if (this.visibilityAttached || typeof document === 'undefined') return;
    document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', this.onVisibility);
    this.visibilityAttached = true;
  }

  private detachVisibility(): void {
    if (!this.visibilityAttached) return;
    document.removeEventListener('visibilitychange', this.onVisibility);
    this.visibilityAttached = false;
  }

  private readonly onVisibility = (): void => {
    const ctx = this.ctx;
    if (document.visibilityState === 'hidden') {
      this.hiddenAt = ctx !== null ? { ctx, ctxTimeS: ctx.currentTime, perfMs: performance.now() } : null;
      return;
    }
    const h = this.hiddenAt;
    this.hiddenAt = null;
    if (h === null || ctx === null || h.ctx !== ctx) return;
    const elapsedMs = performance.now() - h.perfMs;
    if (elapsedMs <= 0) return;
    this.hiddenRatio = round3(((ctx.currentTime - h.ctxTimeS) * 1000) / elapsedMs);
    this.log.push(
      'audio',
      `hidden ${(elapsedMs / 1000).toFixed(1)}s clock ratio=${this.hiddenRatio} state=${ctx.state}`,
    );
  };
}
```

### Task 8: InputProbe — keys, wheel, visibility, gamepads, Media Session counts, DeviceMotion

**Files:**
- Create: `src/probe/inputs.ts`
- Test: `tests/unit/inputs.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes:
  - From T4, `src/probe/log.ts`: `EventLog.push(kind, detail)`, `EventLog.subscribe(fn: (e: LogEntry) => void): () => void`, `type LogEntry` and `errText(e: unknown): string` (value import). The kinds used are `'key'`, `'wheel'`, `'gamepad'`, `'visibility'`, `'motion'` and `'mediasession'`.
  - DOM:
    - `Window`: `addEventListener`/`removeEventListener`, `document`, `navigator.getGamepads()`, `requestAnimationFrame`/`cancelAnimationFrame`, and `DeviceMotionEvent`, with the optional static `requestPermission()`.
    - The event and object types `KeyboardEvent`, `WheelEvent`, `DeviceMotionEvent` and `Gamepad`.
- Produces: in `src/probe/inputs.ts`, `export interface InputCounts`, `export interface MotionReport` and `export class InputProbe { constructor(log: EventLog, target?: Window /* defaults to window */); attach(): void; detach(): void; readonly counts: InputCounts; probeMotion(durationMs?: number /* 5000 */): Promise<MotionReport> }`. The behaviours below are the ones other tasks rely on.
  - **`counts`:** a fresh copy on every read.
    - `keys` holds the distinct values of `key || code || 'keyCode:<n>'` from keydown events, in first-seen order, up to 64.
    - `mediaSession` counts the log entries of kind `'mediasession'` pushed after `attach()`, keyed by the detail.
    - `gamepads` holds the distinct gamepad ids.
  - **`attach()` and `detach()`:**
    - `attach()` is idempotent. It adds keydown and keyup listeners, a passive wheel listener, a `visibilitychange` listener on `target.document`, the log subscription, and the rAF gamepad poll.
    - `detach()` removes all of them.
    - Neither ever listens to `deviceorientation`.
  - **Log lines:**
    - `key`: `<keydown|keyup> key=<key> code=<code> keyCode=<n>`.
    - `wheel`: `dx=<n> dy=<n> mode=<deltaMode>`, deltas to 2 decimals.
    - `visibility`: `visible` or `hidden`.
    - `gamepad`: `connected index=<i> id=<id> mapping=<m|none> buttons=<n> axes=<n>`, `pad=<i> button=<b> down|up`, `pad=<i> axis=<a> value=<v>` (only after the axis has moved at least 0.25 since it was last logged) and `disconnected index=<i>`. A button already held when a pad first appears is logged as `down`.
    - `motion`: `events=<n> linear=<n> gravityOnly=<n> rate=<r|n/a>Hz range=<min..max|n/a>`, `DeviceMotionEvent unsupported`, `permission denied` and `permission error <err>`.
  - **`MotionReport`:**
    - `available` is true when at least one event carried finite `acceleration` or `accelerationIncludingGravity` data. API presence alone does not count; T2's `Features.deviceMotion` already reports that.
    - `accelRange` is `[min, max]` over every finite gravity-free acceleration component, to 2 decimals, or null if there were none.
    - `rateHz` is `(events − 1) · 1000 / (last − first arrival)`, using `performance.now()` and rounded to 0.1; it is null with fewer than 2 events.
    - `permission` is `'not-required'` when `requestPermission` is absent. A `'denied'` or `'error'` result returns 0 events without listening.
    - If there is no `DeviceMotionEvent` at all, the report has `available: false` and `permission: 'not-required'`.

**Cross-task premises:**
- T4: `EventLog.subscribe(fn)` delivers every later `push` synchronously and returns an unsubscribe function. `LogEntry` (`{ tMs, kind, detail }`) is exported. `LogKind` includes `'key'`, `'wheel'`, `'gamepad'`, `'visibility'`, `'motion'` and `'mediasession'`.
- T7 (same author): AudioProbe registers the Media Session handlers and pushes `log.push('mediasession', <action>)` with the bare action name. InputProbe never calls `setActionHandler`, which would replace AudioProbe's handler.
- T10:
  - Constructs `new InputProbe(log)` with the browser `window` and calls `attach()` once on load.
  - Reads `counts` for the UI and the summary.
  - Calls `probeMotion(5000)` directly from the **Probe motion (5 s)** click listener. A `requestPermission()` prompt therefore runs inside the user gesture: it is the first `await`, so it starts synchronously.
- T4 `summary.ts` imports the types `InputCounts` and `MotionReport`. Their keys contain no forbidden coordinate names, and `mediaSession` keys are Media Session action names.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing InputProbe test**

Create `tests/unit/inputs.test.ts`. It uses a fake window (a listener registry, a document, `getGamepads`, a manual rAF queue and an optional `DeviceMotionEvent`) and fake timers.

```ts
import { describe, it, expect, vi, beforeEach, afterEach } from 'vitest';
import { InputProbe } from '../../src/probe/inputs';
import { EventLog } from '../../src/probe/log';

type Listener = (e: unknown) => void;

class FakeTarget {
  readonly listeners = new Map<string, Set<Listener>>();
  readonly added: { type: string; options: unknown }[] = [];
  addEventListener(type: string, fn: Listener, options?: unknown): void {
    this.added.push({ type, options });
    if (!this.listeners.has(type)) this.listeners.set(type, new Set());
    this.listeners.get(type)?.add(fn);
  }
  removeEventListener(type: string, fn: Listener): void {
    this.listeners.get(type)?.delete(fn);
  }
  dispatch(type: string, event: object): void {
    for (const fn of this.listeners.get(type) ?? []) fn({ type, ...event });
  }
  count(type: string): number {
    return this.listeners.get(type)?.size ?? 0;
  }
}

class FakeDocument extends FakeTarget {
  visibilityState: DocumentVisibilityState = 'visible';
}

interface FakePad {
  index: number;
  id: string;
  mapping: string;
  buttons: { pressed: boolean; value: number }[];
  axes: number[];
}

class FakeWindow extends FakeTarget {
  readonly document = new FakeDocument();
  pads: (FakePad | null)[] = [];
  readonly navigator = { getGamepads: (): (FakePad | null)[] => this.pads };
  DeviceMotionEvent?: unknown;
  private frames = new Map<number, () => void>();
  private nextFrame = 1;
  requestAnimationFrame(cb: () => void): number {
    const id = this.nextFrame++;
    this.frames.set(id, cb);
    return id;
  }
  cancelAnimationFrame(id: number): void {
    this.frames.delete(id);
  }
  pendingFrames(): number {
    return this.frames.size;
  }
  /** Runs one animation frame. */
  frame(): void {
    const cbs = [...this.frames.values()];
    this.frames.clear();
    for (const cb of cbs) cb();
  }
}

function pad(over: Partial<FakePad> = {}): FakePad {
  return {
    index: 0,
    id: 'TESLA VIRTUAL GAMEPAD',
    mapping: 'standard',
    buttons: Array.from({ length: 8 }, () => ({ pressed: false, value: 0 })),
    axes: [0, 0],
    ...over,
  };
}

let win: FakeWindow;
let log: EventLog;
let probe: InputProbe;
const details = (kind: string): string[] => log.entries().filter((e) => e.kind === kind).map((e) => e.detail);

beforeEach(() => {
  vi.useFakeTimers();
  win = new FakeWindow();
  log = new EventLog();
  probe = new InputProbe(log, win as unknown as Window);
});

afterEach(() => {
  probe.detach();
  vi.useRealTimers();
});

describe('InputProbe.attach — keys, wheel, visibility', () => {
  it('logs keydown/keyup with key, code and keyCode; counts distinct keys on keydown', () => {
    probe.attach();
    win.dispatch('keydown', { key: 'MediaTrackNext', code: 'MediaTrackNext', keyCode: 176 });
    win.dispatch('keyup', { key: 'MediaTrackNext', code: 'MediaTrackNext', keyCode: 176 });
    win.dispatch('keydown', { key: 'MediaTrackNext', code: 'MediaTrackNext', keyCode: 176 });
    win.dispatch('keydown', { key: 'e', code: 'KeyE', keyCode: 69 });
    win.dispatch('keyup', { key: 'q', code: 'KeyQ', keyCode: 81 }); // keyup alone does not count
    expect(details('key')[0]).toBe('keydown key=MediaTrackNext code=MediaTrackNext keyCode=176');
    expect(details('key')[1]).toBe('keyup key=MediaTrackNext code=MediaTrackNext keyCode=176');
    expect(details('key')).toHaveLength(5);
    expect(probe.counts.keys).toEqual(['MediaTrackNext', 'e']);
  });

  it('falls back to code, then keyCode, when key is empty', () => {
    probe.attach();
    win.dispatch('keydown', { key: '', code: 'F13', keyCode: 124 });
    win.dispatch('keydown', { key: '', code: '', keyCode: 255 });
    expect(probe.counts.keys).toEqual(['F13', 'keyCode:255']);
  });

  it('logs wheel deltas and mode with a passive listener', () => {
    probe.attach();
    win.dispatch('wheel', { deltaX: 0, deltaY: -120.456, deltaMode: 0 });
    win.dispatch('wheel', { deltaX: 3, deltaY: 0, deltaMode: 1 });
    expect(probe.counts.wheel).toBe(2);
    expect(details('wheel')).toEqual(['dx=0 dy=-120.46 mode=0', 'dx=3 dy=0 mode=1']);
    expect(win.added.find((a) => a.type === 'wheel')?.options).toEqual({ passive: true });
  });

  it('counts visibility changes and logs the new state', () => {
    probe.attach();
    win.document.visibilityState = 'hidden';
    win.document.dispatch('visibilitychange', {});
    win.document.visibilityState = 'visible';
    win.document.dispatch('visibilitychange', {});
    expect(probe.counts.visibilityChanges).toBe(2);
    expect(details('visibility')).toEqual(['hidden', 'visible']);
  });

  it('counts Media Session actions that AudioProbe pushes into the shared log', () => {
    log.push('mediasession', 'play'); // before attach: not counted
    probe.attach();
    log.push('mediasession', 'nexttrack');
    log.push('mediasession', 'nexttrack');
    log.push('mediasession', 'previoustrack');
    log.push('audio', 'running');
    expect(probe.counts.mediaSession).toEqual({ nexttrack: 2, previoustrack: 1 });
  });

  it('attach() is idempotent: one listener per event', () => {
    probe.attach();
    probe.attach();
    expect(win.count('keydown')).toBe(1);
    expect(win.pendingFrames()).toBe(1);
    win.dispatch('keydown', { key: 'a', code: 'KeyA', keyCode: 65 });
    expect(details('key')).toHaveLength(1);
  });

  it('counts is a snapshot copy', () => {
    probe.attach();
    win.dispatch('keydown', { key: 'a', code: 'KeyA', keyCode: 65 });
    const c = probe.counts;
    c.keys.push('zzz');
    c.mediaSession.play = 9;
    expect(probe.counts.keys).toEqual(['a']);
    expect(probe.counts.mediaSession).toEqual({});
  });

  it('detach() removes every listener, the rAF loop and the log subscription', () => {
    probe.attach();
    probe.detach();
    win.dispatch('keydown', { key: 'a', code: 'KeyA', keyCode: 65 });
    win.dispatch('wheel', { deltaX: 0, deltaY: 1, deltaMode: 0 });
    win.document.dispatch('visibilitychange', {});
    log.push('mediasession', 'play');
    expect(probe.counts).toEqual({ mediaSession: {}, keys: [], wheel: 0, gamepads: [], visibilityChanges: 0 });
    expect(win.pendingFrames()).toBe(0);
    for (const t of ['keydown', 'keyup', 'wheel']) expect(win.count(t)).toBe(0);
    expect(win.document.count('visibilitychange')).toBe(0);
  });
});

describe('InputProbe gamepad polling', () => {
  it('logs a new pad, its button presses/releases and large axis moves, frame by frame', () => {
    probe.attach();
    win.frame(); // no pads yet
    const p = pad();
    p.buttons[5] = { pressed: true, value: 1 }; // the press that makes Chromium expose the pad
    win.pads = [p];
    win.frame();
    expect(details('gamepad')).toEqual([
      'connected index=0 id=TESLA VIRTUAL GAMEPAD mapping=standard buttons=8 axes=2',
      'pad=0 button=5 down',
    ]);
    p.buttons[5] = { pressed: false, value: 0 };
    p.axes[1] = 0.1; // jitter below 0.25: not logged
    win.frame();
    p.axes[1] = -0.8;
    win.frame();
    win.frame(); // unchanged: nothing new
    expect(details('gamepad').slice(2)).toEqual(['pad=0 button=5 up', 'pad=0 axis=1 value=-0.8']);
    expect(probe.counts.gamepads).toEqual(['TESLA VIRTUAL GAMEPAD']);
    expect(win.pendingFrames()).toBe(1); // loop keeps itself scheduled
  });

  it('logs disconnects and does not duplicate a reconnecting pad id', () => {
    probe.attach();
    win.pads = [pad()];
    win.frame();
    win.pads = [null];
    win.frame();
    win.pads = [pad()];
    win.frame();
    expect(details('gamepad').filter((d) => d.startsWith('disconnected'))).toEqual(['disconnected index=0']);
    expect(details('gamepad').filter((d) => d.startsWith('connected'))).toHaveLength(2);
    expect(probe.counts.gamepads).toEqual(['TESLA VIRTUAL GAMEPAD']);
  });

  it('tolerates getGamepads throwing', () => {
    probe.attach();
    win.navigator.getGamepads = () => {
      throw new Error('SecurityError');
    };
    expect(() => win.frame()).not.toThrow();
    expect(win.pendingFrames()).toBe(1);
  });
});

describe('InputProbe.probeMotion', () => {
  function motion(x: number | null, y: number | null, z: number | null, g: number | null = null): object {
    return {
      acceleration: x === null && y === null && z === null ? null : { x, y, z },
      accelerationIncludingGravity: g === null ? null : { x: 0, y: 0, z: g },
    };
  }

  it('reports unavailable when DeviceMotionEvent does not exist', async () => {
    await expect(probe.probeMotion(1000)).resolves.toEqual({
      available: false, permission: 'not-required', events: 0, rateHz: null, accelRange: null,
    });
  });

  it('listens for durationMs without a prompt, measuring rate and the acceleration range', async () => {
    win.DeviceMotionEvent = class {};
    const p = probe.probeMotion(1000);
    expect(win.count('devicemotion')).toBe(1);
    for (let i = 0; i <= 10; i++) {
      win.dispatch('devicemotion', motion(i === 3 ? -2.5 : 0.1, 0.2, i === 7 ? 3.25 : 0));
      await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(i < 10 ? 90 : 0);
    }
    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(1000);
    const r = await p;
    expect(r).toEqual({ available: true, permission: 'not-required', events: 11, rateHz: 11.1, accelRange: [-2.5, 3.25] });
    expect(win.count('devicemotion')).toBe(0);
    expect(details('motion').at(-1)).toBe('events=11 linear=11 gravityOnly=0 rate=11.1Hz range=-2.5..3.25');
  });

  it('requests permission when required and reports granted', async () => {
    const requestPermission = vi.fn(() => Promise.resolve('granted'));
    win.DeviceMotionEvent = Object.assign(class {}, { requestPermission });
    const p = probe.probeMotion(500);
    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(0);
    win.dispatch('devicemotion', motion(1, 1, 1));
    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(500);
    const r = await p;
    expect(requestPermission).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    expect(r.permission).toBe('granted');
    expect(r.events).toBe(1);
    expect(r.rateHz).toBeNull(); // one event: no interval
  });

  it('reports denied without listening', async () => {
    win.DeviceMotionEvent = Object.assign(class {}, { requestPermission: () => Promise.resolve('denied') });
    const r = await probe.probeMotion(500);
    expect(r).toEqual({ available: false, permission: 'denied', events: 0, rateHz: null, accelRange: null });
    expect(win.added.some((a) => a.type === 'devicemotion')).toBe(false);
  });

  it('reports error when requestPermission throws', async () => {
    win.DeviceMotionEvent = Object.assign(class {}, {
      requestPermission: () => Promise.reject(new Error('NotAllowedError')),
    });
    expect((await probe.probeMotion(500)).permission).toBe('error');
  });

  it('gravity-only events count as available but give no linear range', async () => {
    win.DeviceMotionEvent = class {};
    const p = probe.probeMotion(500);
    win.dispatch('devicemotion', motion(null, null, null, 9.81));
    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(500);
    const r = await p;
    expect(r.available).toBe(true);
    expect(r.accelRange).toBeNull();
  });

  it('events with all-null data (sensorless desktop) are not "available"', async () => {
    win.DeviceMotionEvent = class {};
    const p = probe.probeMotion(500);
    win.dispatch('devicemotion', motion(null, null, null, null));
    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(500);
    const r = await p;
    expect(r).toEqual({ available: false, permission: 'not-required', events: 1, rateHz: null, accelRange: null });
  });

  it('never subscribes to deviceorientation', async () => {
    win.DeviceMotionEvent = class {};
    probe.attach();
    const p = probe.probeMotion(100);
    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(100);
    await p;
    expect(win.added.map((a) => a.type)).not.toContain('deviceorientation');
    expect(win.added.map((a) => a.type)).not.toContain('deviceorientationabsolute');
  });
});
```
