- [ ] **Step 3: Implement InputProbe**

Create `src/probe/inputs.ts`:

```ts
import type { EventLog, LogEntry } from './log';
import { errText } from './log';

export interface InputCounts {
  mediaSession: Record<string, number>;
  keys: string[];
  wheel: number;
  gamepads: string[];
  visibilityChanges: number;
}

export interface MotionReport {
  available: boolean;
  permission: 'granted' | 'denied' | 'not-required' | 'error';
  events: number;
  rateHz: number | null;
  accelRange: [number, number] | null;
}

/** DeviceMotionEvent as exposed on window; requestPermission exists only where a prompt is required. */
type DeviceMotionCtor = (new (type: string) => Event) & { requestPermission?: () => Promise<string> };

const MAX_DISTINCT_KEYS = 64; // keeps the QR summary bounded
const AXIS_LOG_STEP = 0.25; // log an axis only when it moved this far since its last logged value

function round(v: number, digits: number): number {
  const f = 10 ** digits;
  return Math.round(v * f) / f;
}

function finite(v: number | null | undefined): v is number {
  return typeof v === 'number' && Number.isFinite(v);
}

/**
 * Logs every input path the car might deliver: keys (incl. MediaTrack*), wheel, visibility,
 * gamepads (rAF polling) and DeviceMotion. Media Session actions are registered by AudioProbe
 * (one handler per action) and counted here from the shared log.
 */
export class InputProbe {
  private attached = false;
  private rafId: number | null = null;
  private unsubscribe: (() => void) | null = null;
  private readonly mediaSession = new Map<string, number>();
  private readonly keys: string[] = [];
  private wheel = 0;
  private readonly gamepads: string[] = [];
  private visibilityChanges = 0;
  private readonly padIds = new Map<number, string>();
  private readonly padButtons = new Map<number, boolean[]>();
  private readonly padAxes = new Map<number, number[]>();

  constructor(
    private readonly log: EventLog,
    private readonly target: Window = window,
  ) {}

  get counts(): InputCounts {
    return {
      mediaSession: Object.fromEntries(this.mediaSession),
      keys: [...this.keys],
      wheel: this.wheel,
      gamepads: [...this.gamepads],
      visibilityChanges: this.visibilityChanges,
    };
  }

  /** keydown/keyup (key, code, keyCode), wheel (deltaX/Y, deltaMode), visibilitychange, gamepad polling via rAF. */
  attach(): void {
    if (this.attached) return;
    this.attached = true;
    const t = this.target;
    t.addEventListener('keydown', this.onKey);
    t.addEventListener('keyup', this.onKey);
    t.addEventListener('wheel', this.onWheel, { passive: true });
    t.document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', this.onVisibility);
    this.unsubscribe = this.log.subscribe(this.onLogEntry);
    this.rafId = t.requestAnimationFrame(this.onFrame);
  }

  detach(): void {
    if (!this.attached) return;
    this.attached = false;
    const t = this.target;
    t.removeEventListener('keydown', this.onKey);
    t.removeEventListener('keyup', this.onKey);
    t.removeEventListener('wheel', this.onWheel);
    t.document.removeEventListener('visibilitychange', this.onVisibility);
    this.unsubscribe?.();
    this.unsubscribe = null;
    if (this.rafId !== null) t.cancelAnimationFrame(this.rafId);
    this.rafId = null;
  }

  /** Listens to devicemotion for durationMs (requests permission if DeviceMotionEvent.requestPermission exists). Never uses deviceorientation. */
  async probeMotion(durationMs = 5000): Promise<MotionReport> {
    const ctor = (this.target as unknown as { DeviceMotionEvent?: DeviceMotionCtor }).DeviceMotionEvent;
    if (typeof ctor !== 'function') {
      this.log.push('motion', 'DeviceMotionEvent unsupported');
      return { available: false, permission: 'not-required', events: 0, rateHz: null, accelRange: null };
    }
    let permission: MotionReport['permission'] = 'not-required';
    if (typeof ctor.requestPermission === 'function') {
      try {
        // First await of the click handler's call chain: still inside the user gesture.
        permission = (await ctor.requestPermission()) === 'granted' ? 'granted' : 'denied';
      } catch (e) {
        this.log.push('motion', `permission error ${errText(e)}`);
        return { available: false, permission: 'error', events: 0, rateHz: null, accelRange: null };
      }
      if (permission === 'denied') {
        this.log.push('motion', 'permission denied');
        return { available: false, permission, events: 0, rateHz: null, accelRange: null };
      }
    }
    return this.listenMotion(Math.max(0, durationMs), permission);
  }

  private listenMotion(durationMs: number, permission: MotionReport['permission']): Promise<MotionReport> {
    return new Promise((resolve) => {
      let events = 0;
      let linear = 0; // events carrying gravity-free acceleration
      let gravityOnly = 0; // events carrying only accelerationIncludingGravity
      let firstMs = 0;
      let lastMs = 0;
      let min = Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
      let max = Number.NEGATIVE_INFINITY;
      const track = (v: number | null | undefined): boolean => {
        if (!finite(v)) return false;
        if (v < min) min = v;
        if (v > max) max = v;
        return true;
      };
      const onMotion = (e: DeviceMotionEvent): void => {
        const now = performance.now();
        if (events === 0) firstMs = now;
        lastMs = now;
        events++;
        const a = e.acceleration;
        let hasLinear = false;
        if (a !== null) {
          const x = track(a.x); // evaluate all three: each widens the range
          const y = track(a.y);
          const z = track(a.z);
          hasLinear = x || y || z;
        }
        if (hasLinear) linear++;
        else {
          const g = e.accelerationIncludingGravity;
          if (g !== null && (finite(g.x) || finite(g.y) || finite(g.z))) gravityOnly++;
        }
      };
      this.target.addEventListener('devicemotion', onMotion);
      setTimeout(() => {
        this.target.removeEventListener('devicemotion', onMotion);
        const rateHz = events >= 2 && lastMs > firstMs ? round(((events - 1) * 1000) / (lastMs - firstMs), 1) : null;
        const accelRange: [number, number] | null = linear > 0 ? [round(min, 2), round(max, 2)] : null;
        const report: MotionReport = { available: linear + gravityOnly > 0, permission, events, rateHz, accelRange };
        this.log.push(
          'motion',
          `events=${events} linear=${linear} gravityOnly=${gravityOnly} rate=${rateHz ?? 'n/a'}Hz range=${accelRange === null ? 'n/a' : accelRange.join('..')}`,
        );
        resolve(report);
      }, durationMs);
    });
  }

  private readonly onKey = (e: KeyboardEvent): void => {
    this.log.push('key', `${e.type} key=${e.key} code=${e.code} keyCode=${e.keyCode}`);
    const id = e.key || e.code || `keyCode:${e.keyCode}`;
    if (e.type === 'keydown' && !this.keys.includes(id) && this.keys.length < MAX_DISTINCT_KEYS) this.keys.push(id);
  };

  private readonly onWheel = (e: WheelEvent): void => {
    this.wheel++;
    this.log.push('wheel', `dx=${round(e.deltaX, 2)} dy=${round(e.deltaY, 2)} mode=${e.deltaMode}`);
  };

  private readonly onVisibility = (): void => {
    this.visibilityChanges++;
    this.log.push('visibility', this.target.document.visibilityState);
  };

  private readonly onLogEntry = (e: LogEntry): void => {
    if (e.kind === 'mediasession') this.mediaSession.set(e.detail, (this.mediaSession.get(e.detail) ?? 0) + 1);
  };

  private readonly onFrame = (): void => {
    this.rafId = null;
    if (!this.attached) return;
    this.scanGamepads();
    this.rafId = this.target.requestAnimationFrame(this.onFrame);
  };

  private scanGamepads(): void {
    const nav = this.target.navigator;
    if (typeof nav.getGamepads !== 'function') return;
    let pads: (Gamepad | null)[];
    try {
      pads = nav.getGamepads();
    } catch {
      return; // e.g. blocked by Permissions-Policy
    }
    const seen = new Set<number>();
    for (const pad of pads) {
      if (pad === null) continue;
      seen.add(pad.index);
      if (this.padIds.get(pad.index) !== pad.id) {
        this.padIds.set(pad.index, pad.id);
        this.padButtons.set(pad.index, pad.buttons.map(() => false));
        this.padAxes.set(pad.index, pad.axes.map(() => 0));
        if (!this.gamepads.includes(pad.id)) this.gamepads.push(pad.id);
        this.log.push(
          'gamepad',
          `connected index=${pad.index} id=${pad.id} mapping=${pad.mapping || 'none'} buttons=${pad.buttons.length} axes=${pad.axes.length}`,
        );
      }
      this.diffPad(pad);
    }
    for (const index of [...this.padIds.keys()]) {
      if (seen.has(index)) continue;
      this.log.push('gamepad', `disconnected index=${index}`);
      this.padIds.delete(index);
      this.padButtons.delete(index);
      this.padAxes.delete(index);
    }
  }

  private diffPad(pad: Gamepad): void {
    const buttons = this.padButtons.get(pad.index);
    const axes = this.padAxes.get(pad.index);
    if (buttons === undefined || axes === undefined) return;
    pad.buttons.forEach((b, i) => {
      if (b.pressed === (buttons[i] ?? false)) return;
      buttons[i] = b.pressed;
      this.log.push('gamepad', `pad=${pad.index} button=${i} ${b.pressed ? 'down' : 'up'}`);
    });
    pad.axes.forEach((v, i) => {
      if (Math.abs(v - (axes[i] ?? 0)) < AXIS_LOG_STEP) return;
      axes[i] = v;
      this.log.push('gamepad', `pad=${pad.index} axis=${i} value=${round(v, 2)}`);
    });
  }
}
```

### Task 9: Storage probe, probe service worker and the anchor WAV

**Files:**
- Create: `src/probe/storage-probe.ts`
- Create: `public/probe/sw.js`
- Create: `tools/make-anchor-wav.ts`
- Create: `public/probe/anchor.wav` (generated by `npm run gen:anchor`, 160 044 bytes, committed with the phase)
- Test: `tests/unit/storage-probe.test.ts`
- Test: `tests/unit/make-anchor-wav.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes:
  - `src/platform/storage.ts` (T2): `storageAvailable(): boolean`, `getJSON<T>(key: string, fallback: T): T`, `setJSON(key: string, value: unknown): boolean` (keys passed un-prefixed; T2 adds `tesbox.v1.`).
  - `src/probe/log.ts` (T4 Part A): `errText(e: unknown): string` — `"<Name>: <message>"`, or just the message for a plain `Error`; `withTimeout<T>(p: Promise<T>, ms: number, label: string): Promise<T>` — rejects with `<label> timed out after <ms> ms`, otherwise settles like `p`.
  - Browser globals: `caches: CacheStorage`, `navigator.serviceWorker: ServiceWorkerContainer`, `Response`.
  - Node (tool only): `node:fs`, `node:path`, `node:url`.
- Produces:
  - `src/probe/storage-probe.ts`:
    - `export interface StorageReport { localStorage: boolean; visits: number; firstSeen: string | null; lastSeen: string | null; cacheStorage: boolean; cachePersisted: boolean | null; cacheAgeS: number | null; swSupported: boolean; swRegistered: boolean; swControlled: boolean; error?: string }` (contract shape).
    - `export function runStorageProbe(nowIso?: string): Promise<StorageReport>` — **never rejects**. Semantics: `visits` counts this visit (0 if localStorage is unusable); `firstSeen` = first recorded visit (this one on the first run); `lastSeen` = the **previous** visit (null on the first); `cachePersisted` = an entry from an earlier visit was found (null when Cache Storage is unusable); `cacheAgeS` = its age in seconds at 0.1 s resolution (null if none or unreadable); `swControlled` = the page load was already controlled (the worker survived from an earlier visit); `error` = `"; "`-joined `"<part>: <message>"` entries (`localStorage: unavailable`, `cacheStorage: unavailable`, `serviceWorker: unsupported`, thrown messages, `… timed out after 5000 ms`), absent when everything worked. Every awaited browser call is capped at 5 s.
    - Storage keys: `'probe.visits'` (number), `'probe.firstSeen'`, `'probe.lastSeen'` (ISO strings). Cache: `caches.open('tesbox-probe')` → `match('__persist')` → `put('__persist', new Response(JSON.stringify({ ts: <epoch ms> })))`. SW: `navigator.serviceWorker.register('./sw.js', { scope: './' })`.
  - `public/probe/sw.js`: served at `/probe/sw.js`, scope `/probe/`; `install` → `skipWaiting()`, `activate` → `clients.claim()`; **no fetch handler**.
  - `tools/make-anchor-wav.ts`: `export interface AnchorOptions { seconds: number; sampleRate: number; rmsDbfs: number; seed: number }`; `export const ANCHOR_DEFAULTS: AnchorOptions` (`10`, `8000`, `-60`, `0x5eed7e5b`); `export const ANCHOR_PATH: string` (absolute path of `public/probe/anchor.wav`); `export function anchorSamples(opts?: AnchorOptions): Int16Array`; `export function encodeWav16Mono(samples: Int16Array, sampleRate: number): Uint8Array`; `export function makeAnchorWav(opts?: AnchorOptions): Uint8Array`. Writes the file only when run directly (`npm run gen:anchor`).
  - `public/probe/anchor.wav` → URL `/probe/anchor.wav` (T10 passes it to `new AudioProbe(log, workletUrl, '/probe/anchor.wav')`): RIFF/WAVE PCM, 8 kHz, mono, 16-bit, 10.0 s, deterministic TPDF noise at −60.0 dBFS RMS (dBFS = 20·log10(rms/32768)), peak ≈ −52 dBFS, sha256 `2bd54ee0de186db2c1ecfc2e565ce1648c468aaf538aa3006314c7591b04d1cc`.

**Cross-task premises:** (open each named file and confirm before coding)
- T1 `package.json` has the script `"gen:anchor": "tsx tools/make-anchor-wav.ts"` and `tsx` in devDependencies; `tsconfig.json` includes `tools` and `tests` with `types: ["vite/client", "node"]`; `vite.config.ts` has `test: { include: ['tests/unit/**/*.test.ts'], environment: 'node' }` and does not override Vite's default `publicDir` (`public/`), so `public/probe/*` is served at `/probe/*` by dev/preview and copied to `dist/probe/`.
- T1 `eslint.config.js` ignores `public/` — `sw.js` is plain browser JavaScript and is not linted or type-checked.

- T2 `src/platform/storage.ts` exports `storageAvailable`, `getJSON`, `setJSON` (and `remove`) with the contract signatures and adds the `tesbox.v1.` prefix itself. The unit test replaces this module with `vi.mock`, so only the export names and signatures matter here.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing storage-probe test (includes RF5 and the sw.js behaviour test)**

Create `tests/unit/storage-probe.test.ts`. `src/platform/storage.ts` is replaced by an in-memory fake with the same contract, `caches`/`navigator` are stubbed per test, and the RF5 cases cover unavailable *and* throwing localStorage / Cache Storage (including a `caches` getter that throws, as Chromium does in sandboxed contexts). The `sw.js` test evaluates the real file against a fake `self`.

```ts
import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';

// In-memory stand-in for src/platform/storage.ts (same contract: prefix handled inside, try/catch).
const mem = vi.hoisted(() => ({
  store: new Map<string, string>(),
  available: true,
  throwOnRead: false,
  failWrites: false,
}));

vi.mock('../../src/platform/storage', () => ({
  storageAvailable: (): boolean => mem.available,
  getJSON<T>(key: string, fallback: T): T {
    if (mem.throwOnRead) throw new Error('boom');
    const raw = mem.store.get(key);
    return raw === undefined ? fallback : (JSON.parse(raw) as T);
  },
  setJSON(key: string, value: unknown): boolean {
    if (mem.failWrites) return false;
    mem.store.set(key, JSON.stringify(value));
    return true;
  },
  remove(key: string): void {
    mem.store.delete(key);
  },
}));

import { runStorageProbe } from '../../src/probe/storage-probe';

interface FakeCacheOptions {
  failOpen?: boolean;
  failPut?: boolean;
  hangOpen?: boolean;
}

function installCaches(opts: FakeCacheOptions = {}) {
  const store = new Map<string, string>();
  const opened: string[] = [];
  const cache = {
    match: async (key: string): Promise<Response | undefined> => {
      const body = store.get(key);
      return body === undefined ? undefined : new Response(body);
    },
    put: async (key: string, res: Response): Promise<void> => {
      if (opts.failPut) throw new DOMException('quota', 'QuotaExceededError');
      store.set(key, await res.text());
    },
  };
  const caches = {
    open: (name: string): Promise<typeof cache> => {
      opened.push(name);
      if (opts.hangOpen) return new Promise(() => undefined);
      if (opts.failOpen) return Promise.reject(new DOMException('denied', 'SecurityError'));
      return Promise.resolve(cache);
    },
  };
  vi.stubGlobal('caches', caches);
  return { store, opened };
}

function installNavigator(sw: { controller?: object | null; register?: (url: string, o: object) => Promise<unknown> } | null) {
  const register = vi.fn(sw?.register ?? (async () => ({ scope: '/probe/' })));
  const nav = sw === null ? { userAgent: 'test' } : { userAgent: 'test', serviceWorker: { controller: sw.controller ?? null, register } };
  vi.stubGlobal('navigator', nav);
  return { register };
}

const T0 = '2026-10-06T10:00:00.000Z';
const T1 = '2026-10-06T10:01:30.000Z'; // +90 s
const T2 = '2026-10-07T08:00:00.000Z';

beforeEach(() => {
  mem.store.clear();
  mem.available = true;
  mem.throwOnRead = false;
  mem.failWrites = false;
});

afterEach(() => {
  vi.unstubAllGlobals();
  vi.useRealTimers();
});

describe('runStorageProbe', () => {
  it('first visit: counts 1, writes the cache marker and registers ./sw.js with scope ./', async () => {
    const { opened, store } = installCaches();
    const { register } = installNavigator({});
    const r = await runStorageProbe(T0);
    expect(r).toEqual({
      localStorage: true,
      visits: 1,
      firstSeen: T0,
      lastSeen: null,
      cacheStorage: true,
      cachePersisted: false,
      cacheAgeS: null,
      swSupported: true,
      swRegistered: true,
      swControlled: false,
    });
    expect(opened).toEqual(['tesbox-probe']);
    expect(JSON.parse(store.get('__persist') ?? 'null')).toEqual({ ts: Date.parse(T0) });
    expect(register).toHaveBeenCalledWith('./sw.js', { scope: './' });
    expect(mem.store.get('probe.visits')).toBe('1');
    expect(mem.store.get('probe.lastSeen')).toBe(JSON.stringify(T0));
  });

  it('later visits: counter grows, firstSeen sticks, lastSeen is the previous visit, cache age measured', async () => {
    installCaches();
    installNavigator({});
    await runStorageProbe(T0);
    const second = await runStorageProbe(T1);
    expect(second.visits).toBe(2);
    expect(second.firstSeen).toBe(T0);
    expect(second.lastSeen).toBe(T0);
    expect(second.cachePersisted).toBe(true);
    expect(second.cacheAgeS).toBe(90);
    const third = await runStorageProbe(T2);
    expect(third.visits).toBe(3);
    expect(third.firstSeen).toBe(T0);
    expect(third.lastSeen).toBe(T1);
    expect(third.cacheAgeS).toBe((Date.parse(T2) - Date.parse(T1)) / 1000);
    expect(third.error).toBeUndefined();
  });

  it('reports swControlled when the page load is already controlled', async () => {
    installCaches();
    installNavigator({ controller: { state: 'activated' } });
    const r = await runStorageProbe(T0);
    expect(r.swControlled).toBe(true);
  });

  it('treats corrupt stored values as absent', async () => {
    installCaches();
    installNavigator({});
    mem.store.set('probe.visits', JSON.stringify('lots'));
    mem.store.set('probe.firstSeen', JSON.stringify(5));
    mem.store.set('probe.lastSeen', JSON.stringify({}));
    const r = await runStorageProbe(T0);
    expect(r.visits).toBe(1);
    expect(r.firstSeen).toBe(T0);
    expect(r.lastSeen).toBeNull();
  });

  it('a corrupt cache entry still counts as persisted, with unknown age', async () => {
    const { store } = installCaches();
    installNavigator({});
    store.set('__persist', 'not json');
    const r = await runStorageProbe(T0);
    expect(r.cachePersisted).toBe(true);
    expect(r.cacheAgeS).toBeNull();
    expect(r.cacheStorage).toBe(true);
  });

  // RF5: unavailable or throwing storage → false flags + error string, never a rejection.
  it('RF5: localStorage unavailable → localStorage false, error set, other probes still run', async () => {
    installCaches();
    installNavigator({});
    mem.available = false;
    const r = await runStorageProbe(T0);
    expect(r.localStorage).toBe(false);
    expect(r.visits).toBe(0);
    expect(r.firstSeen).toBeNull();
    expect(r.error).toContain('localStorage: unavailable');
    expect(r.cacheStorage).toBe(true);
    expect(r.swRegistered).toBe(true);
  });

  it('RF5: localStorage throwing → resolves with localStorage false and the error message', async () => {
    installCaches();
    installNavigator({});
    mem.throwOnRead = true;
    const r = await runStorageProbe(T0);
    expect(r.localStorage).toBe(false);
    expect(r.error).toContain('localStorage: boom');
  });

  it('RF5: localStorage writes failing → localStorage false', async () => {
    installCaches();
    installNavigator({});
    mem.failWrites = true;
    const r = await runStorageProbe(T0);
    expect(r.localStorage).toBe(false);
    expect(r.error).toContain('localStorage: write failed');
  });

  it('RF5: Cache Storage missing → cacheStorage false, cachePersisted null', async () => {
    installNavigator({});
    const r = await runStorageProbe(T0);
    expect(r.cacheStorage).toBe(false);
    expect(r.cachePersisted).toBeNull();
    expect(r.cacheAgeS).toBeNull();
    expect(r.error).toContain('cacheStorage: unavailable');
  });

  it('RF5: caches.open rejecting → resolves with cacheStorage false and the DOMException name', async () => {
    installCaches({ failOpen: true });
    installNavigator({});
    const r = await runStorageProbe(T0);
    expect(r.cacheStorage).toBe(false);
    expect(r.cachePersisted).toBeNull();
    expect(r.error).toContain('cacheStorage: SecurityError: denied');
  });

  it('RF5: reading `caches` throwing (sandboxed context) → resolves', async () => {
    installNavigator({});
    Object.defineProperty(globalThis, 'caches', {
      configurable: true,
      get() {
        throw new DOMException('Cache storage is disabled', 'SecurityError');
      },
    });
    try {
      const r = await runStorageProbe(T0);
      expect(r.cacheStorage).toBe(false);
      expect(r.error).toContain('cacheStorage: SecurityError');
    } finally {
      delete (globalThis as { caches?: unknown }).caches;
    }
  });

  it('RF5: cache.put failing → cacheStorage false but the read result is kept', async () => {
    installCaches({ failPut: true });
    installNavigator({});
    const r = await runStorageProbe(T0);
    expect(r.cacheStorage).toBe(false);
    expect(r.cachePersisted).toBe(false);
    expect(r.error).toContain('QuotaExceededError');
  });

  it('a hung caches.open times out after 5 s instead of hanging the probe', async () => {
    vi.useFakeTimers();
    installCaches({ hangOpen: true });
    installNavigator({});
    const pending = runStorageProbe(T0);
    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(5000);
    const r = await pending;
    expect(r.cacheStorage).toBe(false);
    expect(r.error).toContain('caches.open timed out after 5000 ms');
    expect(r.swRegistered).toBe(true);
  });

  it('service worker unsupported → swSupported false with an error entry', async () => {
    installCaches();
    installNavigator(null);
    const r = await runStorageProbe(T0);
    expect(r.swSupported).toBe(false);
    expect(r.swRegistered).toBe(false);
    expect(r.error).toContain('serviceWorker: unsupported');
  });

  it('service worker registration rejecting → swRegistered false, message kept', async () => {
    installCaches();
    installNavigator({ register: () => Promise.reject(new TypeError('bad MIME type')) });
    const r = await runStorageProbe(T0);
    expect(r.swSupported).toBe(true);
    expect(r.swRegistered).toBe(false);
    expect(r.error).toContain('serviceWorker: TypeError: bad MIME type');
  });

  it('RF5: everything broken at once still resolves (never rejects)', async () => {
    mem.throwOnRead = true;
    installCaches({ failOpen: true });
    installNavigator({ register: () => Promise.reject(new Error('nope')) });
    await expect(runStorageProbe(T0)).resolves.toMatchObject({
      localStorage: false,
      cacheStorage: false,
      swRegistered: false,
    });
  });
});

describe('public/probe/sw.js', () => {
  type Listener = (event: { waitUntil(p: Promise<unknown>): void }) => void;

  it('skips waiting on install, claims clients on activate, and never intercepts fetches', async () => {
    const src = readFileSync(new URL('../../public/probe/sw.js', import.meta.url), 'utf8');
    const listeners = new Map<string, Listener>();
    const self = {
      addEventListener: (type: string, fn: Listener) => listeners.set(type, fn),
      skipWaiting: vi.fn(() => Promise.resolve()),
      clients: { claim: vi.fn(() => Promise.resolve()) },
    };
    new Function('self', src)(self);
    expect([...listeners.keys()].sort()).toEqual(['activate', 'install']);
    const waited: Promise<unknown>[] = [];
    const event = { waitUntil: (p: Promise<unknown>) => waited.push(p) };
    listeners.get('install')?.(event);
    listeners.get('activate')?.(event);
    await Promise.all(waited);
    expect(self.skipWaiting).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    expect(self.clients.claim).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    expect(waited).toHaveLength(2);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `src/probe/storage-probe.ts`**

Each part (localStorage, Cache Storage, service worker) runs in its own `try/catch`, so one failing API never hides the others and the function cannot reject. `withTimeout` (T4's shared helper in `log.ts`, called with `STEP_TIMEOUT_MS`) caps every awaited browser call at 5 s (a hung `caches.open` must not hang the probe page).

```ts
/**
 * Storage persistence probe (spec §12, §16 Q8): localStorage visit counter, Cache Storage
 * round-trip and Service Worker registration.
 *
 * runStorageProbe() NEVER rejects: every unavailable API or thrown error becomes a false flag
 * plus a message in `error` ("; "-joined, one entry per failing part).
 */
import { getJSON, setJSON, storageAvailable } from '../platform/storage';
import { errText, withTimeout } from './log';

export interface StorageReport {
  localStorage: boolean;
  /** Visits including this one (0 when localStorage is unusable). */
  visits: number;
  /** ISO time of the first recorded visit (this visit on first run). */
  firstSeen: string | null;
  /** ISO time of the PREVIOUS visit (null on the first visit). */
  lastSeen: string | null;
  cacheStorage: boolean;
  /** true = an entry written by an earlier visit was found; null = Cache Storage unusable. */
  cachePersisted: boolean | null;
  /** Age of that earlier entry in seconds (0.1 s resolution), null if none/unreadable. */
  cacheAgeS: number | null;
  swSupported: boolean;
  swRegistered: boolean;
  /** This page load was already controlled by a service worker (i.e. it survived from a previous visit). */
  swControlled: boolean;
  error?: string;
}

const VISITS_KEY = 'probe.visits';
const FIRST_SEEN_KEY = 'probe.firstSeen';
const LAST_SEEN_KEY = 'probe.lastSeen';
const CACHE_NAME = 'tesbox-probe';
const PERSIST_KEY = '__persist';
const STEP_TIMEOUT_MS = 5000;

function isCount(v: unknown): v is number {
  return typeof v === 'number' && Number.isInteger(v) && v >= 0;
}

function probeLocalStorage(r: StorageReport, nowIso: string, errors: string[]): void {
  if (!storageAvailable()) {
    errors.push('localStorage: unavailable');
    return;
  }
  const prevVisits = getJSON<unknown>(VISITS_KEY, 0);
  const prevFirst = getJSON<unknown>(FIRST_SEEN_KEY, null);
  const prevLast = getJSON<unknown>(LAST_SEEN_KEY, null);
  r.visits = (isCount(prevVisits) ? prevVisits : 0) + 1;
  r.firstSeen = typeof prevFirst === 'string' ? prevFirst : nowIso;
  r.lastSeen = typeof prevLast === 'string' ? prevLast : null;
  const written =
    setJSON(VISITS_KEY, r.visits) && setJSON(FIRST_SEEN_KEY, r.firstSeen) && setJSON(LAST_SEEN_KEY, nowIso);
  if (!written) {
    errors.push('localStorage: write failed');
    return;
  }
  r.localStorage = true;
}

async function readAgeS(res: Response, nowMs: number): Promise<number | null> {
  try {
    const body: unknown = await res.json();
    if (typeof body !== 'object' || body === null || !('ts' in body)) return null;
    const ts = (body as { ts: unknown }).ts;
    if (typeof ts !== 'number' || !Number.isFinite(ts) || !Number.isFinite(nowMs)) return null;
    return Math.round((nowMs - ts) / 100) / 10;
  } catch {
    return null; // corrupt entry: it still persisted, but its age is unknown
  }
}

async function probeCacheStorage(r: StorageReport, nowIso: string, errors: string[]): Promise<void> {
  // Reading `caches` itself throws SecurityError in sandboxed/opaque-origin contexts.
  const cacheStorage = (globalThis as { caches?: CacheStorage }).caches;
  if (cacheStorage === undefined) {
    errors.push('cacheStorage: unavailable');
    return;
  }
  const nowMs = Date.parse(nowIso);
  const cache = await withTimeout(cacheStorage.open(CACHE_NAME), STEP_TIMEOUT_MS, 'caches.open');
  const previous = await withTimeout(cache.match(PERSIST_KEY), STEP_TIMEOUT_MS, 'cache.match');
  r.cachePersisted = previous !== undefined;
  r.cacheAgeS = previous === undefined ? null : await readAgeS(previous, nowMs);
  const body = JSON.stringify({ ts: nowMs });
  await withTimeout(
    cache.put(PERSIST_KEY, new Response(body, { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })),
    STEP_TIMEOUT_MS,
    'cache.put',
  );
  r.cacheStorage = true;
}

async function probeServiceWorker(r: StorageReport, errors: string[]): Promise<void> {
  const nav = (globalThis as { navigator?: Navigator }).navigator;
  const container: ServiceWorkerContainer | undefined = nav?.serviceWorker;
  if (!container) {
    errors.push('serviceWorker: unsupported');
    return;
  }
  r.swSupported = true;
  r.swControlled = Boolean(container.controller);
  // Relative to the probe page (/probe/): script /probe/sw.js, scope /probe/.
  await withTimeout(container.register('./sw.js', { scope: './' }), STEP_TIMEOUT_MS, 'serviceWorker.register');
  r.swRegistered = true;
}

export async function runStorageProbe(nowIso: string = new Date().toISOString()): Promise<StorageReport> {
  const report: StorageReport = {
    localStorage: false,
    visits: 0,
    firstSeen: null,
    lastSeen: null,
    cacheStorage: false,
    cachePersisted: null,
    cacheAgeS: null,
    swSupported: false,
    swRegistered: false,
    swControlled: false,
  };
  const errors: string[] = [];
  try {
    probeLocalStorage(report, nowIso, errors);
  } catch (e) {
    report.localStorage = false;
    errors.push(`localStorage: ${errText(e)}`);
  }
  try {
    await probeCacheStorage(report, nowIso, errors);
  } catch (e) {
    report.cacheStorage = false;
    errors.push(`cacheStorage: ${errText(e)}`);
  }
  try {
    await probeServiceWorker(report, errors);
  } catch (e) {
    report.swRegistered = false;
    errors.push(`serviceWorker: ${errText(e)}`);
  }
  if (errors.length > 0) report.error = errors.join('; ');
  return report;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Create `public/probe/sw.js`**

```js
/* Tesbox probe service worker (served at /probe/sw.js, scope /probe/).
 *
 * Deliberately minimal: it exists only so the probe can test whether a service worker can be
 * registered in the car and whether control survives drives and sleep (spec §16 Q8). It has no
 * fetch handler, so it can never serve a stale probe page.
 */
self.addEventListener('install', (event) => {
  event.waitUntil(self.skipWaiting());
});

self.addEventListener('activate', (event) => {
  event.waitUntil(self.clients.claim());
});
```

- [ ] **Step 7: Write the failing anchor-WAV test**

Create `tests/unit/make-anchor-wav.test.ts`. The last test pins the committed `public/probe/anchor.wav` to the generator output, so the binary can never drift from its source.

```ts
import { existsSync, readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { ANCHOR_DEFAULTS, ANCHOR_PATH, anchorSamples, encodeWav16Mono, makeAnchorWav } from '../../tools/make-anchor-wav';

function ascii(bytes: Uint8Array, offset: number, length: number): string {
  return String.fromCharCode(...bytes.subarray(offset, offset + length));
}

function stats(s: Int16Array): { rmsDbfs: number; peakDbfs: number; mean: number } {
  let sumSq = 0;
  let peak = 0;
  let sum = 0;
  for (const v of s) {
    sumSq += v * v;
    sum += v;
    peak = Math.max(peak, Math.abs(v));
  }
  return {
    rmsDbfs: 20 * Math.log10(Math.sqrt(sumSq / s.length) / 32768),
    peakDbfs: 20 * Math.log10(peak / 32768),
    mean: sum / s.length,
  };
}

describe('make-anchor-wav', () => {
  it('writes a canonical PCM header: 8 kHz, mono, 16-bit, 10 s', () => {
    const wav = makeAnchorWav();
    const dv = new DataView(wav.buffer, wav.byteOffset, wav.byteLength);
    expect(wav.length).toBe(44 + 10 * 8000 * 2);
    expect(ascii(wav, 0, 4)).toBe('RIFF');
    expect(dv.getUint32(4, true)).toBe(wav.length - 8);
    expect(ascii(wav, 8, 4)).toBe('WAVE');
    expect(ascii(wav, 12, 4)).toBe('fmt ');
    expect(dv.getUint32(16, true)).toBe(16);
    expect(dv.getUint16(20, true)).toBe(1);
    expect(dv.getUint16(22, true)).toBe(1);
    expect(dv.getUint32(24, true)).toBe(8000);
    expect(dv.getUint32(28, true)).toBe(16000);
    expect(dv.getUint16(32, true)).toBe(2);
    expect(dv.getUint16(34, true)).toBe(16);
    expect(ascii(wav, 36, 4)).toBe('data');
    expect(dv.getUint32(40, true)).toBe(160000);
  });

  it('is longer than 5 s (spec §6.4 anchor requirement)', () => {
    const s = anchorSamples();
    expect(s.length / ANCHOR_DEFAULTS.sampleRate).toBe(10);
  });

  it('is low-level noise at −60 dBFS RMS (±0.5 dB), peaks below −40 dBFS, no DC', () => {
    const s = stats(anchorSamples());
    expect(Math.abs(s.rmsDbfs + 60)).toBeLessThan(0.5);
    expect(s.peakDbfs).toBeLessThan(-40);
    expect(Math.abs(s.mean)).toBeLessThan(0.5);
  });

  it('is not digital silence and not a constant pattern', () => {
    const s = anchorSamples();
    expect(new Set(s).size).toBeGreaterThan(50);
  });

  it('is deterministic: identical bytes on every call, different with another seed', () => {
    const a = Buffer.from(makeAnchorWav());
    expect(a.equals(Buffer.from(makeAnchorWav()))).toBe(true);
    expect(a.equals(Buffer.from(makeAnchorWav({ ...ANCHOR_DEFAULTS, seed: 1 })))).toBe(false);
  });

  it('encodes sample values little-endian after the header', () => {
    const wav = encodeWav16Mono(Int16Array.from([1, -2, 32767, -32768]), 8000);
    const dv = new DataView(wav.buffer);
    expect([dv.getInt16(44, true), dv.getInt16(46, true), dv.getInt16(48, true), dv.getInt16(50, true)]).toEqual([
      1, -2, 32767, -32768,
    ]);
  });

  it('the committed public/probe/anchor.wav equals the generator output (run npm run gen:anchor)', () => {
    expect(existsSync(ANCHOR_PATH)).toBe(true);
    expect(readFileSync(ANCHOR_PATH).equals(Buffer.from(makeAnchorWav()))).toBe(true);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 9: Implement `tools/make-anchor-wav.ts`**

Pure functions plus a guarded `main()` that runs only when the file is executed directly (`process.argv[1]` resolves to this file), so importing it from the test never writes anything. xorshift32 + TPDF noise; the mean is removed and the result scaled to exactly −60 dBFS RMS before rounding to 16-bit.

```ts
/**
 * Generates public/probe/anchor.wav for the "direct + anchor" output route (spec §6.4): a looping,
 * near-silent FILE source > 5 s long that keeps the browser's media session alive.
 *
 * Format: RIFF/WAVE, PCM, 8 kHz, mono, 16-bit, 10 s. Content: deterministic triangular (TPDF)
 * noise, DC removed, scaled to −60 dBFS RMS, where dBFS = 20·log10(rms / 32768).
 * Noise (not digital silence) so that a player which ignores all-zero audio still treats it as
 * audible. PRNG: xorshift32 (Marsaglia 2003, "Xorshift RNGs", J. Stat. Softw. 8(14)).
 *
 * Run: npm run gen:anchor   (the committed file must equal this output; a unit test checks it)
 */
import { mkdirSync, writeFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { dirname, resolve } from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';

export interface AnchorOptions {
  seconds: number;
  sampleRate: number;
  rmsDbfs: number;
  seed: number;
}

export const ANCHOR_DEFAULTS: AnchorOptions = { seconds: 10, sampleRate: 8000, rmsDbfs: -60, seed: 0x5eed7e5b };

export const ANCHOR_PATH = resolve(dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), '../public/probe/anchor.wav');

/** xorshift32 → uniform in [0, 1). Seed 0 is replaced by 1 (0 is a fixed point). */
function xorshift32(seed: number): () => number {
  let s = seed >>> 0 || 1;
  return () => {
    s = (s ^ (s << 13)) >>> 0;
    s = (s ^ (s >>> 17)) >>> 0;
    s = (s ^ (s << 5)) >>> 0;
    return s / 4294967296;
  };
}

/** Deterministic 16-bit noise samples at exactly `rmsDbfs` (before integer rounding). */
export function anchorSamples(opts: AnchorOptions = ANCHOR_DEFAULTS): Int16Array {
  const n = Math.round(opts.seconds * opts.sampleRate);
  const rnd = xorshift32(opts.seed);
  const x = new Float64Array(n);
  let mean = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
    const v = rnd() + rnd() - 1; // triangular on (−1, 1)
    x[i] = v;
    mean += v;
  }
  mean = n > 0 ? mean / n : 0;
  let sumSq = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
    const v = (x[i] ?? 0) - mean;
    x[i] = v;
    sumSq += v * v;
  }
  const rms = n > 0 ? Math.sqrt(sumSq / n) : 0;
  const gain = rms > 0 ? (Math.pow(10, opts.rmsDbfs / 20) * 32768) / rms : 0;
  const out = new Int16Array(n);
  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
    out[i] = Math.max(-32768, Math.min(32767, Math.round((x[i] ?? 0) * gain)));
  }
  return out;
}

/** Canonical 44-byte-header PCM WAV (little-endian), mono 16-bit. */
export function encodeWav16Mono(samples: Int16Array, sampleRate: number): Uint8Array {
  const dataBytes = samples.length * 2;
  const bytes = new Uint8Array(44 + dataBytes);
  const dv = new DataView(bytes.buffer);
  const ascii = (offset: number, text: string): void => {
    for (let i = 0; i < text.length; i++) dv.setUint8(offset + i, text.charCodeAt(i));
  };
  ascii(0, 'RIFF');
  dv.setUint32(4, 36 + dataBytes, true);
  ascii(8, 'WAVE');
  ascii(12, 'fmt ');
  dv.setUint32(16, 16, true); // fmt chunk size
  dv.setUint16(20, 1, true); // PCM
  dv.setUint16(22, 1, true); // channels
  dv.setUint32(24, sampleRate, true);
  dv.setUint32(28, sampleRate * 2, true); // byte rate = rate × blockAlign
  dv.setUint16(32, 2, true); // block align = channels × 2 bytes
  dv.setUint16(34, 16, true); // bits per sample
  ascii(36, 'data');
  dv.setUint32(40, dataBytes, true);
  for (let i = 0; i < samples.length; i++) dv.setInt16(44 + i * 2, samples[i] ?? 0, true);
  return bytes;
}

export function makeAnchorWav(opts: AnchorOptions = ANCHOR_DEFAULTS): Uint8Array {
  return encodeWav16Mono(anchorSamples(opts), opts.sampleRate);
}

function main(): void {
  const bytes = makeAnchorWav();
  mkdirSync(dirname(ANCHOR_PATH), { recursive: true });
  writeFileSync(ANCHOR_PATH, bytes);
  console.log(`wrote ${ANCHOR_PATH} (${bytes.length} bytes)`);
}

// Run only when executed directly (tsx tools/make-anchor-wav.ts), not when imported by tests.
const entry = process.argv[1];
if (entry !== undefined && resolve(entry) === fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)) main();
```

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes (exact contract signatures):

  - T5 `src/probe/gps.ts`: `GPS_MODES: GpsMode[]`, `modeName(m: GpsMode): string`, `class GpsRunner` (`constructor(geo, log)`, `running`, `run(mode, durationS, isHidden?)`, `stop()`, `static previousCrash()`), `type GpsMode`; `src/probe/gps-stats.ts`: `type GpsModeSummary`.
  - T6 `src/probe/audio-worklet.ts` as a Vite worker URL: `import workletUrl from './audio-worklet.ts?worker&url'` (typed by `vite/client`'s `declare module '*?worker&url'`); T6 `src/probe/audio-protocol.ts`: `PROBE_TONE_HZ` (= 55).
  - T7 `src/probe/audio.ts`: `class AudioProbe` (`constructor(log, workletUrl, anchorUrl)`, `start(route, latencyHint)`, `setRoute(route)`, `setTone(toneHz)`, `benchmark(ks?, secondsEach?)`, `resume()`, `recreate()`, `stop()`, `hiddenClockRatio`, `snapshot`, `lastWorkletReport`), `type AudioProbeState`, `type BenchResult`; the log line `audio` `hidden <s>s clock ratio=<r> …`, pushed right after `hiddenClockRatio` is updated.
  - T8 `src/probe/inputs.ts`: `class InputProbe` (`constructor(log, target?)`, `attach()`, `counts`, `probeMotion(durationMs?)`), `type MotionReport`.
  - T9 `src/probe/storage-probe.ts`: `runStorageProbe(nowIso?): Promise<StorageReport>`, `type StorageReport`; asset `/probe/anchor.wav`.
- Produces (the DOM contract T12's e2e specs rely on):
  - Seven sections in order, each `<section id> <h2>`: `#build`, `#env`, `#audio`, `#gps`, `#inputs`, `#storage`, `#export`; footer `Not affiliated with Tesla, Inc.`; one external module script, no inline script/style (CSP).
  - Audio: radios `input[name="latency"]` (`balanced` checked, `playback`) and `input[name="route"]` (`direct`, `media-element`, `direct-anchor` checked); buttons `#audio-start`, `#audio-resume`, `#audio-recreate`, `#audio-bench`, `#audio-tone` (**Pitch step**: enabled only while audio runs; each tap toggles the probe tone 55 ↔ 110 Hz via `setTone()` and logs `note` `tone <hz> Hz on <route>`, an audible tap stimulus for judging each route's tap → sound delay, checklist 2.3b), `#audio-stop`; readouts refreshed every 250 ms: `#audio-state` (`AudioContextState` or `none`), `#audio-route` (route or `—`), `#audio-quanta` (integer text), `#audio-hidden-ratio`, `#audio-report` (last worklet report: quanta, busy %, window, context time, `perfInWorklet`; `—` before the first), `#audio-status`, `#audio-snapshot` (JSON); `#bench-results tbody tr` (one row per `BenchResult`), `#bench-status` (`Done: headroom k = <n>`); observation groups for the selected route in `#observations`: `button[data-q][data-v][aria-pressed]` with `data-q` ∈ `miniplayer | survivesMinimise | wheelTilt | music`; `#obs-route` = selected route.
  - GPS: `#gps-seconds`, one button per mode `#gps-run-<modeName>` (e.g. `#gps-run-poll-hiacc-off`), `#gps-stop`, `#gps-live` (`idle` when no run is active, otherwise starts with the mode name), `#gps-results tbody tr[data-mode][data-fixes]` (one row per finished run, appended), `#gps-crash` banner.
  - Inputs: `#input-counts`, `#motion-run`, `#motion-result`, `#log-view` (last 30 entries, newest first, `t=<s>s <kind> <detail>`).
  - Storage: `#storage-report` (`<dl>`, contains `Visits` once `runStorageProbe()` resolved; runs on load).
  - Export: selects `#note-model`, `#note-music-resume`, `#note-anc` (→ `notes.model`, `notes.musicResumesAfterStop`, `notes.ancInterference` when set; also `notes.version`, `notes.gpsCrash`, one `notes['bench.<latencyHint>']` per benchmarked hint — so both legs of the latencyHint A/B reach the QR — one `notes['motion.<n>']` per motion run, so the parked baseline survives the driving run, and one `notes['latency.<route>']` per route that ran once Chromium reported a non-zero `outputLatency` — `base=<baseLatency> out=<outputLatency> hint=<latencyHint>`, written by the 250 ms readout refresh while that route runs (Chromium reports `outputLatency = 0` until the output device has produced timing data, ≈ 1 s after Start, so it is never recorded at start/switch time). The value is context-level — the same for every route of one context — so it is the context-latency part of spec §6.4's per-route latency record; the extra `<audio>` buffering of `media-element` is judged by ear with **Pitch step** (checklist 2.3b)); the summary's `audio` is the live snapshot, or the snapshot taken at the last **Stop audio** while audio is stopped, plus the latest `bench`/`headroomK`; `#qr-show`, `#qr-prev`, `#qr-next`; `#qr-list figure.qr` each with a `<canvas>` and `<figcaption>QR i/n</figcaption>` (only the current page is visible); `#qr-status`; `#summary-json` = pretty-printed JSON of exactly the summary that was QR-encoded.
  - Query params: `quickbench=1` → benchmark `[1, 2]` × 1 s (else `[1, 2, 4, 8, 16]` × 3 s); `gpsSeconds=<integer 1…3600>` (else 60); `b` ignored (cache-buster).
  - Every `button`, `.choice` label and `select` has `min-height: var(--touch)`, where `--touch` = `minTouchCssPx(devicePixelRatio)` px, set on `<html>` via CSSOM at boot and on resize (CSSOM writes are allowed under `style-src 'self'`).

**Cross-task premises:** (open each named file and confirm before coding)
- T1 `vite.config.ts` has the multi-page input `probe: …/probe/index.html` (so `npm run build` emits `dist/probe/index.html`), and `src/env.d.ts` contains `/// <reference types="vite/client" />` (source of the `'*?worker&url'` module type).
- T1 `checkVersion()` never rejects (failures come back in `error`).
- T2 `detectFeatures()` returns an object whose values are all booleans (`esModules: true` included).
- T3 `chunkText(text)` returns chunks that start with `TBX i/n\n`, each ≤ 1100 + header bytes (so `encodeQr` never throws for them); `drawQr(canvas, m)` sets `canvas.width`/`canvas.height` itself and paints a light quiet zone and dark modules on the 2D context.
- T4 `buildSummary` throws if a forbidden coordinate key appears (handled here as an export error); `ProbeSummary` and `RouteObservation` are exported types.
- T5 `GPS_MODES` order is poll/off, poll/on, watch/off, watch/on and `modeName` yields `poll-hiacc-off` etc.; `GpsRunner.run()` resolves with the summary (geolocation errors are counted, not thrown); fix log entries have `kind 'gps'` and a detail starting with `fix`; `previousCrash()` reads **and clears** the marker, so this page calls it exactly once at load, and the runner itself then sets `crashedPreviously: true` on the first later run of that mode only (this page does not override it).
- T4 Part B (`src/probe/summary.ts` + its test) is executed as Step 0 of this task: T4 deliberately stopped after Part A because `summary.ts` type-imports from T5, T7, T8 and T9.
- T6 `src/probe/audio-worklet.ts` imports only environment-free modules (`./work`, `./audio-protocol`, `../contracts`), so Vite bundles it as one self-contained worker asset (`dist/assets/audio-worklet-<hash>.js`, IIFE) that `audioWorklet.addModule()` can load under `script-src 'self'`.
- T7 `AudioProbe` serialises its operations; when none is pending, `start()`/`recreate()`/`resume()` run synchronously up to their first `await`, so `new AudioContext` and `ctx.resume()` happen inside the click listener (spec §6.3); route `<audio>.play()` runs after `addModule` (or after the 500 ms resume check) resolves and relies on the document's sticky user activation. A call queued behind a pending operation runs in a later microtask, where Chromium's sticky user activation (document-user-activation autoplay policy) still covers context creation and `play()`. Its constructor does not touch Web Audio, and `start()` after `stop()` rebuilds, so this page keeps one instance for the whole visit (recoveries survive Stop/Start); `snapshot`, `hiddenClockRatio` and `lastWorkletReport` are cheap getters (read every 250 ms). `stop()` resets `snapshot` to `state: 'none'` with null latencies. `setTone(hz)` posts the tone to the current node and keeps it for rebuilt nodes (the same instance serves the whole visit, so this page's 55/110 toggle state stays in sync). T7 registers the Media Session handlers; this page never calls `setActionHandler`.
- T5 `GpsRunner.run(mode, durationS)` defaults `isHidden` to `document.visibilityState === 'hidden'` (false without a document), so this page does not pass its own copy of that rule.
- T8 `InputProbe.attach()` is called once; `counts` is safe to read at any time; `probeMotion()` starts any permission request synchronously.
- T9 `runStorageProbe()` never rejects and registers `./sw.js` relative to `/probe/`; the anchor file is at `/probe/anchor.wav`.
- T11 nginx redirects `/probe` → `/probe/` (relative URLs here assume the trailing slash) and serves the CSP `script-src 'self'; style-src 'self'; media-src 'self' blob:`.
- T12's e2e specs use exactly the ids, attributes and texts listed under Produces.

All commands run from the repository root.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing page test**

Create `tests/unit/probe-page.test.ts`. It checks the section contract, CSP cleanliness of the markup, the radio values, that every id `main.ts` looks up exists in the HTML, and the forced dark theme / touch sizing in the CSS.

```ts
import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';

const read = (p: string): string => readFileSync(new URL(`../../${p}`, import.meta.url), 'utf8');
const html = read('probe/index.html');
const css = read('src/probe/probe.css');
const main = read('src/probe/main.ts');

const SECTIONS = ['build', 'env', 'audio', 'gps', 'inputs', 'storage', 'export'];

describe('probe/index.html', () => {
  it('has the seven contract sections in order, each a <section id> with an <h2>', () => {
    const ids = [...html.matchAll(/<section id="([a-z-]+)">\s*<h2>/g)].map((m) => m[1]);
    expect(ids).toEqual(SECTIONS);
  });

  it('is CSP-clean: one external module script, no inline scripts/styles/handlers', () => {
    const scripts = [...html.matchAll(/<script\b[^>]*>/g)].map((m) => m[0]);
    expect(scripts).toEqual(['<script type="module" src="/src/probe/main.ts">']);
    expect(html).not.toMatch(/<style\b/);
    expect(html).not.toMatch(/\sstyle="/);
    expect(html).not.toMatch(/\son[a-z]+="/);
    expect(html).toContain('<link rel="stylesheet" href="/src/probe/probe.css" />');
  });

  it('offers both latencyHint values and all three output routes', () => {
    const radios = (name: string) => [...html.matchAll(new RegExp(`name="${name}" value="([a-z-]+)"`, 'g'))].map((m) => m[1]);
    expect(radios('latency')).toEqual(['balanced', 'playback']);
    expect(radios('route')).toEqual(['direct', 'media-element', 'direct-anchor']);
  });

  it('contains every element id that main.ts looks up', () => {
    const used = new Set([...main.matchAll(/\bel(?:<[A-Za-z]+>)?\('([a-z0-9-]+)'\)/g)].map((m) => m[1]));
    used.add('bench-results');
    used.add('gps-results');
    used.add('env-features');
    expect(used.size).toBeGreaterThan(30);
    for (const id of used) expect(html, `#${id}`).toContain(`id="${id}"`);
  });

  it('carries the non-affiliation footer', () => {
    expect(html).toContain('Not affiliated with Tesla, Inc.');
  });
});

describe('src/probe/probe.css', () => {
  it('forces the dark theme without prefers-color-scheme', () => {
    expect(css).toContain('color-scheme: dark;');
    expect(css).not.toContain('prefers-color-scheme');
  });

  it('sizes buttons, choices and selects from the runtime --touch variable', () => {
    expect(css).toMatch(/button \{[^}]*min-height: var\(--touch\);/);
    expect(css).toMatch(/\.choice \{[^}]*min-height: var\(--touch\);/);
    expect(css).toMatch(/select \{[^}]*min-height: var\(--touch\);/);
    expect(main).toContain("setProperty('--touch', `${minTouchCssPx(");
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Create `src/probe/probe.css`**

Dark theme forced with explicit colours (no colour-scheme media queries); `--touch` defaults to the value for dpr 1.53 until `main.ts` overwrites it.

```css
/* Tesbox probe — dark theme forced: the car reports colour-scheme preferences unreliably (spec §3). */
:root {
  color-scheme: dark;
  --bg: #0e1116;
  --panel: #161b22;
  --line: #2d3540;
  --text: #e6edf3;
  --muted: #8d98a5;
  --accent: #58b4ff;
  --ok: #3fb950;
  --bad: #ff6b61;
  --warn: #e3b341;
  /* Overwritten at runtime with minTouchCssPx(devicePixelRatio) (≥ 22 mm on any Tesla panel). */
  --touch: 108px;
  --gap: 12px;
}

* {
  box-sizing: border-box;
}

[hidden] {
  display: none !important;
}

html,
body {
  margin: 0;
  background: var(--bg);
  color: var(--text);
  font: 18px/1.4 system-ui, -apple-system, 'Segoe UI', Roboto, sans-serif;
}

body {
  max-width: 1200px;
  margin: 0 auto;
  padding: 0 16px 48px;
}

h1 {
  font-size: 1.6rem;
  margin: 16px 0 4px;
}

h2 {
  font-size: 1.3rem;
  margin: 0 0 12px;
}

h3 {
  font-size: 1.05rem;
  margin: 20px 0 8px;
}

.muted {
  color: var(--muted);
}

section {
  background: var(--panel);
  border: 1px solid var(--line);
  border-radius: 14px;
  padding: 16px;
  margin: 16px 0;
}

.buttons {
  display: flex;
  flex-wrap: wrap;
  gap: var(--gap);
  margin: 12px 0;
}

button {
  min-height: var(--touch);
  min-width: var(--touch);
  padding: 8px 20px;
  font: inherit;
  font-weight: 600;
  color: var(--text);
  background: #1f2833;
  border: 2px solid var(--line);
  border-radius: 12px;
  touch-action: manipulation;
  cursor: pointer;
}

button:active {
  background: #2b3a4a;
}

button:disabled {
  opacity: 0.4;
  cursor: default;
}

button[aria-pressed='true'] {
  border-color: var(--accent);
  background: #123049;
}

fieldset {
  border: 1px solid var(--line);
  border-radius: 12px;
  margin: 12px 0;
  padding: 8px 12px 12px;
}

.choices {
  display: flex;
  flex-wrap: wrap;
  gap: var(--gap);
}

.choice {
  display: inline-flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 12px;
  min-height: var(--touch);
  padding: 0 20px;
  border: 2px solid var(--line);
  border-radius: 12px;
  touch-action: manipulation;
}

.choice input {
  width: 28px;
  height: 28px;
  margin: 0;
  accent-color: var(--accent);
}

.notes {
  display: grid;
  gap: var(--gap);
}

.notes label {
  display: grid;
  gap: 6px;
}

select {
  min-height: var(--touch);
  font: inherit;
  color: var(--text);
  background: #1f2833;
  border: 2px solid var(--line);
  border-radius: 12px;
  padding: 0 12px;
}

.kv {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: max-content 1fr;
  gap: 4px 16px;
  margin: 12px 0;
}

.kv dt {
  color: var(--muted);
}

.kv dd {
  margin: 0;
  overflow-wrap: anywhere;
  font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
}

.scroll {
  overflow-x: auto;
}

table {
  border-collapse: collapse;
  width: 100%;
  font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
}

th,
td {
  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--line);
  padding: 6px 10px;
  text-align: left;
  white-space: nowrap;
}

td.yes {
  color: var(--ok);
}

td.no {
  color: var(--bad);
}

pre {
  max-height: 40vh;
  overflow: auto;
  white-space: pre-wrap;
  overflow-wrap: anywhere;
  background: #0a0d12;
  border: 1px solid var(--line);
  border-radius: 10px;
  padding: 10px;
  font-size: 0.8rem;
}

pre:empty {
  display: none;
}

.banner {
  background: #3a2c05;
  border: 2px solid var(--warn);
  border-radius: 12px;
  padding: 12px;
}

.obs-row {
  display: grid;
  gap: 8px;
  margin: 12px 0;
}

.obs-row .buttons {
  margin: 0;
}

.modes {
  list-style: none;
  margin: 0;
  padding: 0;
  display: flex;
  flex-wrap: wrap;
  gap: var(--gap);
}

.log {
  margin: 0;
  padding-left: 1.5em;
  max-height: 50vh;
  overflow: auto;
  font: 0.85rem/1.5 ui-monospace, 'SFMono-Regular', Menlo, Consolas, monospace;
  overflow-wrap: anywhere;
}

.qr {
  margin: 12px 0;
}

.qr canvas {
  display: block;
  max-width: 100%;
  height: auto;
  image-rendering: pixelated;
  background: #fff;
}

.qr figcaption {
  margin-top: 6px;
  font-weight: 600;
}

footer {
  margin: 24px 0;
  text-align: center;
}
```
