- [ ] **Step 5: Create `src/probe/main.ts`**

All DOM wiring. Every asynchronous action has a rejection handler that writes to the event log and the section's status line; `start()`, `recreate()` and `probeMotion()` are called synchronously inside their click listeners (user activation). Route changes after Start call `setRoute()` without recreating the context; observations are kept per route; the hidden-period clock ratio is attributed to the route that was active while hidden, on AudioProbe's `hidden …` log line. **Pitch step** toggles the tone 55 ↔ 110 Hz with `setTone()` (the tester's audible tap stimulus for each route's delay). One `AudioProbe` serves the whole visit (Stop → Start reuses it), every benchmark and motion run is kept in `notes`, and so is each route's context latency — written by the 250 ms readout refresh once Chromium reports a non-zero `outputLatency` (it reads 0 until the output device has produced timing data), and context-level, i.e. the same for every route of one context. A benchmark or recreate that settles after **Stop audio** (or a failed rebuild) returns the page to `idle` rather than `running`, and the snapshot taken at **Stop audio** stands in for the empty live one in the export.

```ts
/**
 * Tesbox in-car probe page (spec §15 Phase 0, §16). Wiring and display only: every measurement
 * lives in the imported probe modules. Coordinates never reach this file's output (summary.ts
 * enforces it with assertNoCoordinates).
 */
import { minTouchCssPx, type OutputRoute } from '../contracts';
import { BUILD_SHA, checkVersion } from '../platform/build-info';
import { detectFeatures, getWebglRenderer, parseMcu, viewportInfo, type Features } from '../platform/detect';
import { chunkText, drawQr, encodeQr } from '../platform/qr';
import { AudioProbe, type AudioProbeState, type BenchResult } from './audio';
import { PROBE_TONE_HZ } from './audio-protocol';
import workletUrl from './audio-worklet.ts?worker&url';
import { GPS_MODES, GpsRunner, modeName, type GpsMode } from './gps';
import type { GpsModeSummary } from './gps-stats';
import { InputProbe, type MotionReport } from './inputs';
import { errText, EventLog, type LogEntry } from './log';
import { runStorageProbe, type StorageReport } from './storage-probe';
import { buildSummary, formatEntry, type ProbeSummary, type RouteObservation } from './summary';

// ───────────────────────────── Constants & query params ─────────────────────────────

const ANCHOR_URL = '/probe/anchor.wav';
const ROUTES: readonly OutputRoute[] = ['direct', 'media-element', 'direct-anchor'];
const HINTS: readonly ('balanced' | 'playback')[] = ['balanced', 'playback'];
const LOG_VIEW_SIZE = 30;

const params = new URLSearchParams(location.search);
const quickBench = params.get('quickbench') === '1';
const BENCH_KS = quickBench ? [1, 2] : [1, 2, 4, 8, 16];
const BENCH_SECONDS = quickBench ? 1 : 3;
const gpsSeconds = parseGpsSeconds(params.get('gpsSeconds'));
// `b` is a cache-buster only (spec §12) and deliberately ignored.

function parseGpsSeconds(raw: string | null): number {
  const n = raw === null ? Number.NaN : Number(raw);
  return Number.isInteger(n) && n >= 1 && n <= 3600 ? n : 60;
}

// ───────────────────────────── DOM helpers ─────────────────────────────

function el<T extends HTMLElement = HTMLElement>(id: string): T {
  const node = document.getElementById(id);
  if (node === null) throw new Error(`probe markup is missing #${id}`);
  return node as T;
}

function make<K extends keyof HTMLElementTagNameMap>(tag: K, text?: string, className?: string): HTMLElementTagNameMap[K] {
  const node = document.createElement(tag);
  if (text !== undefined) node.textContent = text;
  if (className !== undefined) node.className = className;
  return node;
}

function setKv(dl: HTMLElement, rows: readonly (readonly [string, string])[]): void {
  const nodes: HTMLElement[] = [];
  for (const [k, v] of rows) nodes.push(make('dt', k), make('dd', v));
  dl.replaceChildren(...nodes);
}

function tbody(tableId: string): HTMLTableSectionElement {
  const body = el<HTMLTableElement>(tableId).tBodies[0];
  if (body === undefined) throw new Error(`#${tableId} has no <tbody>`);
  return body;
}

function fmtNum(v: number | null, digits = 0): string {
  return v === null ? '—' : v.toFixed(digits);
}

function fmtPct(v: number): string {
  return `${v.toFixed(0)}%`;
}

// ───────────────────────────── Shared state ─────────────────────────────

const log = new EventLog();
const features: Features = detectFeatures();
const webgl = getWebglRenderer();
const mcu = parseMcu(webgl);
const inputs = new InputProbe(log);
const gpsRunner = 'geolocation' in navigator ? new GpsRunner(navigator.geolocation, log) : null;

type AudioPhase = 'idle' | 'starting' | 'running' | 'busy';
/** One AudioProbe per page visit: recoveries survive Stop/Start (start() after stop() rebuilds). */
let audio: AudioProbe | null = null;
let audioPhase: AudioPhase = 'idle';
let bench: BenchResult[] = [];
let headroomK: number | null = null;
/** Snapshot taken at the last Stop audio: stop() resets the live snapshot to state 'none'. */
let lastAudioState: AudioProbeState | null = null;
/** Pitch step state: the probe tone toggles an octave, 55 ↔ 110 Hz (AudioProbe keeps it across rebuilds). */
let toneHz = PROBE_TONE_HZ;

const gpsSummaries: GpsModeSummary[] = [];
let gpsLive: { mode: string; fixes: number; other: number; endsAtMs: number } | null = null;

let motion: MotionReport | null = null;
let motionRuns = 0;
let storage: StorageReport | null = null;
const notes: Record<string, string> = {};
const routes: Partial<Record<OutputRoute, RouteObservation>> = {};
const routesUsed = new Set<OutputRoute>();

let qrPage = 0;
let qrCount = 0;

function fail(what: string, e: unknown, statusId?: string): void {
  const msg = `${what}: ${errText(e)}`;
  log.push('error', msg);
  if (statusId !== undefined) el(statusId).textContent = msg;
}

// ───────────────────────────── Touch sizing & environment ─────────────────────────────

function applyTouchSize(): void {
  document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--touch', `${minTouchCssPx(window.devicePixelRatio || 1)}px`);
}

function renderEnv(): void {
  const vp = viewportInfo();
  setKv(el('env-kv'), [
    ['User agent', navigator.userAgent],
    ['devicePixelRatio', String(vp.dpr)],
    ['screen', `${vp.screen[0]}×${vp.screen[1]}`],
    ['inner', `${vp.inner[0]}×${vp.inner[1]}`],
    ['visualViewport', vp.visual === null ? 'n/a' : `${Math.round(vp.visual[0])}×${Math.round(vp.visual[1])}`],
    ['maxTouchPoints', String(vp.maxTouchPoints)],
    ['WebGL renderer', webgl ?? 'unavailable'],
    ['MCU guess', mcu],
    ['Min touch target', `${minTouchCssPx(vp.dpr || 1)} CSS px`],
  ]);
}

function renderFeatures(): void {
  const rows = Object.entries(features).map(([name, ok]) => {
    const tr = make('tr');
    tr.append(make('td', name), make('td', ok ? 'yes' : 'no', ok ? 'yes' : 'no'));
    return tr;
  });
  tbody('env-features').replaceChildren(...rows);
}

// ───────────────────────────── Build ─────────────────────────────

async function initBuild(): Promise<void> {
  el('build-sha').textContent = BUILD_SHA;
  const status = el('build-status');
  const v = await checkVersion();
  if (v.error !== undefined) {
    status.textContent = `Version check failed: ${v.error}`;
    notes.version = 'check-failed';
    return;
  }
  if (v.stale && v.remote !== null) {
    const link = make('a', `?b=${v.remote}`);
    link.href = `?b=${encodeURIComponent(v.remote)}`;
    status.replaceChildren('Stale build — reload with ', link);
    notes.version = `stale; server has ${v.remote}`;
    return;
  }
  status.textContent = 'Up to date';
  notes.version = 'up-to-date';
}

// ───────────────────────────── Audio ─────────────────────────────

function checkedValue<T extends string>(name: string, allowed: readonly T[], fallback: T): T {
  const value = document.querySelector<HTMLInputElement>(`input[name="${name}"]:checked`)?.value;
  const match = allowed.find((a) => a === value);
  return match ?? fallback;
}

const selectedRoute = (): OutputRoute => checkedValue('route', ROUTES, 'direct-anchor');
const selectedHint = (): 'balanced' | 'playback' => checkedValue('latency', HINTS, 'balanced');

function setAudioPhase(phase: AudioPhase): void {
  audioPhase = phase;
  const running = phase === 'running';
  el<HTMLButtonElement>('audio-start').disabled = phase !== 'idle';
  el<HTMLButtonElement>('audio-resume').disabled = !running;
  el<HTMLButtonElement>('audio-recreate').disabled = !running;
  el<HTMLButtonElement>('audio-bench').disabled = !running;
  el<HTMLButtonElement>('audio-tone').disabled = !running;
  el<HTMLButtonElement>('audio-stop').disabled = !(running || phase === 'busy');
}

/** Phase once a benchmark/recreate settles: a Stop pressed during it, or a failed rebuild, leaves no context. */
function settledPhase(probe: AudioProbe): AudioPhase {
  return probe.snapshot.state === 'none' ? 'idle' : 'running';
}

function renderAudio(): void {
  const s = audio?.snapshot ?? null;
  // Context latency of the running route (spec §6.4), recorded once Chromium has measured it:
  // outputLatency reads 0 until the output device has produced timing data (≈ 1 s after Start).
  // Context-level (the same for every route of one context); media-element's extra <audio>
  // buffering is judged by ear with Pitch step (checklist 2.3b).
  if (s !== null && s.state === 'running' && s.route !== null && s.outputLatency !== null && s.outputLatency > 0) {
    notes[`latency.${s.route}`] = `base=${s.baseLatency ?? 'n/a'} out=${s.outputLatency} hint=${s.latencyHint}`;
  }
  el('audio-state').textContent = s === null ? 'none' : s.state;
  el('audio-route').textContent = s?.route ?? '—';
  el('audio-quanta').textContent = String(s?.quanta ?? 0);
  el('audio-hidden-ratio').textContent = fmtNum(audio?.hiddenClockRatio ?? null, 3);
  const r = audio?.lastWorkletReport ?? null;
  const busy = r === null || r.windowMs <= 0 ? 'n/a' : `${((r.busyMs / r.windowMs) * 100).toFixed(1)}%`;
  el('audio-report').textContent =
    r === null
      ? '—'
      : `quanta=${r.quanta} busy=${busy} window=${r.windowMs.toFixed(0)} ms ctxTime=${r.currentTime.toFixed(2)} s perfInWorklet=${r.perfInWorklet}`;
  el('audio-snapshot').textContent = s === null ? '' : JSON.stringify(s, null, 1);
}

function renderBench(): void {
  const rows = bench.map((r) => {
    const tr = make('tr');
    tr.append(
      make('td', String(r.k)),
      make('td', r.ratio.toFixed(4)),
      make('td', r.overloaded ? 'yes' : 'no', r.overloaded ? 'no' : 'yes'),
      make('td', r.busyFrac === null ? 'n/a' : fmtPct(r.busyFrac * 100)),
    );
    return tr;
  });
  tbody('bench-results').replaceChildren(...rows);
  el('bench-status').textContent = headroomK === null ? 'not run' : `Done: headroom k = ${headroomK}`;
}

function startAudio(): void {
  const route = selectedRoute();
  const hint = selectedHint();
  let probe: AudioProbe;
  try {
    probe = audio ?? new AudioProbe(log, workletUrl, ANCHOR_URL); // one instance per page: recoveries + bench survive Stop/Start
  } catch (e) {
    fail('audio', e, 'audio-status');
    return;
  }
  audio = probe;
  setAudioPhase('starting');
  el('audio-status').textContent = 'starting…';
  // With no AudioProbe operation pending (always the case here: Start is enabled only while idle),
  // start() runs synchronously up to its first await, i.e. inside this click (user activation).
  probe.start(route, hint).then(
    () => {
      routesUsed.add(route);
      setAudioPhase('running');
      el('audio-status').textContent = `running (${route}, ${hint})`;
      if (selectedRoute() !== route) onRouteChange(); // radio changed while starting
    },
    (e: unknown) => {
      probe.stop().catch(() => undefined); // clears any half-built state, Media Session and listeners
      setAudioPhase('idle');
      fail('audio start', e, 'audio-status');
    },
  );
}

function resumeAudio(): void {
  const probe = audio;
  if (probe === null) return;
  probe.resume().then(
    (ok) => {
      el('audio-status').textContent = ok ? 'resume ok' : 'resume failed — try Recreate';
    },
    (e: unknown) => fail('audio resume', e, 'audio-status'),
  );
}

function recreateAudio(): void {
  const probe = audio;
  if (probe === null) return;
  setAudioPhase('busy');
  el('audio-status').textContent = 'recreating…';
  // Called inside the tap: with no AudioProbe operation pending, recreate() builds the new AudioContext
  // synchronously, before its first await; a queued recreate relies on Chromium's sticky user activation.
  probe.recreate().then(
    () => {
      setAudioPhase(settledPhase(probe));
      el('audio-status').textContent = 'recreated';
    },
    (e: unknown) => {
      setAudioPhase(settledPhase(probe));
      fail('audio recreate', e, 'audio-status');
    },
  );
}

function runBenchmark(): void {
  const probe = audio;
  if (probe === null) return;
  setAudioPhase('busy');
  el('bench-status').textContent = `running k = ${BENCH_KS.join(', ')} × ${BENCH_SECONDS} s…`;
  probe.benchmark(BENCH_KS, BENCH_SECONDS).then(
    (r) => {
      bench = r.results;
      headroomK = r.headroomK;
      // One note per latencyHint, so both legs of the Intel A/B reach the QR (bench/headroomK keep only the latest).
      const s = probe.snapshot;
      notes[`bench.${s.latencyHint}`] =
        `${r.results.map((b) => `k${b.k}=${b.ratio}${b.overloaded ? '!' : ''}`).join(' ')} headroomK=${r.headroomK}` +
        ` busy=${r.results.map((b) => b.busyFrac ?? 'n/a').join(',')}` +
        ` sr=${s.sampleRate ?? 'n/a'} baseLat=${s.baseLatency ?? 'n/a'} outLat=${s.outputLatency ?? 'n/a'} perfInWorklet=${String(s.perfInWorklet)}`;
      setAudioPhase(settledPhase(probe)); // a Stop pressed during the benchmark leaves no context
      renderBench(); // AudioProbe.benchmark() already logged `bench headroomK=<k>` — no second entry here
    },
    (e: unknown) => {
      setAudioPhase(settledPhase(probe)); // e.g. Stop during the benchmark: back to idle, Start re-enabled
      fail('benchmark', e, 'bench-status');
    },
  );
}

function stopAudio(): void {
  const probe = audio;
  if (probe === null) return;
  const s = probe.snapshot;
  if (s.state !== 'none') lastAudioState = s; // the export keeps this after stop() resets the snapshot
  probe.stop().then(
    () => {
      setAudioPhase('idle');
      el('audio-status').textContent = 'stopped';
    },
    (e: unknown) => {
      setAudioPhase('idle');
      fail('audio stop', e, 'audio-status');
    },
  );
}

function onRouteChange(): void {
  const route = selectedRoute();
  renderObservations();
  const probe = audio;
  if (probe === null || (audioPhase !== 'running' && audioPhase !== 'busy')) return;
  probe.setRoute(route).then(
    () => {
      routesUsed.add(route);
      el('audio-status').textContent = `running (${route})`;
    },
    (e: unknown) => fail(`route ${route}`, e, 'audio-status'),
  );
}

/** Pitch step: an audible tap stimulus; the tester judges the tap → pitch-change delay of each route (checklist 2.3b). */
function stepTone(): void {
  const probe = audio;
  if (probe === null) return;
  toneHz = toneHz === PROBE_TONE_HZ ? PROBE_TONE_HZ * 2 : PROBE_TONE_HZ;
  probe.setTone(toneHz);
  log.push('note', `tone ${toneHz} Hz on ${probe.snapshot.route ?? selectedRoute()}`);
}

/**
 * Attributes a hidden-period audio-clock ratio to the active route, exactly when AudioProbe measured
 * a fresh one: it pushes `audio` `hidden <s>s clock ratio=…` right after updating hiddenClockRatio,
 * and pushes nothing for a hidden period it could not measure (so no stale ratio is copied).
 */
function onHiddenRatioLogged(e: LogEntry): void {
  const probe = audio;
  if (e.kind !== 'audio' || !e.detail.startsWith('hidden ') || probe === null) return;
  const route = probe.snapshot.route;
  const ratio = probe.hiddenClockRatio;
  if (route === null || ratio === null) return;
  observationFor(route).hiddenClockRatio = ratio;
  routesUsed.add(route);
  renderObservations();
}

// ───────────────────────────── Route observations ─────────────────────────────

type YesNoKey = 'miniplayer' | 'survivesMinimise' | 'wheelTilt';
type ObsKey = YesNoKey | 'music';

const QUESTIONS: readonly { key: ObsKey; label: string; options: readonly string[] }[] = [
  { key: 'miniplayer', label: 'Miniplayer shows Tesbox while the browser is minimised?', options: ['yes', 'no', '?'] },
  { key: 'survivesMinimise', label: 'Tone keeps playing while minimised?', options: ['yes', 'no', '?'] },
  { key: 'wheelTilt', label: 'Left scroll-wheel tilt logged as nexttrack/previoustrack or MediaTrack key?', options: ['yes', 'no', '?'] },
  { key: 'music', label: 'Your music when Tesbox audio started:', options: ['paused', 'mixed', '?'] },
];

function observationFor(route: OutputRoute): RouteObservation {
  const existing = routes[route];
  if (existing !== undefined) return existing;
  const created: RouteObservation = { miniplayer: '?', survivesMinimise: '?', wheelTilt: '?', music: '?', hiddenClockRatio: null };
  routes[route] = created;
  return created;
}

function setObservation(o: RouteObservation, key: ObsKey, value: string): void {
  if (key === 'music') {
    if (value === 'paused' || value === 'mixed' || value === '?') o.music = value;
    return;
  }
  if (value === 'yes' || value === 'no' || value === '?') o[key] = value;
}

function isObsKey(v: string | undefined): v is ObsKey {
  return QUESTIONS.some((q) => q.key === v);
}

function renderObservations(): void {
  const route = selectedRoute();
  el('obs-route').textContent = route;
  const current = routes[route];
  const rows = QUESTIONS.map((q) => {
    const row = make('div', undefined, 'obs-row');
    const buttons = make('div', undefined, 'buttons');
    for (const option of q.options) {
      const b = make('button', option);
      b.type = 'button';
      b.dataset.q = q.key;
      b.dataset.v = option;
      b.setAttribute('aria-pressed', String(current !== undefined && current[q.key] === option));
      buttons.append(b);
    }
    row.append(make('span', q.label), buttons);
    return row;
  });
  const ratio = current?.hiddenClockRatio ?? null;
  const ratioLine = make('p', `Hidden clock ratio for this route: ${fmtNum(ratio, 3)}`, 'muted');
  el('observations').replaceChildren(...rows, ratioLine);
}

function onObservationClick(ev: Event): void {
  const target = ev.target;
  if (!(target instanceof HTMLElement)) return;
  const button = target.closest<HTMLButtonElement>('button[data-q]');
  const key = button?.dataset.q;
  const value = button?.dataset.v;
  if (button === null || !isObsKey(key) || value === undefined) return;
  const route = selectedRoute();
  setObservation(observationFor(route), key, value);
  routesUsed.add(route);
  log.push('note', `${route} ${key}=${value}`);
  renderObservations();
}

// ───────────────────────────── GPS ─────────────────────────────

function setGpsButtons(running: boolean): void {
  for (const b of el('gps-modes').querySelectorAll<HTMLButtonElement>('button')) b.disabled = running || gpsRunner === null;
  el<HTMLButtonElement>('gps-stop').disabled = !running;
}

function renderGpsLive(): void {
  const live = gpsLive;
  if (gpsRunner === null) {
    el('gps-live').textContent = 'Geolocation API unavailable';
    return;
  }
  if (live === null) {
    el('gps-live').textContent = 'idle';
    return;
  }
  const left = Math.max(0, Math.ceil((live.endsAtMs - performance.now()) / 1000));
  el('gps-live').textContent = `${live.mode}: ${live.fixes} fixes, ${live.other} other events, ${left} s left`;
}

function renderGpsResults(): void {
  const rows = gpsSummaries.map((s) => {
    const tr = make('tr');
    tr.dataset.mode = s.mode;
    tr.dataset.fixes = String(s.fixes);
    const cells = [
      s.mode,
      String(s.durationS),
      String(s.fixes),
      `${s.errors.permission}/${s.errors.unavailable}/${s.errors.timeout}`,
      `${fmtNum(s.intervalMedianMs)}/${fmtNum(s.intervalP95Ms)}`,
      `${fmtNum(s.ageMedianMs)}/${fmtNum(s.ageP95Ms)}`,
      fmtPct(s.staleOver3sPct),
      fmtPct(s.duplicatePct),
      fmtPct(s.speedNullPct),
      fmtPct(s.speedZeroPct),
      fmtPct(s.speedZeroWhileMovingPct),
      fmtNum(s.accuracyMedianM, 1),
      fmtPct(s.altitudePresentPct),
      String(s.fixesWhileHidden),
      fmtNum(s.maxSpeedMps, 1),
      s.crashedPreviously ? 'yes' : 'no',
    ];
    tr.append(...cells.map((c) => make('td', c)));
    return tr;
  });
  tbody('gps-results').replaceChildren(...rows);
}

function runGps(mode: GpsMode): void {
  const runner = gpsRunner;
  if (runner === null || runner.running) return;
  const name = modeName(mode);
  gpsLive = { mode: name, fixes: 0, other: 0, endsAtMs: performance.now() + gpsSeconds * 1000 };
  setGpsButtons(true);
  renderGpsLive();
  runner
    .run(mode, gpsSeconds) // T5's default isHidden reads document.visibilityState
    .then(
      (s) => {
        gpsSummaries.push(s); // T5 already flags the first run of a mode whose crash marker previousCrash() consumed
        renderGpsResults();
      },
      (e: unknown) => fail(`gps ${name}`, e, 'gps-live'),
    )
    .finally(() => {
      gpsLive = null;
      setGpsButtons(false);
      renderGpsLive();
    });
}

function initGps(): void {
  el('gps-seconds').textContent = String(gpsSeconds);
  const crash = GpsRunner.previousCrash();
  if (crash !== null) {
    const banner = el('gps-crash');
    banner.textContent = `The previous ${crash.mode} run (started ${crash.startedAt}) never finished — the page crashed or was reloaded during it.`;
    banner.hidden = false;
    notes.gpsCrash = `${crash.mode} started ${crash.startedAt}`;
    log.push('gps', `previous run did not finish: ${crash.mode}`);
  }
  const items = GPS_MODES.map((mode) => {
    const name = modeName(mode);
    const li = make('li');
    const b = make('button', `Run ${gpsSeconds} s — ${name}`);
    b.type = 'button';
    b.id = `gps-run-${name}`;
    b.addEventListener('click', () => runGps(mode));
    li.append(b);
    return li;
  });
  el('gps-modes').replaceChildren(...items);
  el('gps-stop').addEventListener('click', () => gpsRunner?.stop());
  log.subscribe((e) => {
    if (e.kind !== 'gps' || gpsLive === null) return;
    if (e.detail.startsWith('fix')) gpsLive.fixes++;
    else gpsLive.other++;
  });
  setGpsButtons(false);
  renderGpsLive();
}

// ───────────────────────────── Inputs & event log ─────────────────────────────

let logRenderPending = false;

function renderLog(): void {
  logRenderPending = false;
  const items = log
    .entries()
    .slice(-LOG_VIEW_SIZE)
    .reverse()
    .map((e) => make('li', formatEntry(e)));
  el('log-view').replaceChildren(...items);
}

function scheduleLogRender(): void {
  if (logRenderPending) return;
  logRenderPending = true;
  window.setTimeout(renderLog, 100);
}

function renderInputCounts(): void {
  const c = inputs.counts;
  const media = Object.entries(c.mediaSession)
    .map(([action, n]) => `${action}×${n}`)
    .join(', ');
  setKv(el('input-counts'), [
    ['Media Session', media || 'none'],
    ['Keys', c.keys.length > 0 ? c.keys.join(', ') : 'none'],
    ['Wheel events', String(c.wheel)],
    ['Gamepads', c.gamepads.length > 0 ? c.gamepads.join(', ') : 'none'],
    ['Visibility changes', String(c.visibilityChanges)],
    ['Log events', String(log.count())],
  ]);
}

function renderMotion(r: MotionReport): void {
  const range = r.accelRange === null ? 'n/a' : `${r.accelRange[0].toFixed(2)}…${r.accelRange[1].toFixed(2)} m/s²`;
  el('motion-result').textContent =
    `available=${r.available} permission=${r.permission} events=${r.events} rate=${fmtNum(r.rateHz, 1)} Hz range=${range}`;
}

function probeMotion(): void {
  const b = el<HTMLButtonElement>('motion-run');
  b.disabled = true;
  el('motion-result').textContent = 'listening for 5 s…';
  // Called synchronously in the click: DeviceMotionEvent.requestPermission needs user activation.
  inputs.probeMotion(5000).then(
    (r) => {
      motion = r; // the summary's `motion` is the latest run; every run is also kept as a note
      notes[`motion.${++motionRuns}`] =
        `available=${r.available} permission=${r.permission} events=${r.events} rate=${r.rateHz ?? 'n/a'} range=${r.accelRange?.join('..') ?? 'n/a'}`;
      renderMotion(r);
      b.disabled = false;
    },
    (e: unknown) => {
      b.disabled = false;
      fail('motion', e, 'motion-result');
    },
  );
}

// ───────────────────────────── Storage ─────────────────────────────

function renderStorage(r: StorageReport): void {
  setKv(el('storage-report'), [
    ['localStorage', String(r.localStorage)],
    ['Visits', String(r.visits)],
    ['First seen', r.firstSeen ?? '—'],
    ['Previous visit', r.lastSeen ?? '—'],
    ['Cache Storage', String(r.cacheStorage)],
    ['Cache entry from earlier visit', r.cachePersisted === null ? 'n/a' : String(r.cachePersisted)],
    ['Cache entry age', r.cacheAgeS === null ? '—' : `${r.cacheAgeS} s`],
    ['Service worker supported', String(r.swSupported)],
    ['Service worker registered', String(r.swRegistered)],
    ['Page controlled at load', String(r.swControlled)],
    ['Errors', r.error ?? 'none'],
  ]);
}

function initStorage(): void {
  runStorageProbe().then(
    (r) => {
      storage = r;
      renderStorage(r);
      log.push(
        'storage',
        `localStorage=${r.localStorage} visits=${r.visits} cache=${r.cacheStorage} persisted=${String(r.cachePersisted)} sw=${r.swRegistered} controlled=${r.swControlled}`,
      );
    },
    (e: unknown) => fail('storage', e),
  );
}

// ───────────────────────────── Export (QR) ─────────────────────────────

function selectValue(id: string): string {
  return el<HTMLSelectElement>(id).value;
}

function collectSummary(): ProbeSummary {
  for (const r of routesUsed) observationFor(r);
  const allNotes: Record<string, string> = { ...notes };
  const model = selectValue('note-model');
  const musicResume = selectValue('note-music-resume');
  const anc = selectValue('note-anc');
  if (model !== '') allNotes.model = model;
  if (musicResume !== '') allNotes.musicResumesAfterStop = musicResume;
  if (anc !== '') allNotes.ancInterference = anc;
  // While audio is stopped the live snapshot is empty (state 'none'): export the one taken at the last Stop.
  const live = audio?.snapshot ?? null;
  const snap = live !== null && live.state === 'none' && lastAudioState !== null ? lastAudioState : live;
  return buildSummary(
    {
      sha: BUILD_SHA,
      at: new Date().toISOString(),
      env: { ua: navigator.userAgent, viewport: viewportInfo(), webgl, mcu, features },
      audio: snap === null ? null : { ...snap, bench: [...bench], headroomK },
      routes: { ...routes },
      gps: [...gpsSummaries],
      motion,
      storage,
      inputs: inputs.counts,
      notes: allNotes,
    },
    log,
  );
}

function showQrPage(page: number): void {
  qrPage = Math.min(Math.max(page, 0), Math.max(qrCount - 1, 0));
  const figures = el('qr-list').querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>('figure');
  figures.forEach((f, i) => {
    f.hidden = i !== qrPage;
  });
  el<HTMLButtonElement>('qr-prev').disabled = qrPage <= 0;
  el<HTMLButtonElement>('qr-next').disabled = qrPage >= qrCount - 1;
}

function showQr(): void {
  const status = el('qr-status');
  let summary: ProbeSummary;
  try {
    summary = collectSummary();
  } catch (e) {
    fail('export', e, 'qr-status');
    return;
  }
  const json = JSON.stringify(summary);
  el('summary-json').textContent = JSON.stringify(summary, null, 2);
  const chunks = chunkText(json);
  const figures: HTMLElement[] = [];
  try {
    chunks.forEach((chunk, i) => {
      const figure = make('figure', undefined, 'qr');
      const canvas = make('canvas');
      drawQr(canvas, encodeQr(chunk));
      figure.append(canvas, make('figcaption', `QR ${i + 1}/${chunks.length}`));
      figures.push(figure);
    });
  } catch (e) {
    fail('QR encode', e, 'qr-status');
    return;
  }
  el('qr-list').replaceChildren(...figures);
  qrCount = figures.length;
  showQrPage(0);
  const bytes = new TextEncoder().encode(json).length;
  status.textContent = `${bytes} bytes in ${qrCount} QR code(s). Scan every page with your phone, in order.`;
  log.push('note', `exported ${qrCount} QR code(s)`);
}

// ───────────────────────────── Boot ─────────────────────────────

function boot(): void {
  window.addEventListener('error', (e) => log.push('error', e.message || 'script error'));
  window.addEventListener('unhandledrejection', (e: PromiseRejectionEvent) => log.push('error', `unhandled: ${errText(e.reason)}`));
  log.subscribe(scheduleLogRender);

  applyTouchSize();
  renderEnv();
  renderFeatures();
  window.addEventListener('resize', () => {
    applyTouchSize();
    renderEnv();
  });
  window.visualViewport?.addEventListener('resize', renderEnv);

  el('audio-start').addEventListener('click', startAudio);
  el('audio-resume').addEventListener('click', resumeAudio);
  el('audio-recreate').addEventListener('click', recreateAudio);
  el('audio-bench').addEventListener('click', runBenchmark);
  el('audio-tone').addEventListener('click', stepTone);
  el('audio-stop').addEventListener('click', stopAudio);
  for (const input of document.querySelectorAll<HTMLInputElement>('input[name="route"]')) {
    input.addEventListener('change', onRouteChange);
  }
  el('observations').addEventListener('click', onObservationClick);
  log.subscribe(onHiddenRatioLogged);
  setAudioPhase('idle');
  renderObservations();
  renderBench();

  initGps();

  inputs.attach();
  el('motion-run').addEventListener('click', probeMotion);

  el('qr-show').addEventListener('click', showQr);
  el('qr-prev').addEventListener('click', () => showQrPage(qrPage - 1));
  el('qr-next').addEventListener('click', () => showQrPage(qrPage + 1));

  window.setInterval(() => {
    renderAudio();
    renderInputCounts();
    renderGpsLive();
  }, 250);
  renderAudio();
  renderInputCounts();
  renderLog();

  initBuild().catch((e: unknown) => fail('version check', e, 'build-status'));
  initStorage();
}

boot();
```

### Task 11: Security headers (single source), Vite preview headers, nginx, Docker

**Files:**
- Create: `deploy/csp.ts`
- Create: `deploy/security-headers.inc`
- Create: `nginx.conf`
- Create: `Dockerfile`
- Create: `docker-compose.yml`
- Create: `deploy/headers-plugin.ts`
- Modify: `vite.config.ts` (import `headersPlugin` and add it to `plugins`)
- Test: `tests/unit/csp.test.ts` (header constants + nginx/include/Dockerfile/compose consistency)
- Test: `tests/unit/headers-plugin.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes:
  - `vite`: `import type { Plugin } from 'vite'` (hook `configurePreviewServer(server)` → `server.middlewares.use(fn)`).
  - `node:http`: `IncomingMessage`, `ServerResponse` (types; real instances in the test).
  - T1 `vite.config.ts` (`plugins: [versionPlugin(sha)]`), T1 `deploy/build-sha.ts` (`GITHUB_SHA` env → short SHA), T1 `deploy/version-plugin.ts` (emits `dist/version.json`), T1 `package-lock.json` + `npm ci`/`npm run build`.
- Produces:
  - `deploy/csp.ts` (exact contract values): `export const CSP: string`, `export const NO_STORE_PATHS: string[]` (`'/'`, `'/index.html'`, `'/sw.js'`, `'/version.json'`, `'/probe/'`, `'/probe/index.html'`, `'/probe/sw.js'`), `export const SECURITY_HEADERS: Record<string, string>` (CSP, `X-Content-Type-Options: nosniff`, `Referrer-Policy: no-referrer`, `Permissions-Policy: geolocation=(self), camera=(), microphone=()`), `export const IMMUTABLE = 'public, max-age=31536000, immutable'`.
  - `deploy/headers-plugin.ts`: `export function cacheControlFor(pathname: string): string` (`/assets/*` → `IMMUTABLE`; NO_STORE_PATHS and extension-less paths → `no-store`; other files → `no-cache`); `export function headersFor(url: string): Record<string, string>` (`SECURITY_HEADERS` + `Cache-Control`, query ignored); `export function headersMiddleware(req: IncomingMessage, res: ServerResponse, next: () => void): void` (sets and pins those headers; `/probe` → `301 Location: /probe/<query>`); `export function headersPlugin(): Plugin` (`name: 'tesbox-headers'`, preview server only — the dev server is untouched).
  - `deploy/security-headers.inc`: the four `add_header … always;` lines, values verbatim from `SECURITY_HEADERS`.
  - `nginx.conf` (installed as `/etc/nginx/conf.d/default.conf`): port 8080, `absolute_redirect off`, MIME additions `mjs`/`webmanifest`/`wasm` (+ `wav`, which nginx's `mime.types` lacks), gzip, every `location` includes `/etc/nginx/conf.d/security-headers.inc`; `/assets/` immutable (not on errors); each NO_STORE_PATH has its own exact `location` with `Cache-Control: no-store` (also on 404); `/probe` → 301 `/probe/`; everything else `no-cache` with SPA fallback `try_files $uri $uri/ /index.html` (fallback HTML resolves to the no-store `/index.html` location).
  - `Dockerfile`: build stage `node:22-alpine` on `$BUILDPLATFORM` (`npm ci` layer first, then `ARG GITHUB_SHA=dev` + `ENV`, so a new SHA keeps the dependency layer cached), runtime `nginxinc/nginx-unprivileged:alpine` (non-root, `EXPOSE 8080`, `HEALTHCHECK` via `wget -qO- http://127.0.0.1:8080/version.json`). CI (T12) builds it with `--build-arg GITHUB_SHA=<sha>`.
  - `docker-compose.yml`: service `tesbox`, image `ghcr.io/atvriders/tesbox:latest`, ports `"8080:8080"`.

**Cross-task premises:** (open each named file and confirm before coding)
- T1 `vite.config.ts` contains the line `import { versionPlugin } from './deploy/version-plugin.ts';` and the line `  plugins: [versionPlugin(sha)],`. T1 imports config-reachable TypeScript **with** the `.ts` suffix and enables `"allowImportingTsExtensions": true` in `tsconfig.json` (so Vite 8.3 prints no `configLoader: 'native'` warning). This task follows that convention in `vite.config.ts` and in `deploy/headers-plugin.ts` (`'./csp.ts'`). If T1's imports turn out to be extension-less instead, drop the `.ts` suffix in both places (Step 15 says how to tell).
- T1 `deploy/build-sha.ts` returns `'dev'` when `GITHUB_SHA` is unset; the Dockerfile's `ARG GITHUB_SHA=dev` makes an omitted build-arg yield `dev` rather than an empty string.
- T1 `.dockerignore` excludes at least `node_modules`, `dist`, `.git`, `test-results`, `playwright-report` — otherwise `COPY . .` would overwrite the container's `npm ci` install with the host's `node_modules`.
- T1 `versionPlugin` emits `dist/version.json` (healthcheck target) and Vite's default `assetsDir` (`assets`) is unchanged, so hashed output lives under `/assets/`.
- T9 and T10 must have landed before Steps 16–17: the build needs T10's `probe/index.html` (T1: any build must run after T10; without it `npm run build` fails with `[UNRESOLVED_ENTRY] Cannot resolve entry module probe/index.html`), and Step 16 curls T9's `public/probe/anchor.wav` (served at `/probe/anchor.wav`; T9 also provides `public/probe/sw.js`). This task runs in wave W2, before T9 (W3) and T10 (W5), so Steps 16–17 are deferred until T10 has landed (see the note before Step 16); Steps 1–15 and 18 need neither.
- Waves share one checkout: W2 runs this task next to T2, T3, T4 Part A and T6. When Step 15 or Step 18 reports failures or diagnostics only in files owned by a sibling task of the same wave that has not finished (e.g. T3's `tests/unit/qr.test.ts` importing `encodeQr` before T3 Step 8, or T6's tests importing modules not yet created), wait for that task to land and re-run; never edit another task's files.
- T12's `headers.spec.ts` imports `CSP`, `IMMUTABLE`, `NO_STORE_PATHS` from `deploy/csp.ts` and checks the container; T12's CI runs the image built from this `Dockerfile`. T12 (which creates `NOTICES` and `LICENSE`) later inserts two `COPY` lines after `COPY --from=build /app/dist /usr/share/nginx/html`, so the MIT notice of T3's adapted QR encoder ships in the image's web root; this task's Dockerfile test checks line containment only, so it stays green.

All commands run from the repository root.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing consistency test**

Create `tests/unit/csp.test.ts`. Every file is read inside its own test, so each missing file fails only its own tests.

```ts
import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { CSP, IMMUTABLE, NO_STORE_PATHS, SECURITY_HEADERS } from '../../deploy/csp';

const read = (p: string): string => readFileSync(new URL(`../../${p}`, import.meta.url), 'utf8');
const INCLUDE = 'include /etc/nginx/conf.d/security-headers.inc;';

/** nginx.conf `location` blocks (this config never nests braces inside a location). */
function locationBlocks(conf: string): { head: string; body: string }[] {
  const blocks: { head: string; body: string }[] = [];
  const re = /^\s*location\s+([^{\n]+)\{([^}]*)\}/gm;
  for (let m = re.exec(conf); m !== null; m = re.exec(conf)) {
    blocks.push({ head: (m[1] ?? '').trim(), body: m[2] ?? '' });
  }
  return blocks;
}

describe('deploy/csp.ts', () => {
  it('locks the CSP: self-only, no inline/eval, no third parties, blob: media for the media-element route', () => {
    expect(CSP).toBe(
      "default-src 'self'; script-src 'self'; style-src 'self'; img-src 'self' data:; font-src 'self'; media-src 'self' blob:; connect-src 'self'; worker-src 'self'; object-src 'none'; base-uri 'self'; frame-ancestors 'none'",
    );
    expect(CSP).not.toMatch(/unsafe-|https?:|\*/);
  });

  it('exposes the security header set and cache constants', () => {
    expect(SECURITY_HEADERS).toEqual({
      'Content-Security-Policy': CSP,
      'X-Content-Type-Options': 'nosniff',
      'Referrer-Policy': 'no-referrer',
      'Permissions-Policy': 'geolocation=(self), camera=(), microphone=()',
    });
    expect(IMMUTABLE).toBe('public, max-age=31536000, immutable');
    expect(NO_STORE_PATHS).toEqual(['/', '/index.html', '/sw.js', '/version.json', '/probe/', '/probe/index.html', '/probe/sw.js']);
  });
});

describe('deploy/security-headers.inc', () => {
  it('contains exactly one `add_header … always;` line per SECURITY_HEADERS entry, values verbatim', () => {
    const lines = read('deploy/security-headers.inc')
      .split('\n')
      .filter((l) => l.startsWith('add_header'));
    expect(lines).toEqual(Object.entries(SECURITY_HEADERS).map(([k, v]) => `add_header ${k} "${v}" always;`));
  });
});

describe('nginx.conf', () => {
  const conf = (): string => read('nginx.conf');
  const blocks = (): { head: string; body: string }[] => locationBlocks(conf());

  it('nginx.conf + security-headers.inc together carry the CSP verbatim and every NO_STORE_PATH', () => {
    const both = conf() + read('deploy/security-headers.inc');
    expect(both).toContain(CSP);
    for (const p of NO_STORE_PATHS) expect(conf()).toContain(`location = ${p} {`);
  });

  it('every location includes the security headers (add_header is not inherited)', () => {
    const all = blocks();
    expect(all.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(NO_STORE_PATHS.length + 3);
    for (const b of all) expect(b.body, `location ${b.head}`).toContain(INCLUDE);
  });

  it('NO_STORE_PATHS send no-store (also on 404), /assets/ is immutable but not on errors', () => {
    const all = blocks();
    for (const p of NO_STORE_PATHS) {
      const block = all.find((b) => b.head === `= ${p}`);
      expect(block?.body, p).toContain('add_header Cache-Control "no-store" always;');
    }
    const assets = all.find((b) => b.head === '/assets/');
    expect(assets?.body).toContain(`add_header Cache-Control "${IMMUTABLE}";`);
  });

  it('serves on 8080 with relative redirects, MIME additions, gzip and the SPA fallback', () => {
    const c = conf();
    const all = blocks();
    expect(c).toMatch(/listen 8080;/);
    expect(c).toContain('absolute_redirect off;');
    expect(c).toContain('include /etc/nginx/mime.types;');
    for (const t of ['application/javascript mjs;', 'application/manifest+json webmanifest;', 'application/wasm wasm;', 'audio/wav wav;']) {
      expect(c).toContain(t);
    }
    expect(c).toContain('gzip on;');
    expect(c).toMatch(/gzip_types[^;]*application\/javascript/);
    expect(all.find((b) => b.head === '/')?.body).toContain('try_files $uri $uri/ /index.html;');
    expect(all.find((b) => b.head === '= /probe')?.body).toContain('return 301 /probe/$is_args$args;');
  });
});

describe('Dockerfile and docker-compose.yml', () => {
  it('builds on the build platform and serves dist from non-root nginx with the include installed', () => {
    const df = read('Dockerfile');
    for (const line of [
      'FROM --platform=$BUILDPLATFORM node:22-alpine AS build',
      'ARG GITHUB_SHA',
      'ENV GITHUB_SHA=$GITHUB_SHA',
      'RUN npm ci',
      'RUN npm run build',
      'FROM nginxinc/nginx-unprivileged:alpine',
      'COPY nginx.conf /etc/nginx/conf.d/default.conf',
      'COPY deploy/security-headers.inc /etc/nginx/conf.d/security-headers.inc',
      'COPY --from=build /app/dist /usr/share/nginx/html',
      'EXPOSE 8080',
      'wget -qO- http://127.0.0.1:8080/version.json',
    ]) {
      expect(df).toContain(line);
    }
  });

  it('compose pulls the public GHCR image on port 8080 and uses the placeholder domain only', () => {
    const compose = read('docker-compose.yml');
    expect(compose).toContain('image: ghcr.io/atvriders/tesbox:latest');
    expect(compose).toContain('"8080:8080"');
    expect(compose).toContain('https://tesbox.example.com');
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Create `deploy/csp.ts` (the single source)**

```ts
/**
 * Single source of truth for Tesbox HTTP response headers (spec §12).
 * Consumers: deploy/headers-plugin.ts (vite preview), deploy/security-headers.inc + nginx.conf
 * (container; kept identical by tests/unit/csp.test.ts) and tests/e2e/headers.spec.ts.
 */
export const CSP =
  "default-src 'self'; script-src 'self'; style-src 'self'; img-src 'self' data:; font-src 'self'; media-src 'self' blob:; connect-src 'self'; worker-src 'self'; object-src 'none'; base-uri 'self'; frame-ancestors 'none'";

/** Paths that must never be cached (the Tesla browser caches HTML indefinitely, spec §3). */
export const NO_STORE_PATHS = [
  '/',
  '/index.html',
  '/sw.js',
  '/version.json',
  '/probe/',
  '/probe/index.html',
  '/probe/sw.js',
];

export const SECURITY_HEADERS: Record<string, string> = {
  'Content-Security-Policy': CSP,
  'X-Content-Type-Options': 'nosniff',
  'Referrer-Policy': 'no-referrer',
  'Permissions-Policy': 'geolocation=(self), camera=(), microphone=()',
};

/** Cache-Control for content-hashed build output under /assets/. */
export const IMMUTABLE = 'public, max-age=31536000, immutable';
```

Run: `npx vitest run tests/unit/csp.test.ts`

- [ ] **Step 5: Create `deploy/security-headers.inc`**

The CSP value below must stay byte-identical to `CSP` in `deploy/csp.ts` (Step 9 enforces it). `always` makes nginx send these headers on error responses too.

```nginx
# Tesbox security headers — included by EVERY location in nginx.conf, because nginx drops all
# inherited add_header directives in a location that declares its own add_header.
# Values must equal SECURITY_HEADERS in deploy/csp.ts (enforced by tests/unit/csp.test.ts).
add_header Content-Security-Policy "default-src 'self'; script-src 'self'; style-src 'self'; img-src 'self' data:; font-src 'self'; media-src 'self' blob:; connect-src 'self'; worker-src 'self'; object-src 'none'; base-uri 'self'; frame-ancestors 'none'" always;
add_header X-Content-Type-Options "nosniff" always;
add_header Referrer-Policy "no-referrer" always;
add_header Permissions-Policy "geolocation=(self), camera=(), microphone=()" always;
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Create `nginx.conf`**

Notes that matter: a `types {}` block in `server` would *replace* the inherited MIME map, so the stock `mime.types` is included first and the additions follow (nginx merges blocks within one context; it logs a harmless `duplicate extension "wasm"` warning because recent `mime.types` already lists `wasm`). `absolute_redirect off` keeps redirects relative, so the container port (8080) never leaks through a TLS proxy or tunnel. Immutable caching omits `always`, so a 404 under `/assets/` is never cached for a year.

```nginx
# Tesbox static site for nginxinc/nginx-unprivileged (non-root, port 8080).
# Installed as /etc/nginx/conf.d/default.conf; the Dockerfile copies deploy/security-headers.inc
# next to it. Every location includes that file (add_header is not inherited into a location that
# declares its own add_header). Cache rules mirror deploy/csp.ts; tests/unit/csp.test.ts checks them.
server {
    listen 8080;
    server_name _;
    root /usr/share/nginx/html;
    index index.html;

    server_tokens off;
    # Behind a TLS proxy/tunnel the container port must never leak into redirects.
    absolute_redirect off;
    charset utf-8;

    include /etc/nginx/mime.types;
    types {
        application/javascript mjs;
        application/manifest+json webmanifest;
        application/wasm wasm;
        audio/wav wav;
    }

    gzip on;
    gzip_vary on;
    gzip_min_length 512;
    gzip_types text/css application/javascript application/json application/manifest+json application/wasm image/svg+xml text/plain;

    # Content-hashed build output: cache forever (not "always", so a 404 is never cached for a year).
    location /assets/ {
        include /etc/nginx/conf.d/security-headers.inc;
        add_header Cache-Control "public, max-age=31536000, immutable";
        try_files $uri =404;
    }

    # Never cached: HTML entry points, service workers, version.json.
    location = / {
        include /etc/nginx/conf.d/security-headers.inc;
        add_header Cache-Control "no-store" always;
        try_files /index.html =404;
    }
    location = /index.html {
        include /etc/nginx/conf.d/security-headers.inc;
        add_header Cache-Control "no-store" always;
        try_files $uri =404;
    }
    location = /sw.js {
        include /etc/nginx/conf.d/security-headers.inc;
        add_header Cache-Control "no-store" always;
        try_files $uri =404;
    }
    location = /version.json {
        include /etc/nginx/conf.d/security-headers.inc;
        add_header Cache-Control "no-store" always;
        try_files $uri =404;
    }
    location = /probe/ {
        include /etc/nginx/conf.d/security-headers.inc;
        add_header Cache-Control "no-store" always;
        try_files /probe/index.html =404;
    }
    location = /probe/index.html {
        include /etc/nginx/conf.d/security-headers.inc;
        add_header Cache-Control "no-store" always;
        try_files $uri =404;
    }
    location = /probe/sw.js {
        include /etc/nginx/conf.d/security-headers.inc;
        add_header Cache-Control "no-store" always;
        try_files $uri =404;
    }

    # The probe resolves sw.js/anchor.wav relative to /probe/, so the slash is mandatory.
    location = /probe {
        include /etc/nginx/conf.d/security-headers.inc;
        return 301 /probe/$is_args$args;
    }

    # Everything else: other static files revalidate; unknown paths fall back to the SPA shell
    # (internal redirect to /index.html, which is no-store).
    location / {
        include /etc/nginx/conf.d/security-headers.inc;
        add_header Cache-Control "no-cache";
        try_files $uri $uri/ /index.html;
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 7: Create `Dockerfile`**

```dockerfile
# syntax=docker/dockerfile:1
# Build once on the runner's native platform; only the final COPY stage is per-architecture.
FROM --platform=$BUILDPLATFORM node:22-alpine AS build
WORKDIR /app
COPY package.json package-lock.json ./
RUN npm ci
# Short SHA shown in the app and served in /version.json (deploy/build-sha.ts). Defaults to "dev".
# Declared after npm ci so a new SHA (every CI commit) does not invalidate the cached dependency layer.
ARG GITHUB_SHA=dev
ENV GITHUB_SHA=$GITHUB_SHA
COPY . .
RUN npm run build

FROM nginxinc/nginx-unprivileged:alpine
COPY nginx.conf /etc/nginx/conf.d/default.conf
COPY deploy/security-headers.inc /etc/nginx/conf.d/security-headers.inc
COPY --from=build /app/dist /usr/share/nginx/html
EXPOSE 8080
HEALTHCHECK --interval=30s --timeout=5s --start-period=10s --retries=3 \
  CMD wget -qO- http://127.0.0.1:8080/version.json > /dev/null || exit 1
```

- [ ] **Step 8: Create `docker-compose.yml`**

```yaml
# Tesbox — self-hosted static app.
# Serve it from YOUR public HTTPS domain (e.g. https://tesbox.example.com) through a reverse proxy
# or tunnel that forwards to port 8080: geolocation and AudioWorklet only work in a secure context,
# and the car's browser cannot reach private LAN addresses.
services:
  tesbox:
    image: ghcr.io/atvriders/tesbox:latest
    # To build locally instead of pulling: comment out "image" and uncomment "build".
    # build: .
    restart: unless-stopped
    ports:
      - "8080:8080"
    security_opt:
      - no-new-privileges:true
```

- [ ] **Step 10: Write the failing preview-headers test**

Create `tests/unit/headers-plugin.test.ts`. It drives the middleware with real `node:http` request/response objects, checks that later `setHeader` calls cannot override the pinned headers (Vite's preview sets `Cache-Control: no-cache` on HTML), checks the `/probe` redirect, and checks the wiring in `vite.config.ts`.

```ts
import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { IncomingMessage, ServerResponse } from 'node:http';
import { Socket } from 'node:net';
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { CSP, IMMUTABLE, NO_STORE_PATHS } from '../../deploy/csp';
import { cacheControlFor, headersFor, headersMiddleware, headersPlugin } from '../../deploy/headers-plugin';

function request(url: string): { req: IncomingMessage; res: ServerResponse } {
  const req = new IncomingMessage(new Socket());
  req.url = url;
  return { req, res: new ServerResponse(req) };
}

describe('cacheControlFor (mirrors nginx.conf)', () => {
  it('no-store for every NO_STORE_PATH', () => {
    for (const p of NO_STORE_PATHS) expect(cacheControlFor(p), p).toBe('no-store');
  });

  it('immutable for hashed assets, no-cache for other files, no-store for extension-less SPA paths', () => {
    expect(cacheControlFor('/assets/index-AbC123.js')).toBe(IMMUTABLE);
    expect(cacheControlFor('/probe/anchor.wav')).toBe('no-cache');
    expect(cacheControlFor('/lab')).toBe('no-store');
    expect(cacheControlFor('/probe')).toBe('no-store');
  });
});

describe('headersFor', () => {
  it('returns the security headers plus Cache-Control and ignores the query string', () => {
    const h = headersFor('/version.json?b=abc1234');
    expect(h['Content-Security-Policy']).toBe(CSP);
    expect(h['X-Content-Type-Options']).toBe('nosniff');
    expect(h['Referrer-Policy']).toBe('no-referrer');
    expect(h['Permissions-Policy']).toBe('geolocation=(self), camera=(), microphone=()');
    expect(h['Cache-Control']).toBe('no-store');
  });
});

describe('headersMiddleware', () => {
  it('sets the headers, calls next, and pins them against later overrides (Vite sets no-cache on HTML)', () => {
    const { req, res } = request('/probe/');
    const next = vi.fn();
    headersMiddleware(req, res, next);
    expect(next).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    expect(res.getHeader('content-security-policy')).toBe(CSP);
    res.setHeader('Cache-Control', 'no-cache');
    res.setHeader('content-security-policy', "default-src *");
    expect(res.getHeader('cache-control')).toBe('no-store');
    expect(res.getHeader('content-security-policy')).toBe(CSP);
    res.setHeader('Content-Type', 'text/html');
    expect(res.getHeader('content-type')).toBe('text/html');
  });

  it('handles consecutive requests independently', () => {
    const a = request('/assets/app-1.js');
    const b = request('/index.html');
    headersMiddleware(a.req, a.res, () => undefined);
    headersMiddleware(b.req, b.res, () => undefined);
    expect(a.res.getHeader('cache-control')).toBe(IMMUTABLE);
    expect(b.res.getHeader('cache-control')).toBe('no-store');
    b.res.setHeader('Cache-Control', 'max-age=60');
    expect(b.res.getHeader('cache-control')).toBe('no-store');
    expect(a.res.getHeader('cache-control')).toBe(IMMUTABLE);
  });

  it('redirects /probe to /probe/ with a relative Location, keeping the query', () => {
    const { req, res } = request('/probe?b=abc1234');
    const next = vi.fn();
    headersMiddleware(req, res, next);
    expect(next).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(res.statusCode).toBe(301);
    expect(res.getHeader('location')).toBe('/probe/?b=abc1234');
  });
});

describe('headersPlugin', () => {
  it('registers the middleware on the preview server only', () => {
    const plugin = headersPlugin();
    expect(plugin.name).toBe('tesbox-headers');
    expect(plugin.configureServer).toBeUndefined();
    const use = vi.fn();
    expect(typeof plugin.configurePreviewServer).toBe('function');
    const hook = plugin.configurePreviewServer as unknown as (server: { middlewares: { use: typeof use } }) => void;
    hook({ middlewares: { use } });
    expect(use).toHaveBeenCalledWith(headersMiddleware);
  });

  it('is wired into vite.config.ts', () => {
    const config = readFileSync(new URL('../../vite.config.ts', import.meta.url), 'utf8');
    expect(config).toMatch(/import \{ headersPlugin \} from '\.\/deploy\/headers-plugin(\.ts)?';/);
    expect(config).toContain('headersPlugin()');
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 12: Create `deploy/headers-plugin.ts`**

The middleware is registered directly inside `configurePreviewServer` (not via a returned post-hook), so it runs before Vite's static-file middleware. Vite's preview serves `/probe` (no slash) as the *main* SPA page, so the redirect is mirrored here exactly as nginx does it.

```ts
/**
 * Vite preview-server headers that mirror nginx.conf, so local e2e runs (no Docker) see the same
 * CSP and caching rules as the container (spec §12). Dev server (`vite`) is deliberately untouched.
 */
import type { IncomingMessage, ServerResponse } from 'node:http';
import type { Plugin } from 'vite';
import { IMMUTABLE, NO_STORE_PATHS, SECURITY_HEADERS } from './csp.ts';

/** Mirrors nginx.conf: /assets/ immutable; NO_STORE_PATHS and extension-less (SPA/HTML) paths no-store; other files no-cache. */
export function cacheControlFor(pathname: string): string {
  if (pathname.startsWith('/assets/')) return IMMUTABLE;
  if (NO_STORE_PATHS.includes(pathname)) return 'no-store';
  const lastSegment = pathname.slice(pathname.lastIndexOf('/') + 1);
  return lastSegment.includes('.') ? 'no-cache' : 'no-store';
}

/** All headers for a request URL (query string ignored). */
export function headersFor(url: string): Record<string, string> {
  const q = url.indexOf('?');
  const pathname = q === -1 ? url : url.slice(0, q);
  return { ...SECURITY_HEADERS, 'Cache-Control': cacheControlFor(pathname) };
}

/**
 * Sets `headers` and pins them: later setHeader() calls for the same names (e.g. Vite's own
 * `Cache-Control: no-cache` on HTML) are ignored, so nginx and preview agree.
 */
function pinHeaders(res: ServerResponse, headers: Record<string, string>): void {
  const owned = new Set<string>();
  for (const [name, value] of Object.entries(headers)) {
    res.setHeader(name, value);
    owned.add(name.toLowerCase());
  }
  const setHeader = res.setHeader.bind(res);
  res.setHeader = ((name: string, value: number | string | readonly string[]) =>
    owned.has(name.toLowerCase()) ? res : setHeader(name, value)) as ServerResponse['setHeader'];
}

/** Connect middleware: pins headers; redirects /probe → /probe/ like nginx (relative Location). */
export function headersMiddleware(req: IncomingMessage, res: ServerResponse, next: () => void): void {
  const url = req.url ?? '/';
  const q = url.indexOf('?');
  const pathname = q === -1 ? url : url.slice(0, q);
  const search = q === -1 ? '' : url.slice(q);
  pinHeaders(res, headersFor(url));
  if (pathname === '/probe') {
    res.statusCode = 301;
    res.setHeader('Location', `/probe/${search}`);
    res.end();
    return;
  }
  next();
}

export function headersPlugin(): Plugin {
  return {
    name: 'tesbox-headers',
    configurePreviewServer(server) {
      // Registered directly (not via a returned post-hook) so it runs before Vite's static middleware.
      server.middlewares.use(headersMiddleware);
    },
  };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 1: Create `playwright.config.ts`**

```ts
import { defineConfig, devices } from '@playwright/test';

/** Set E2E_BASE_URL to test a running container (CI); otherwise build + `vite preview` locally. */
const externalBaseUrl = process.env.E2E_BASE_URL;
const isCI = Boolean(process.env.CI);

export default defineConfig({
  testDir: 'tests/e2e',
  timeout: 60_000,
  forbidOnly: isCI,
  retries: isCI ? 1 : 0,
  workers: 1,
  reporter: isCI ? [['list'], ['html', { open: 'never' }]] : 'list',
  use: {
    baseURL: externalBaseUrl ?? 'http://localhost:4173',
    trace: 'retain-on-failure',
  },
  projects: [
    {
      name: 'chromium',
      use: {
        ...devices['Desktop Chrome'],
        launchOptions: { args: ['--autoplay-policy=no-user-gesture-required'] },
      },
    },
  ],
  ...(externalBaseUrl === undefined
    ? {
        webServer: {
          command: 'npm run build && npm run preview',
          url: 'http://localhost:4173/version.json',
          reuseExistingServer: !isCI,
          timeout: 120_000,
        },
      }
    : {}),
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `tests/e2e/shell.spec.ts`**

```ts
import { expect, test } from '@playwright/test';

test('index boots: #unsupported hidden, build SHA shown, no CSP violations', async ({ page, request }) => {
  const res = await request.get('/version.json');
  expect(res.ok()).toBe(true);
  const { sha } = (await res.json()) as { sha: string };
  expect(sha.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);

  const cspErrors: string[] = [];
  page.on('console', (msg) => {
    if (msg.type() === 'error' && /Content Security Policy/i.test(msg.text())) cspErrors.push(msg.text());
  });
  const pageErrors: string[] = [];
  page.on('pageerror', (err) => pageErrors.push(err.message));

  await page.goto('/');
  await expect(page.locator('#unsupported')).toBeHidden();
  await expect(page.locator('body')).toContainText(sha);
  expect(cspErrors).toEqual([]);
  expect(pageErrors).toEqual([]);
});

test('probe page loads all sections without CSP violations or page errors', async ({ page }) => {
  const cspErrors: string[] = [];
  page.on('console', (msg) => {
    if (msg.type() === 'error' && /Content Security Policy/i.test(msg.text())) cspErrors.push(msg.text());
  });
  const pageErrors: string[] = [];
  page.on('pageerror', (err) => pageErrors.push(err.message));

  await page.goto('/probe/');
  for (const id of ['build', 'env', 'audio', 'gps', 'inputs', 'storage', 'export']) {
    await expect(page.locator(`section#${id} > h2`)).toBeVisible();
  }
  await expect(page.locator('#storage-report')).toContainText('Visits');
  expect(cspErrors).toEqual([]);
  expect(pageErrors).toEqual([]);
});

test('probe still loads when localStorage and Cache Storage throw (RF5)', async ({ page }) => {
  const pageErrors: string[] = [];
  page.on('pageerror', (err) => pageErrors.push(err.message));
  // Sandboxed / storage-blocked contexts: both getters throw SecurityError.
  await page.addInitScript(() => {
    const deny = (): never => {
      throw new DOMException('denied', 'SecurityError');
    };
    Object.defineProperty(window, 'localStorage', { configurable: true, get: deny });
    Object.defineProperty(window, 'caches', { configurable: true, get: deny });
  });
  await page.goto('/probe/');
  await expect(page.locator('#storage-report')).toContainText('localStorage: unavailable');
  await expect(page.locator('#storage-report')).toContainText('cacheStorage: SecurityError');
  expect(pageErrors).toEqual([]);
});
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Write `tests/e2e/probe-audio.spec.ts`**

The 3 s contract window starts at the Start click.

```ts
import { expect, test, type Page } from '@playwright/test';

const ROUTES = ['direct', 'media-element', 'direct-anchor'] as const;

async function quanta(page: Page): Promise<number> {
  return Number((await page.locator('#audio-quanta').textContent()) ?? '0');
}

test('Start audio runs the worklet, every route keeps it running, quick benchmark finishes', async ({ page }) => {
  await page.goto('/probe/?quickbench=1');
  await page.locator('#audio-start').click();
  // Contract: Start audio → worklet quanta > 0 within 3 s (planning measured 0.6–2.3 s from the click).
  await expect.poll(() => quanta(page), { timeout: 3_000 }).toBeGreaterThan(0);
  await expect(page.locator('#audio-state')).toHaveText('running');

  for (const route of ROUTES) {
    await page.locator(`input[name="route"][value="${route}"]`).check();
    await expect(page.locator('#audio-route')).toHaveText(route);
    const before = await quanta(page);
    await expect.poll(() => quanta(page), { timeout: 3_000 }).toBeGreaterThan(before);
  }

  await page.locator('#audio-bench').click();
  await expect(page.locator('#bench-results tbody tr')).toHaveCount(2, { timeout: 15_000 });
  await expect(page.locator('#bench-status')).toContainText('headroom k =');
});

test('observation buttons record answers for the selected route', async ({ page }) => {
  await page.goto('/probe/');
  await page.locator('input[name="route"][value="media-element"]').check();
  await expect(page.locator('#obs-route')).toHaveText('media-element');
  const yes = page.locator('#observations button[data-q="miniplayer"][data-v="yes"]');
  await yes.click();
  await expect(yes).toHaveAttribute('aria-pressed', 'true');
  await expect(page.locator('#observations button[data-q="miniplayer"][data-v="no"]')).toHaveAttribute('aria-pressed', 'false');
});

test('export keeps both latencyHint benchmark legs, per-route latency and the snapshot taken at Stop', async ({ page }) => {
  await page.goto('/probe/?quickbench=1');
  for (const hint of ['balanced', 'playback'] as const) {
    await page.locator(`input[name="latency"][value="${hint}"]`).check();
    await page.locator('#audio-start').click();
    await expect(page.locator('#audio-state')).toHaveText('running');
    await page.locator('#audio-bench').click();
    await expect(page.locator('#bench-status')).toContainText('headroom k =', { timeout: 15_000 });
    await page.locator('#audio-stop').click();
    await expect(page.locator('#audio-state')).toHaveText('none');
  }
  await page.locator('#qr-show').click();
  const summary = JSON.parse((await page.locator('#summary-json').textContent()) ?? 'null') as {
    audio: { state: string; latencyHint: string; bench: unknown[] } | null;
    notes: Record<string, string>;
  };
  expect(Object.keys(summary.notes)).toEqual(
    expect.arrayContaining(['bench.balanced', 'bench.playback', 'latency.direct-anchor']),
  );
  // Recorded only once Chromium has measured the output latency (it reads 0 right after start).
  expect(summary.notes['latency.direct-anchor']).not.toMatch(/ out=0 /);
  expect(summary.audio?.state).toBe('running'); // stop() empties the live snapshot; the export uses the one taken at Stop
  expect(summary.audio?.latencyHint).toBe('playback');
  expect(summary.audio?.bench).toHaveLength(2);
});
```

```ts
import { expect, test } from '@playwright/test';

// Fake positions only: a straight line north from 0,0 (spec: no real coordinates anywhere).
test.use({ permissions: ['geolocation'], geolocation: { latitude: 0, longitude: 0, accuracy: 5 } });

test('a 4 s poll run collects fixes and shows a summary row', async ({ page, context }) => {
  await page.goto('/probe/?gpsSeconds=4');
  await expect(page.locator('#gps-seconds')).toHaveText('4');
  await page.locator('#gps-run-poll-hiacc-off').click();
  await expect(page.locator('#gps-live')).toContainText('poll-hiacc-off');

  for (let i = 1; i <= 10; i++) {
    await page.waitForTimeout(500);
    await context.setGeolocation({ latitude: i * 0.0001, longitude: 0, accuracy: 5 });
  }

  const row = page.locator('#gps-results tbody tr[data-mode="poll-hiacc-off"]');
  await expect(row).toHaveCount(1, { timeout: 10_000 });
  expect(Number(await row.getAttribute('data-fixes'))).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(2);
  await expect(page.locator('#gps-live')).toHaveText('idle');
});

test('a reload during a GPS run shows the crash banner once (RF2)', async ({ page }) => {
  await page.goto('/probe/?gpsSeconds=60');
  await page.locator('#gps-run-watch-hiacc-on').click();
  await expect(page.locator('#gps-live')).toContainText('watch-hiacc-on');
  await page.reload(); // the run never finishes: its crash marker stays in localStorage
  await expect(page.locator('#gps-crash')).toBeVisible();
  await expect(page.locator('#gps-crash')).toContainText('watch-hiacc-on');
  await page.reload(); // previousCrash() consumed the marker on the previous load
  await expect(page.locator('#gps-crash')).toBeHidden();
});
```

- [ ] **Step 9: Write `tests/e2e/probe-qr.spec.ts`**

Canvas pixels travel to Node as base64 (one string instead of millions of numbers), `jsqr` decodes each page, `binaryData` is decoded as UTF-8, and the pages are reassembled by their `TBX i/n` headers.

```ts
import { expect, test } from '@playwright/test';
import jsQR from 'jsqr';
import { assertNoCoordinates } from '../../src/probe/summary';

interface CanvasImage {
  width: number;
  height: number;
  b64: string;
}

test('Show QR: every page decodes, chunks reassemble to the summary JSON, no coordinates', async ({ page }) => {
  await page.goto('/probe/');
  await expect(page.locator('#storage-report')).toContainText('Visits');
  await page.locator('#qr-show').click();
  const canvases = page.locator('#qr-list canvas');
  await expect(canvases.first()).toBeVisible();
  await expect(page.locator('#qr-list figcaption').first()).toHaveText(/^QR 1\/\d+$/);

  const images: CanvasImage[] = await canvases.evaluateAll((nodes) =>
    nodes.map((node) => {
      const canvas = node as HTMLCanvasElement;
      const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
      if (ctx === null) throw new Error('canvas has no 2d context');
      const { data } = ctx.getImageData(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
      let binary = '';
      for (let i = 0; i < data.length; i += 0x8000) binary += String.fromCharCode(...data.subarray(i, i + 0x8000));
      return { width: canvas.width, height: canvas.height, b64: btoa(binary) };
    }),
  );
  expect(images.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(1);

  const texts = images.map(({ width, height, b64 }, i) => {
    const code = jsQR(new Uint8ClampedArray(Buffer.from(b64, 'base64')), width, height);
    if (code === null) throw new Error(`QR ${i + 1} did not decode`);
    return new TextDecoder().decode(Uint8Array.from(code.binaryData));
  });
  expect(texts[0]?.startsWith('TBX 1/')).toBe(true);

  const parts = texts.map((t) => {
    const m = /^TBX (\d+)\/(\d+)\n/.exec(t);
    if (m === null) throw new Error(`missing TBX header: ${t.slice(0, 20)}`);
    return { i: Number(m[1]), n: Number(m[2]), body: t.slice(m[0].length) };
  });
  expect(parts.map((p) => p.i)).toEqual(parts.map((_, i) => i + 1));
  expect(new Set(parts.map((p) => p.n))).toEqual(new Set([parts.length]));

  const summary = JSON.parse(parts.map((p) => p.body).join('')) as { v: number };
  expect(summary.v).toBe(1);
  expect(() => assertNoCoordinates(summary)).not.toThrow(); // T4's guard: case-insensitive, any depth
  expect(summary).toEqual(JSON.parse((await page.locator('#summary-json').textContent()) ?? 'null'));

  if (parts.length > 1) {
    await page.locator('#qr-next').click();
    await expect(page.locator('#qr-list figure').nth(1)).toBeVisible();
    await expect(page.locator('#qr-list figure').first()).toBeHidden();
  }
});
```

- [ ] **Step 11: Write `tests/e2e/headers.spec.ts`**

```ts
import { expect, test } from '@playwright/test';
import { CSP, IMMUTABLE, NO_STORE_PATHS } from '../../deploy/csp';

// Header assertions target the nginx container (CI sets E2E_BASE_URL); vite preview is not nginx.
test.skip(!process.env.E2E_BASE_URL, 'set E2E_BASE_URL to a running container to check headers');

test('CSP and security headers on / and /probe/', async ({ request }) => {
  for (const path of ['/', '/probe/']) {
    const res = await request.get(path);
    expect(res.status(), path).toBe(200);
    const h = res.headers();
    expect(h['content-security-policy'], path).toBe(CSP);
    expect(h['x-content-type-options'], path).toBe('nosniff');
    expect(h['referrer-policy'], path).toBe('no-referrer');
  }
});

test('no-store on every NO_STORE_PATH', async ({ request }) => {
  for (const path of NO_STORE_PATHS) {
    const res = await request.get(path);
    expect(res.headers()['cache-control'], path).toBe('no-store');
  }
});

test('hashed assets are immutable and served as JavaScript', async ({ request }) => {
  const html = await (await request.get('/index.html')).text();
  const asset = /\/assets\/[^"']+\.js/.exec(html)?.[0];
  expect(asset, 'an /assets/*.js reference in index.html').toBeDefined();
  const res = await request.get(asset ?? '');
  expect(res.status()).toBe(200);
  expect(res.headers()['cache-control']).toBe(IMMUTABLE);
  expect(res.headers()['content-type']).toContain('javascript');
});

test('/probe redirects to /probe/ with a relative Location', async ({ request }) => {
  const res = await request.get('/probe?b=test', { maxRedirects: 0 });
  expect(res.status()).toBe(301);
  expect(res.headers()['location']).toBe('/probe/?b=test');
});
```

```ts
import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';

const read = (p: string): string => readFileSync(new URL(`../../${p}`, import.meta.url), 'utf8');

const PUBLIC_TEXT_FILES = [
  'README.md',
  'NOTICES',
  'docs/IN_CAR_CHECKLIST.md',
  'docs/CLEAN_ROOM.md',
  'docker-compose.yml',
  'Dockerfile',
  'nginx.conf',
  '.github/workflows/ci.yml',
];

describe('licence and notices', () => {
  it('LICENSE is MIT, 2026 Atvriders', () => {
    const license = read('LICENSE');
    expect(license.startsWith('MIT License')).toBe(true);
    expect(license).toContain('Copyright (c) 2026 Atvriders');
  });

  it('NOTICES lists the test-only jsQR dependency, the QR encoder MIT notice and the non-affiliation line, and ships in the image', () => {
    const notices = read('NOTICES');
    expect(notices).toContain('jsQR — Apache-2.0');
    expect(notices).toContain('Copyright (c) Project Nayuki. (MIT License)'); // MIT notice for the qr.ts adaptation (T3)
    expect(notices).toContain('Not affiliated with or endorsed by Tesla, Inc.');
    // MIT: the notice must accompany every copy, including the published image (Step 21).
    expect(read('Dockerfile')).toContain('COPY NOTICES /usr/share/nginx/html/NOTICES.txt');
    expect(read('Dockerfile')).toContain('COPY LICENSE /usr/share/nginx/html/LICENSE.txt');
  });
});

describe('docs', () => {
  it('README uses the placeholder domain, the disclaimer and links the checklist + clean-room log', () => {
    const readme = read('README.md');
    expect(readme).toContain('https://tesbox.example.com');
    expect(readme).toContain('Not affiliated with or endorsed by Tesla, Inc.');
    expect(readme).toContain('docs/IN_CAR_CHECKLIST.md');
    expect(readme).toContain('docs/CLEAN_ROOM.md');
  });

  it('IN_CAR_CHECKLIST maps every §16 question (Q1–Q11) and separates parked from driving steps', () => {
    const checklist = read('docs/IN_CAR_CHECKLIST.md');
    for (let q = 1; q <= 11; q++) expect(checklist, `Q${q}`).toMatch(new RegExp(`^\\| Q${q} \\|`, 'm'));
    expect(checklist).toMatch(/^## \d\. Parked/m);
    expect(checklist).toMatch(/^## \d\. Driving/m);
    expect(checklist).toContain('Show QR');
  });

  it('CLEAN_ROOM states the binding policy and records the patent check', () => {
    const cleanRoom = read('docs/CLEAN_ROOM.md');
    expect(cleanRoom).toContain('rupteur.app');
    expect(cleanRoom).toContain('juniper-sound-lab');
    expect(cleanRoom).toContain('US 6,959,094');
    expect(cleanRoom).toContain('ISO/IEC 18004');
  });
});

describe('privacy (spec: no real hostnames or IPs in the repo)', () => {
  it('only loopback IPv4 literals appear', () => {
    for (const file of PUBLIC_TEXT_FILES) {
      const ips = read(file).match(/\b\d{1,3}(?:\.\d{1,3}){3}\b/g) ?? [];
      expect(ips.filter((ip) => ip !== '127.0.0.1'), file).toEqual([]);
    }
  });

  it('every absolute URL in user-facing docs is the placeholder domain or localhost', () => {
    for (const file of ['README.md', 'docs/IN_CAR_CHECKLIST.md', 'docker-compose.yml']) {
      const urls = read(file).match(/https?:\/\/[^\s)`'"]+/g) ?? [];
      for (const url of urls) expect(url, file).toMatch(/^(https:\/\/tesbox\.example\.com|http:\/\/localhost(:\d+)?)(\/|$)/);
    }
  });
});

describe('.github/workflows/ci.yml', () => {
  const ci = (): string => read('.github/workflows/ci.yml');

  it('runs on master pushes and PRs with read + packages:write permissions', () => {
    expect(ci()).toMatch(/push:\n\s+branches: \[master\]\n\s+pull_request:/);
    expect(ci()).toMatch(/permissions:\n\s+contents: read\n\s+packages: write/);
  });

  it('chains test → e2e → publish, publishing only on pushes to master', () => {
    expect(ci()).toMatch(/\n {2}e2e:\n {4}needs: test\n/);
    expect(ci()).toMatch(/\n {2}publish:\n {4}needs: \[test, e2e\]\n {4}if: github\.event_name == 'push' && github\.ref == 'refs\/heads\/master'\n/);
  });

  it('tests the container with Playwright and pushes both multi-arch tags', () => {
    expect(ci()).toContain('tags: tesbox:ci');
    expect(ci()).toContain('build-args: GITHUB_SHA=${{ github.sha }}');
    expect(ci()).toContain('docker run -d --name tesbox -p 8080:8080 tesbox:ci');
    expect(ci()).toContain('npx playwright install --with-deps chromium');
    expect(ci()).toContain('E2E_BASE_URL: http://localhost:8080');
    expect(ci()).toContain('platforms: linux/amd64,linux/arm64');
    expect(ci()).toContain('ghcr.io/atvriders/tesbox:latest');
    expect(ci()).toContain('ghcr.io/atvriders/tesbox:${{ github.sha }}');
  });

  it('pins every action by major version', () => {
    const uses = [...ci().matchAll(/uses: (\S+)/g)].map((m) => m[1]);
    const allowed = new Set([
      'actions/checkout@v4',
      'actions/setup-node@v4',
      'docker/setup-qemu-action@v3',
      'docker/setup-buildx-action@v3',
      'docker/login-action@v3',
      'docker/build-push-action@v6',
      'actions/upload-artifact@v4',
      'actions/cache@v4', // Playwright browser cache (spec §12: "Chromium only, cached")
    ]);
    expect(uses.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    for (const u of uses) expect(allowed.has(u ?? ''), u).toBe(true);
  });
});
```
